var array<float> FIB_EXTENSION_RATIOS = array.from(1.000, 1.236, 1.382, 1.500, 1.618, 1.786, 2.000)

// =============================================================================
// Helpers and adaptive settings
// =============================================================================
f_price(float value) =>
    na(value) ? "-" : str.tostring(value, format.mintick)

// round down and long targets round up; short plans use the opposite direction.
f_floor_to_tick(float value) =>
    na(value) ? na : math.floor(value / syminfo.mintick) * syminfo.mintick

f_ceil_to_tick(float value) =>
    na(value) ? na : math.ceil(value / syminfo.mintick) * syminfo.mintick

f_next_fib_extension_level(bool isLong, float anchorLevel, float swingDistance, float entryPrice, float requiredDistance, float maximumDistance) =>
    float selectedLevel = na
    for ratioIndex = 0 to array.size(FIB_EXTENSION_RATIOS) - 1
        float extensionRatio = array.get(FIB_EXTENSION_RATIOS, ratioIndex)
        float extensionOffset = swingDistance * math.max(extensionRatio - 1.0, 0.0)
        float extensionLevel = isLong ? anchorLevel + extensionOffset : anchorLevel - extensionOffset
        float distanceFromEntry = isLong ? extensionLevel - entryPrice : entryPrice - extensionLevel
        if na(selectedLevel) and (distanceFromEntry >= requiredDistance and distanceFromEntry <= maximumDistance)
            selectedLevel := extensionLevel
    selectedLevel

f_build_fib_extension_targets(bool isLong, float anchorLevel, float swingDistance, float entryPrice, float minimumDistance1, float minimumDistance2, float minimumDistance3, float maximumDistance1, float maximumDistance2, float maximumDistance3, float minimumGap) =>
    float selectedLevel1 = f_next_fib_extension_level(isLong, anchorLevel, swingDistance, entryPrice, minimumDistance1, maximumDistance1)
    float selectedDistance1 = na(selectedLevel1) ? minimumDistance1 : isLong ? selectedLevel1 - entryPrice : entryPrice - selectedLevel1
    float boundedDistance1 = math.min(math.max(selectedDistance1, minimumDistance1), maximumDistance1)
    float requiredDistance2 = math.max(minimumDistance2, boundedDistance1 + minimumGap)
    float selectedLevel2 = f_next_fib_extension_level(isLong, anchorLevel, swingDistance, entryPrice, requiredDistance2, maximumDistance2)
    float selectedDistance2 = na(selectedLevel2) ? requiredDistance2 : isLong ? selectedLevel2 - entryPrice : entryPrice - selectedLevel2
    float boundedDistance2 = math.min(math.max(selectedDistance2, requiredDistance2), maximumDistance2)
    float requiredDistance3 = math.max(minimumDistance3, boundedDistance2 + minimumGap)
    float selectedLevel3 = f_next_fib_extension_level(isLong, anchorLevel, swingDistance, entryPrice, requiredDistance3, maximumDistance3)
    float selectedDistance3 = na(selectedLevel3) ? requiredDistance3 : isLong ? selectedLevel3 - entryPrice : entryPrice - selectedLevel3
    float boundedDistance3 = math.min(math.max(selectedDistance3, requiredDistance3), maximumDistance3)
    [(isLong ? entryPrice + boundedDistance1 : entryPrice - boundedDistance1), (isLong ? entryPrice + boundedDistance2 : entryPrice - boundedDistance2), (isLong ? entryPrice + boundedDistance3 : entryPrice - boundedDistance3), selectedLevel1, selectedLevel2, selectedLevel3]

f_ratio(float value) =>
    na(value) ? "—" : str.tostring(value, "#.##") + "×"

f_bar_age(int bars) =>
    bars == 0 ? "الآن" : "قبل " + str.tostring(bars) + " شموع"

// Constant text tables replace long repeated ternary chains without changing any message.
f_text_code(array<string> values, int code) =>
    array.get(values, math.max(0, math.min(code, array.size(values) - 1)))

// النصوص الطويلة معزولة في دوال حتى لا تستهلك عقد النطاق الرئيسي.

f_zone_pattern_text(int patternCode, int baseCount) =>
    string patternName = patternCode == 1 ? "طلب DBR" : patternCode == 2 ? "طلب RBR" : patternCode == -1 ? "عرض RBD" : "عرض DBD"
    patternName + (baseCount > 0 ? " | قاعدة " + str.tostring(baseCount) + " شموع" : " | شمعة منشأ معاكسة")

// Find a compact base immediately before displacement. Demand uses the highest
// body edge as its proximal line and the lowest wick as its distal line; supply
// uses the lowest body edge and the highest wick. If no compact base is present,
// the latest opposite candle remains a conservative one-candle fallback.
f_zone_origin(bool demandSide, int lookbackBars, float maximumBaseBodyRatio) =>
    bool originReady = false
    int farthestOffset = na
    int baseCandleCount = 0
    float proximalLine = na
    float distalLine = na
    for offset = 1 to lookbackBars
        float originCandleRange = high[offset] - low[offset]
        float candleBodyRatio = originCandleRange > syminfo.mintick * 0.50 ? math.abs(close[offset] - open[offset]) / originCandleRange : 1.0
        bool baseCandle = candleBodyRatio <= maximumBaseBodyRatio
        bool oppositeCandle = demandSide ? close[offset] < open[offset] : close[offset] > open[offset]
        bool firstOriginCandle = not originReady and (baseCandle or oppositeCandle)
        if firstOriginCandle or (originReady and offset == farthestOffset + 1 and baseCandle and baseCandleCount < 6)
            originReady := true
            farthestOffset := offset
            baseCandleCount += baseCandle ? 1 : 0
            float bodyHigh = math.max(open[offset], close[offset])
            float bodyLow = math.min(open[offset], close[offset])
            proximalLine := na(proximalLine) ? (demandSide ? bodyHigh : bodyLow) : demandSide ? math.max(proximalLine, bodyHigh) : math.min(proximalLine, bodyLow)
            distalLine := na(distalLine) ? (demandSide ? low[offset] : high[offset]) : demandSide ? math.min(distalLine, low[offset]) : math.max(distalLine, high[offset])
        else if originReady
            break
    int incomingOffset = not na(farthestOffset) ? farthestOffset + 1 : na
    bool incomingAvailable = not na(incomingOffset) and incomingOffset <= lookbackBars
    bool incomingDown = incomingAvailable and close[incomingOffset] < open[incomingOffset]
    bool incomingUp = incomingAvailable and close[incomingOffset] > open[incomingOffset]
    int patternCode = demandSide ? (incomingDown ? 1 : 2) : (incomingUp ? -1 : -2)
    [originReady, farthestOffset, proximalLine, distalLine, baseCandleCount, patternCode]


// too tight for normal noise or too wide for the chart interval are rejected.
f_stop_atr_limits(float seconds) =>
    float minimumMultiple = seconds <= 60 ? 0.60 : seconds <= 5 * 60 ? 0.65 : seconds <= 15 * 60 ? 0.75 : seconds <= 30 * 60 ? 0.85 : seconds <= 60 * 60 ? 1.00 : seconds <= 4 * 60 * 60 ? 1.15 : 1.30
    float maximumMultiple = seconds <= 60 ? 2.50 : seconds <= 5 * 60 ? 2.75 : seconds <= 15 * 60 ? 3.00 : seconds <= 30 * 60 ? 3.25 : seconds <= 60 * 60 ? 3.75 : seconds <= 4 * 60 * 60 ? 4.50 : 5.25
    [minimumMultiple, maximumMultiple]


f_momentum_context(simple bool useEma, simple int fastLength, simple int slowLength, float currentClose, float rsi, int rsiLen) =>
    float fastEma = ta.ema(close, fastLength)
    float slowEma = ta.ema(close, slowLength)
    float fastSma = ta.sma(close, fastLength)
    float slowSma = ta.sma(close, slowLength)
    float fastAverage = useEma ? fastEma : fastSma
    float slowAverage = useEma ? slowEma : slowSma
    bool averagesReady = not na(fastAverage) and not na(slowAverage)
    int averageBias = not averagesReady ? 0 : currentClose > fastAverage and fastAverage > slowAverage ? 1 : currentClose < fastAverage and fastAverage < slowAverage ? -1 : 0
    string averageType = useEma ? "EMA" : "SMA"
    string contextText = (not (averagesReady) ? "المتوسط المتكيف غير جاهز" : "سياق " + (averageType) + " " + str.tostring((fastLength)) + "/" + str.tostring((slowLength)) + ": " + ((averageBias) == 1 ? "صاعد" : (averageBias) == -1 ? "هابط" : "انتقالي")) + "\n" + (na((rsi)) ? "RSI غير جاهز" : "RSI " + str.tostring((rsiLen)) + " = " + str.tostring((rsi), "#.0") + ((rsi) > 55 ? " | زخم صاعد" : (rsi) < 45 ? " | زخم هابط" : " | زخم محايد") + " (ليس دخولاً منفرداً)")
    [fastAverage, slowAverage, averageBias, contextText]

// Adaptive center: momentum changes its smoothing speed. The ATR band changes
// state only on a confirmed close and never creates a trade by itself.
f_vidya_center(float source, simple int length, simple int momentumLength) =>
    float delta = ta.change(source)
    float positiveMomentum = math.sum(math.max(delta, 0.0), momentumLength)
    float negativeMomentum = math.sum(math.max(-delta, 0.0), momentumLength)
    float totalMomentum = positiveMomentum + negativeMomentum
    float efficiency = totalMomentum > 0 ? math.abs(positiveMomentum - negativeMomentum) / totalMomentum : 0.0
    float alpha = 2.0 / (float(length) + 1.0) * efficiency
    var float value = na
    value := na(value[1]) ? source : alpha * source + (1.0 - alpha) * value[1]
    value

f_clear_line(line objectId) =>
    if not na(objectId)
        line.delete(objectId)
    line clearedObject = na
    clearedObject

f_clear_label(label objectId) =>
    if not na(objectId)
        label.delete(objectId)
    label clearedObject = na
    clearedObject

f_clear_box(box objectId) =>
    if not na(objectId)
        box.delete(objectId)
    box clearedObject = na
    clearedObject

// منشئات موحدة لمستويات الخطة؛ نفس خصائص الرسم السابقة دون تكرار معاملات Pine.
f_new_plan_line(float level, color lineColor, int transparency, string lineStyle) =>
    line.new(bar_index, level, bar_index + 1, level, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(lineColor, transparency), style = lineStyle, width = 1, force_overlay = true)

f_nearest_price_level(bool searchAbove, float currentPrice, float minimumDistance, array<float> candidateLevels) =>
    float nearestLevel = na
    for candidateLevel in candidateLevels
        bool candidateValid = not na(candidateLevel) and (searchAbove ? candidateLevel >= currentPrice + minimumDistance : candidateLevel <= currentPrice - minimumDistance)
        if candidateValid and (na(nearestLevel) or (searchAbove ? candidateLevel < nearestLevel : candidateLevel > nearestLevel))
            nearestLevel := candidateLevel
    nearestLevel


// أقرب قمة/قاع تاريخي مؤكد أمام الصفقة. تُستبعد المحاور القديمة جدًا
// والمستويات الواقعة خلف السعر أو خارج غلاف الهدف الحالي.
f_nearest_stored_pivot(bool searchAbove, float currentPrice, float minimumDistance, float maximumDistance, array<float> prices, array<int> pivotBarIndexes, int maximumAgeBars) =>
    float nearestLevel = na
    int count = math.min(array.size(prices), array.size(pivotBarIndexes))
    if count > 0
        for index = 0 to count - 1
            float candidateLevel = array.get(prices, index)
            int candidateBar = array.get(pivotBarIndexes, index)
            float candidateDistance = not na(candidateLevel) ? math.abs(candidateLevel - currentPrice) : na
            bool ageValid = not na(candidateBar) and bar_index - candidateBar <= maximumAgeBars
            bool sideValid = not na(candidateLevel) and (searchAbove ? candidateLevel >= currentPrice + minimumDistance : candidateLevel <= currentPrice - minimumDistance)
            bool distanceValid = sideValid and (na(maximumDistance) or candidateDistance <= maximumDistance)
            if ageValid and distanceValid and (na(nearestLevel) or (searchAbove ? candidateLevel < nearestLevel : candidateLevel > nearestLevel))
                nearestLevel := candidateLevel
    nearestLevel


f_volume_candle_levels_text(bool risingAfterDecline, bool fallingAfterRise, bool peakZoneActive, float peakLow, float peakHigh, bool bottomZoneActive, float bottomLow, float bottomHigh) =>
    string volumeTrendText = risingAfterDecline ? "الحجم النسبي يتحسن بعد هبوط؛ سياق إيجابي مشروط باختراق" : fallingAfterRise ? "الحجم النسبي يتحسن بعد صعود مع ضعف النتيجة؛ حذر من العرض" : "لا يوجد تسارع حجم سياقي واضح"
    string peakText = peakZoneActive ? " | شمعة حجم عند قمة: دعم " + f_price(peakLow) + " / مقاومة " + f_price(peakHigh) : ""
    string bottomText = bottomZoneActive ? " | شمعة حجم عند قاع: دعم " + f_price(bottomLow) + " / مقاومة " + f_price(bottomHigh) : ""
    volumeTrendText + peakText + bottomText

f_price_action_breakouts(bool enabled, int rangeBars, float rangeAtrWidth, float buffer, float atrInputValue, bool volumeOkay, bool volumeIsAvailable, float relativeVolumeValue, float closeLocationValue, bool priorDown, bool priorUp, bool bullBreakQuality, bool bearBreakQuality) =>
    bool insideBar = high < high[1] and low > low[1]
    bool bullInsideSetup = enabled and insideBar and priorDown
    bool bearInsideSetup = enabled and insideBar and priorUp
    float fastRelativeVolume = ta.ema(relativeVolumeValue, 3)
    float slowRelativeVolume = ta.ema(relativeVolumeValue, 8)
    bool relativeVolumeRising = volumeIsAvailable and not na(fastRelativeVolume) and not na(slowRelativeVolume) and fastRelativeVolume > slowRelativeVolume and fastRelativeVolume > fastRelativeVolume[1]
    bool volumeAfterDecline = relativeVolumeRising and priorDown and closeLocationValue >= 0.50
    bool volumeAfterAdvanceWithWeakResult = relativeVolumeRising and priorUp and closeLocationValue <= 0.50
    float rangeHigh = ta.highest(high[1], rangeBars)
    float rangeLow = ta.lowest(low[1], rangeBars)
    float rangeWidth = rangeHigh - rangeLow
    bool rangeReady = enabled and not na(rangeHigh) and not na(rangeLow) and rangeWidth > syminfo.mintick and rangeWidth <= nz(atrInputValue[1], atrInputValue) * rangeAtrWidth
    bool declineBeforeRange = rangeReady and priorDown[rangeBars + 1]
    bool advanceBeforeRange = rangeReady and priorUp[rangeBars + 1]
    bool bullInsideBreakRaw = enabled and barstate.isconfirmed and bullInsideSetup[1] and close > high[2] + buffer
    bool bearInsideBreakRaw = enabled and barstate.isconfirmed and bearInsideSetup[1] and close < low[2] - buffer
    bool bullRangeBreakRaw = enabled and barstate.isconfirmed and declineBeforeRange and close > rangeHigh + buffer
    bool bearRangeBreakRaw = enabled and barstate.isconfirmed and advanceBeforeRange and close < rangeLow - buffer
    [insideBar, bullInsideSetup, bearInsideSetup, volumeAfterDecline, volumeAfterAdvanceWithWeakResult, rangeHigh, rangeLow, rangeReady, bullInsideBreakRaw, bearInsideBreakRaw, bullRangeBreakRaw, bearRangeBreakRaw, (bullInsideBreakRaw and bullBreakQuality and volumeOkay), (bearInsideBreakRaw and bearBreakQuality and volumeOkay), (bullRangeBreakRaw and bullBreakQuality and volumeOkay), (bearRangeBreakRaw and bearBreakQuality and volumeOkay)]

f_cluster_count(float reference, float tolerance, array<float> levels) =>
    int count = 0
    if not na(reference)
        for level in levels
            if not na(level) and math.abs(level - reference) <= tolerance
                count += 1
    count

// ترتيب المصادر: 1) قمم/قيعان ودعم/مقاومة، 2) ترند/قناة،
// 3) فيبوناتشي، 4) متوسطات، 5) شموع طويلة أو عالية الحجم.
// اجتماع مصدرين أو أكثر يضيف علاوة كلاستر، لذلك قد يتفوق الكلاستر على مستوى منفرد.
f_ranked_cluster_level(bool searchAbove, float currentPrice, float minimumDistance, float maximumDistance, float tolerance, array<float> levels) =>
    float bestLevel = na
    int bestScore = -1
    int bestCount = 0
    int bestRank = 99
    float bestDistance = 1e20
    int lastIndex = array.size(levels) - 1
    for candidateIndex = 0 to lastIndex
        float candidateLevel = array.get(levels, candidateIndex)
        float candidateDistance = not na(candidateLevel) ? math.abs(candidateLevel - currentPrice) : na
        bool candidateValid = not na(candidateLevel) and (searchAbove ? candidateLevel >= currentPrice + minimumDistance : candidateLevel <= currentPrice - minimumDistance) and (na(maximumDistance) or candidateDistance <= maximumDistance)
        if candidateValid
            int clusterCount = 1
            int clusterScore = (candidateIndex <= 3 ? 5 : candidateIndex <= 7 ? 4 : candidateIndex <= 9 ? 3 : candidateIndex <= 11 ? 2 : 1)
            for otherIndex = 0 to lastIndex
                if otherIndex != candidateIndex
                    float otherLevel = array.get(levels, otherIndex)
                    float otherDistance = not na(otherLevel) ? math.abs(otherLevel - currentPrice) : na
                    bool otherValid = not na(otherLevel) and (searchAbove ? otherLevel >= currentPrice + minimumDistance : otherLevel <= currentPrice - minimumDistance) and (na(maximumDistance) or otherDistance <= maximumDistance)
                    if otherValid and math.abs(otherLevel - candidateLevel) <= tolerance
                        clusterCount += 1
                        clusterScore += (otherIndex <= 3 ? 5 : otherIndex <= 7 ? 4 : otherIndex <= 9 ? 3 : otherIndex <= 11 ? 2 : 1) + 4
            bool candidateWins = clusterScore > bestScore or clusterScore == bestScore and (clusterCount > bestCount or clusterCount == bestCount and (candidateIndex < bestRank or candidateIndex == bestRank and candidateDistance < bestDistance))
            if candidateWins
                bestLevel := candidateLevel
                bestScore := clusterScore
                bestCount := clusterCount
                bestRank := candidateIndex
                bestDistance := candidateDistance
    [bestLevel, bestScore, bestCount, bestRank]

f_entry_evidence_pass(bool longSide, bool adaptive, bool directBreak, bool earlyQuality, float normalMinimum, float directMinimum, float earlyMinimum, float higherPenalty, int bullishCount, int bearishCount, float bullishPercent, float bearishPercent) =>
    int ownCount = longSide ? bullishCount : bearishCount
    int oppositeCount = longSide ? bearishCount : bullishCount
    float ownPercent = longSide ? bullishPercent : bearishPercent
    float requiredPercent = math.min(90.0, (not adaptive ? normalMinimum : directBreak ? directMinimum : earlyQuality ? earlyMinimum : normalMinimum) + higherPenalty)
    ownPercent >= requiredPercent and ownCount >= oppositeCount + (adaptive and directBreak ? 2 : 1)


f_dealing_range_context(bool enabled, bool retestMode, bool swingCompatible, float lastHigh, float lastLow, int lastHighBar, int lastLowBar, float currentClose) =>
    bool rangeReady = enabled and swingCompatible and not na(lastHigh) and not na(lastLow) and not na(lastHighBar) and not na(lastLowBar) and lastHigh > lastLow
    bool bullishLeg = rangeReady and lastLowBar < lastHighBar
    bool bearishLeg = rangeReady and lastHighBar < lastLowBar
    float rangeSize = rangeReady ? lastHigh - lastLow : na
    float midpoint = rangeReady ? (lastHigh + lastLow) * 0.50 : na
    bool insideRange = rangeReady and currentClose >= lastLow and currentClose <= lastHigh
    bool evidenceReady = retestMode and insideRange and (bullishLeg or bearishLeg)
    int signal = not evidenceReady ? 0 : bullishLeg and currentClose <= midpoint ? 1 : bearishLeg and currentClose >= midpoint ? -1 : 0
    string stateText = (not (enabled) ? "سياق العلاوة/الخصم معطل" : not (rangeReady) ? "سياق العلاوة/الخصم غير جاهز: يلزم محوران مرتبان" : not (insideRange) ? "السعر خارج آخر نطاق تعامل؛ حركة توسع ولا يطبق OTE" : (bullishLeg) ? (((bullishLeg and insideRange and currentClose >= (bullishLeg ? lastHigh - rangeSize * 0.79 : na) and currentClose <= (bullishLeg ? lastHigh - rangeSize * 0.62 : na))) ? "ICT: خصم داخل OTE 62%-79% للصعود" : (currentClose) <= (midpoint) ? "ICT: خصم للصعود لكن خارج OTE" : "ICT: علاوة؛ ليست منطقة ارتداد صاعد مفضلة") : (bearishLeg) ? (((bearishLeg and insideRange and currentClose >= (bearishLeg ? lastLow + rangeSize * 0.62 : na) and currentClose <= (bearishLeg ? lastLow + rangeSize * 0.79 : na))) ? "ICT: علاوة داخل OTE 62%-79% للهبوط" : (currentClose) >= (midpoint) ? "ICT: علاوة للهبوط لكن خارج OTE" : "ICT: خصم؛ ليست منطقة ارتداد هابط مفضلة") : "نطاق تعامل انتقالي")
    [evidenceReady, signal, stateText]

f_fib_retracement_context(bool enabled, bool swingCompatible, float lastHigh, float lastLow, int lastHighBar, int lastLowBar, float currentClose) =>
    bool retracementReady = enabled and swingCompatible and not na(lastHigh) and not na(lastLow) and not na(lastHighBar) and not na(lastLowBar) and lastHigh > lastLow and lastHighBar != lastLowBar
    bool bullishLeg = retracementReady and lastLowBar < lastHighBar
    bool bearishLeg = retracementReady and lastHighBar < lastLowBar
    float legRange = retracementReady ? lastHigh - lastLow : na
    float level236 = bullishLeg ? lastHigh - legRange * 0.236 : bearishLeg ? lastLow + legRange * 0.236 : na
    float level382 = bullishLeg ? lastHigh - legRange * 0.382 : bearishLeg ? lastLow + legRange * 0.382 : na
    float level500 = bullishLeg ? lastHigh - legRange * 0.500 : bearishLeg ? lastLow + legRange * 0.500 : na
    float level618 = bullishLeg ? lastHigh - legRange * 0.618 : bearishLeg ? lastLow + legRange * 0.618 : na
    float level786 = bullishLeg ? lastHigh - legRange * 0.786 : bearishLeg ? lastLow + legRange * 0.786 : na
    float level1000 = bullishLeg ? lastLow : bearishLeg ? lastHigh : na
    string depthText = not retracementReady ? "" : bullishLeg ? currentClose >= level236 ? "تصحيح أقل من 23.6%" : currentClose >= level382 ? "بين 23.6% و38.2%" : currentClose >= level500 ? "بين 38.2% و50%" : currentClose >= level618 ? "بين 50% و61.8%" : currentClose >= level786 ? "بين 61.8% و78.6%" : currentClose >= level1000 ? "بين 78.6% و100%" : "أعمق من 100%؛ إبطال الموجة" : currentClose <= level236 ? "تصحيح أقل من 23.6%" : currentClose <= level382 ? "بين 23.6% و38.2%" : currentClose <= level500 ? "بين 38.2% و50%" : currentClose <= level618 ? "بين 50% و61.8%" : currentClose <= level786 ? "بين 61.8% و78.6%" : currentClose <= level1000 ? "بين 78.6% و100%" : "أعمق من 100%؛ إبطال الموجة"
    string directionText = bullishLeg ? "تصحيح موجة صاعدة" : bearishLeg ? "تصحيح موجة هابطة" : ""
    string levelsText = retracementReady ? "23.6 " + f_price(level236) + " | 38.2 " + f_price(level382) + " | 50 " + f_price(level500) + "\n61.8 " + f_price(level618) + " | 78.6 " + f_price(level786) + " | 100 " + f_price(level1000) : ""
    string stateText = not enabled ? "تصحيح فيبوناتشي معطل" : not retracementReady ? "تصحيح فيبوناتشي غير جاهز: يلزم قاع وقمة مؤكّدان ومرتّبان" : directionText + ": " + depthText + "\n" + levelsText
    [retracementReady, bullishLeg, bearishLeg, level236, level382, level500, level618, level786, level1000, stateText]


f_channel_state_text(int stateCode, string pivotRuleText) =>
    stateCode == 8 ? f_text_code(TXT_CHANNEL_STATE, stateCode) + " | " + pivotRuleText : f_text_code(TXT_CHANNEL_STATE, stateCode)
f_channel_signal(bool upperFakeout, bool lowerFakeout, bool channelIsReadySignal, bool breakoutUp, bool breakoutDown, int bias, float currentClose, float channelMiddle, bool trendIsReadySignal, bool supportBroken, bool resistanceBroken, float channelBase) =>
    upperFakeout ? -1 : lowerFakeout ? 1 : channelIsReadySignal ? (breakoutUp ? 1 : breakoutDown ? -1 : bias == 1 and currentClose >= channelMiddle ? 1 : bias == -1 and currentClose <= channelMiddle ? -1 : 0) : trendIsReadySignal ? (supportBroken ? -1 : resistanceBroken ? 1 : bias == 1 and currentClose >= channelBase ? 1 : bias == -1 and currentClose <= channelBase ? -1 : 0) : 0

// Merge the principal and secondary channel layers into one independent vote.
// a real disagreement produces no directional vote instead of double-counting.
f_merge_channel_layers(bool majorReady, int majorBias, int majorSignal, bool minorReady, int minorBias, int minorSignal) =>
    bool conflict = majorSignal != 0 and minorSignal != 0 and majorSignal != minorSignal
    bool evidenceReady = majorReady or minorReady
    [conflict, evidenceReady, (conflict ? 0 : majorSignal != 0 ? majorSignal : minorSignal), (conflict ? 0 : majorSignal != 0 ? majorBias : minorSignal != 0 ? minorBias : majorReady ? majorBias : minorBias)]


f_wyckoff_phase_state(int mode, int phase) =>
    string accumulationText = phase == 1 ? "المرحلة A: إيقاف الهبوط" : phase == 2 ? "المرحلة B: بناء نطاق التجميع" : phase == 3 ? "المرحلة C: اختبار العرض والربيع" : phase == 4 ? "المرحلة D: قوة ونقاط دعم" : phase == 5 ? "المرحلة E: صعود خارج النطاق" : "غير مكتمل"
    string distributionText = phase == 1 ? "المرحلة A: إيقاف الصعود" : phase == 2 ? "المرحلة B: بناء نطاق التصريف" : phase == 3 ? "المرحلة C: اختبار الطلب والاختراق الوهمي" : phase == 4 ? "المرحلة D: ضعف ونقاط عرض" : phase == 5 ? "المرحلة E: هبوط خارج النطاق" : "غير مكتمل"
    mode == 1 ? "تجميع أصلي محتمل - " + accumulationText : mode == -1 ? "تصريف أصلي محتمل - " + distributionText : "لا يوجد تسلسل وايكوف مكتمل"


f_clear_label_array(array<label> labels) =>
    int labelCount = array.size(labels)
    if labelCount > 0
        for labelIndex = 0 to labelCount - 1
            label storedLabel = array.get(labels, labelIndex)
            if not na(storedLabel)
                label.delete(storedLabel)
    array.clear(labels)


f_new_chart_marker(int markerBar, float markerLevel, string markerText, bool below, color markerColor, float markerAtr, float atrOffset) =>
    float safeMarkerLevel = na(markerLevel) ? close : markerLevel
    float safeMarkerAtr = na(markerAtr) ? syminfo.mintick * 10.0 : markerAtr
    float markerOffset = math.max(safeMarkerAtr * atrOffset, syminfo.mintick * 6.0)
    label.new(markerBar, below ? safeMarkerLevel - markerOffset : safeMarkerLevel + markerOffset, markerText, xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = below ? label.style_label_up : label.style_label_down, color = color.new(markerColor, 28), textcolor = color.white, size = size.tiny, force_overlay = true)

f_push_chart_marker(array<label> labels, int maximumLabels, int markerBar, float markerLevel, string markerText, bool below, color markerColor, float markerAtr, float baseOffset, float laneStep) =>
    while array.size(labels) >= maximumLabels
        label oldestMarker = array.shift(labels)
        if not na(oldestMarker)
            label.delete(oldestMarker)
    int markerLane = array.size(labels) % 3
    label marker = f_new_chart_marker(markerBar, markerLevel, markerText, below, markerColor, markerAtr, baseOffset + markerLane * laneStep)
    array.push(labels, marker)
    marker

// اسم المنطقة جزء من الصندوق نفسه؛ لذلك يتحرك مع آخر شمعة ولا ينشئ ملصقا
// مستقلا فوق السعر. تحديث الحد الأيمن يجعل الاسم دائما بعد الشموع الحالية.
f_refresh_named_zone_box(box zoneBox, int rightBar, string caption, color captionColor) =>
    if not na(zoneBox)
        box.set_right(zoneBox, rightBar)
        box.set_text(zoneBox, caption)
        box.set_text_color(zoneBox, color.new(captionColor, 8))
        box.set_text_size(zoneBox, size.tiny)
        box.set_text_halign(zoneBox, text.align_right)
        box.set_text_valign(zoneBox, text.align_center)

f_refresh_supply_demand_captions(bool updateNow, box demandBox, box supplyBox, int rightBar, color demandColor, color supplyColor) =>
    if updateNow
        f_refresh_named_zone_box(demandBox, rightBar, "منطقة طلب", demandColor)
        f_refresh_named_zone_box(supplyBox, rightBar, "منطقة عرض", supplyColor)

f_close_plan_line(line lineId, int endBar, color baseColor, int transparency, int lineWidth) =>
    if not na(lineId)
        line.set_extend(lineId, extend.none)
        line.set_x2(lineId, endBar)
        line.set_color(lineId, color.new(baseColor, transparency))
        line.set_width(lineId, lineWidth)

f_project(float firstPrice, int firstBar, float secondPrice, int secondBar, int targetBar) =>
    bool validPoints = not na(firstPrice) and not na(secondPrice) and not na(firstBar) and not na(secondBar) and secondBar != firstBar
    validPoints ? secondPrice + (secondPrice - firstPrice) / float(secondBar - firstBar) * float(targetBar - secondBar) : na

// Count only separated reactions after the two defining anchors. A wick may
// enter the narrow trend zone, but the close must return to the valid side.
// Requiring price to leave the zone before another count prevents a cluster of
// adjacent candles from masquerading as several independent touches.
f_update_trend_reactions(bool trendIsReady, int trendBias, float trendBase, float touchTolerance, float closeBuffer, bool geometryChanged, int currentCount, bool currentArmed, int currentLastBar, int minimumSpacing) =>
    int nextCount = currentCount
    bool nextArmed = currentArmed
    int nextLastBar = currentLastBar
    bool rejectionEvent = false
    if not trendIsReady
        nextCount := 0
        nextArmed := false
        nextLastBar := na
    else if geometryChanged
        nextCount := 0
        nextArmed := false
        nextLastBar := na
    else
        bool rejectedZone = barstate.isconfirmed and (trendBias == 1 ? low <= trendBase + touchTolerance and close >= trendBase - closeBuffer : high >= trendBase - touchTolerance and close <= trendBase + closeBuffer)
        bool spacingReady = na(nextLastBar) or bar_index - nextLastBar >= minimumSpacing
        if (trendBias == 1 ? low > trendBase + touchTolerance * 2.0 : high < trendBase - touchTolerance * 2.0)
            nextArmed := true
        if nextArmed and rejectedZone and spacingReady
            nextCount += 1
            nextArmed := false
            nextLastBar := bar_index
            rejectionEvent := true
    [nextCount, nextArmed, nextLastBar, rejectionEvent]

// A valid channel breakout has a classical measured objective equal to the
// perpendicular channel width. Keep only a fresh, still-unreached objective and
// expire it after a bounded number of chart bars.
f_update_channel_objectives(float currentBullTarget, int currentBullBar, float currentBearTarget, int currentBearBar, bool qualifiedBreakUp, bool qualifiedBreakDown, bool upperFakeout, bool lowerFakeout, bool geometryChanged, float upperBoundary, float lowerBoundary, float measuredWidth, int maximumAge) =>
    float nextBullTarget = currentBullTarget
    int nextBullBar = currentBullBar
    float nextBearTarget = currentBearTarget
    int nextBearBar = currentBearBar
    bool bullExpired = not na(nextBullBar) and bar_index - nextBullBar > maximumAge
    bool bearExpired = not na(nextBearBar) and bar_index - nextBearBar > maximumAge
    if geometryChanged
        nextBullTarget := na
        nextBullBar := na
        nextBearTarget := na
        nextBearBar := na
    if bullExpired or upperFakeout or qualifiedBreakDown or (not na(nextBullTarget) and high >= nextBullTarget)
        nextBullTarget := na
        nextBullBar := na
    if bearExpired or lowerFakeout or qualifiedBreakUp or (not na(nextBearTarget) and low <= nextBearTarget)
        nextBearTarget := na
        nextBearBar := na
    float bullCandidate = not na(upperBoundary) and not na(measuredWidth) ? upperBoundary + measuredWidth : na
    float bearCandidate = not na(lowerBoundary) and not na(measuredWidth) ? lowerBoundary - measuredWidth : na
    if qualifiedBreakUp and not na(bullCandidate) and bullCandidate > high + syminfo.mintick
        nextBullTarget := bullCandidate
        nextBullBar := bar_index
    if qualifiedBreakDown and not na(bearCandidate) and bearCandidate < low - syminfo.mintick and bearCandidate >= syminfo.mintick
        nextBearTarget := bearCandidate
        nextBearBar := bar_index
    [nextBullTarget, nextBullBar, nextBearTarget, nextBearBar]

// Pine resolves user-defined functions in declaration order. Keep this helper
// above the principal channel engine because that engine compares anchor IDs.
f_int_changed(int previousValue, int currentValue) =>
    na(previousValue) != na(currentValue) or (not na(previousValue) and not na(currentValue) and previousValue != currentValue)

// Maintain a second, slower set of confirmed pivots for the principal trend.
// returns only the latest valid principal trend and one parallel boundary, so
// the chart never accumulates a forest of historical lines.
f_update_major_channel(float pivotHighCandidate, float pivotLowCandidate, int candidateBar, int pivotStrength, int maximumValidationBars, float atrSeries, float breakAtrPart, float zoneAtrPart, float maximumProjectionAtr, float maximumSlopeAtr, float minimumWidthConsistency, int dowDirection) =>
    var float previousHigh = na
    var float lastHigh = na
    var int previousHighBar = na
    var int lastHighBar = na
    var float previousLow = na
    var float lastLow = na
    var int previousLowBar = na
    var int lastLowBar = na
    var bool risingLineUnbroken = false
    var bool fallingLineUnbroken = false

    bool newHigh = barstate.isconfirmed and not na(pivotHighCandidate)
    bool newLow = barstate.isconfirmed and not na(pivotLowCandidate)
    if newHigh
        previousHigh := lastHigh
        previousHighBar := lastHighBar
        lastHigh := pivotHighCandidate
        lastHighBar := candidateBar
    if newLow
        previousLow := lastLow
        previousLowBar := lastLowBar
        lastLow := pivotLowCandidate
        lastLowBar := candidateBar

    int minimumAnchorBars = pivotStrength + 1
    bool highsReady = not na(previousHigh) and not na(lastHigh) and not na(previousHighBar) and not na(lastHighBar) and lastHighBar - previousHighBar >= minimumAnchorBars
    bool lowsReady = not na(previousLow) and not na(lastLow) and not na(previousLowBar) and not na(lastLowBar) and lastLowBar - previousLowBar >= minimumAnchorBars
    float highSlope = highsReady ? (lastHigh - previousHigh) / float(lastHighBar - previousHighBar) : na
    float lowSlope = lowsReady ? (lastLow - previousLow) / float(lastLowBar - previousLowBar) : na

    if newLow
        bool risingPair = lowsReady and lastLow > previousLow + syminfo.mintick
        risingLineUnbroken := risingPair
        if risingPair
            int validationSpan = bar_index - previousLowBar
            if validationSpan > maximumValidationBars
                risingLineUnbroken := false
            else
                for historyOffset = 0 to validationSpan
                    int historicalBar = bar_index - historyOffset
                    float lineAtHistory = f_project(previousLow, previousLowBar, lastLow, lastLowBar, historicalBar)
                    float historicalAtr = nz(atrSeries[historyOffset], atrSeries)
                    float historicalBuffer = historicalAtr * math.max(breakAtrPart, zoneAtrPart * 0.50)
                    if close[historyOffset] < lineAtHistory - historicalBuffer
                        risingLineUnbroken := false
                        break
    if newHigh
        bool fallingPair = highsReady and lastHigh < previousHigh - syminfo.mintick
        fallingLineUnbroken := fallingPair
        if fallingPair
            int validationSpan = bar_index - previousHighBar
            if validationSpan > maximumValidationBars
                fallingLineUnbroken := false
            else
                for historyOffset = 0 to validationSpan
                    int historicalBar = bar_index - historyOffset
                    float lineAtHistory = f_project(previousHigh, previousHighBar, lastHigh, lastHighBar, historicalBar)
                    float historicalAtr = nz(atrSeries[historyOffset], atrSeries)
                    float historicalBuffer = historicalAtr * math.max(breakAtrPart, zoneAtrPart * 0.50)
                    if close[historyOffset] > lineAtHistory + historicalBuffer
                        fallingLineUnbroken := false
                        break

    bool risingAnchor = lowsReady and risingLineUnbroken and lastLow > previousLow + syminfo.mintick
    bool fallingAnchor = highsReady and fallingLineUnbroken and lastHigh < previousHigh - syminfo.mintick
    bool risingStructure = highsReady and risingAnchor and lastHigh > previousHigh + syminfo.mintick
    bool fallingStructure = lowsReady and fallingAnchor and lastLow < previousLow - syminfo.mintick
    float risingWidth1 = risingStructure ? previousHigh - f_project(previousLow, previousLowBar, lastLow, lastLowBar, previousHighBar) : na
    float risingWidth2 = risingStructure ? lastHigh - f_project(previousLow, previousLowBar, lastLow, lastLowBar, lastHighBar) : na
    float fallingWidth1 = fallingStructure ? f_project(previousHigh, previousHighBar, lastHigh, lastHighBar, previousLowBar) - previousLow : na
    float fallingWidth2 = fallingStructure ? f_project(previousHigh, previousHighBar, lastHigh, lastHighBar, lastLowBar) - lastLow : na
    float majorRisingMaximumWidth = risingStructure ? math.max(risingWidth1, risingWidth2) : na
    float majorFallingMaximumWidth = fallingStructure ? math.max(fallingWidth1, fallingWidth2) : na
    float majorRisingMinimumWidth = risingStructure ? math.min(risingWidth1, risingWidth2) : na
    float majorFallingMinimumWidth = fallingStructure ? math.min(fallingWidth1, fallingWidth2) : na
    float risingConsistency = not na(majorRisingMaximumWidth) and majorRisingMaximumWidth > syminfo.mintick ? majorRisingMinimumWidth / majorRisingMaximumWidth : na
    float fallingConsistency = not na(majorFallingMaximumWidth) and majorFallingMaximumWidth > syminfo.mintick ? majorFallingMinimumWidth / majorFallingMaximumWidth : na
    bool risingChannel = risingStructure and majorRisingMinimumWidth > syminfo.mintick and risingConsistency >= minimumWidthConsistency
    bool fallingChannel = fallingStructure and majorFallingMinimumWidth > syminfo.mintick and fallingConsistency >= minimumWidthConsistency
    int completeBias = risingChannel and not fallingChannel ? 1 : fallingChannel and not risingChannel ? -1 : 0
    int bias = completeBias != 0 ? completeBias : ((risingAnchor and not fallingAnchor and dowDirection != -1) ? 1 : (fallingAnchor and not risingAnchor and dowDirection != 1) ? -1 : 0)
    float width = completeBias == 1 ? majorRisingMaximumWidth : completeBias == -1 ? majorFallingMaximumWidth : na
    float consistency = completeBias == 1 ? risingConsistency : completeBias == -1 ? fallingConsistency : na
    float selectedSlope = bias == 1 ? lowSlope : bias == -1 ? highSlope : na
    float baseNow = bias == 1 ? f_project(previousLow, previousLowBar, lastLow, lastLowBar, bar_index) : bias == -1 ? f_project(previousHigh, previousHighBar, lastHigh, lastHighBar, bar_index) : na
    bool baseRelevant = not na(baseNow) and math.abs(close - baseNow) <= atrSeries * maximumProjectionAtr * 2.0
    bool slopeRelevant = not na(selectedSlope) and math.abs(selectedSlope) <= atrSeries * maximumSlopeAtr
    bool majorTrendReadyCalc = bias != 0 and baseRelevant and slopeRelevant
    bool widthValid = not na(width) and width > syminfo.mintick and width <= atrSeries * maximumProjectionAtr * 2.0
    bool majorChannelReadyCalc = completeBias == bias and majorTrendReadyCalc and widthValid
    float lowerNow = majorChannelReadyCalc ? (bias == 1 ? baseNow : baseNow - width) : na
    float upperNow = majorChannelReadyCalc ? (bias == 1 ? baseNow + width : baseNow) : na
    int firstBar = bias == 1 ? previousLowBar : bias == -1 ? previousHighBar : na
    float firstPrice = bias == 1 ? previousLow : bias == -1 ? previousHigh : na
    int secondBar = bias == 1 ? lastLowBar : bias == -1 ? lastHighBar : na
    float secondPrice = bias == 1 ? lastLow : bias == -1 ? lastHigh : na
    [bias, majorTrendReadyCalc, majorChannelReadyCalc, baseNow, lowerNow, upperNow, width, consistency, firstBar, firstPrice, secondBar, secondPrice]

// compile-size optimization only; calculations and confirmation rules are the
// same as the secondary layer.
f_calculate_major_layer(int pivotStrength, int maximumValidationBars, float atrSeries, float breakAtrPart, float zoneAtrPart, float maximumProjectionAtr, float maximumSlopeAtr, float minimumWidthConsistency, int dowDirection, float closeBreakBuffer, int fakeoutBars, int retestBars, int forwardBars) =>
    float rightHigh = ta.highest(high, pivotStrength)
    float leftHigh = ta.highest(high[pivotStrength + 1], pivotStrength)
    float rightLow = ta.lowest(low, pivotStrength)
    float leftLow = ta.lowest(low[pivotStrength + 1], pivotStrength)
    bool strictHigh = bar_index >= pivotStrength * 2 and high[pivotStrength] > rightHigh and high[pivotStrength] > leftHigh
    bool strictLow = bar_index >= pivotStrength * 2 and low[pivotStrength] < rightLow and low[pivotStrength] < leftLow
    float highCandidate = strictHigh ? high[pivotStrength] : na
    float lowCandidate = strictLow ? low[pivotStrength] : na
    int candidateBar = bar_index - pivotStrength
    [bias, trendReady, channelReady, baseNow, lowerNow, upperNow, width, consistency, firstBar, firstPrice, secondBar, secondPrice] = f_update_major_channel(highCandidate, lowCandidate, candidateBar, pivotStrength, maximumValidationBars, atrSeries, breakAtrPart, zoneAtrPart, maximumProjectionAtr, maximumSlopeAtr, minimumWidthConsistency, dowDirection)
    float middleNow = channelReady ? (lowerNow + upperNow) * 0.50 : na
    bool breakoutUp = channelReady and barstate.isconfirmed and close > upperNow + closeBreakBuffer
    bool breakoutDown = channelReady and barstate.isconfirmed and close < lowerNow - closeBreakBuffer
    bool geometryStable = channelReady and channelReady[1] and bias == bias[1] and not f_int_changed(firstBar[1], firstBar) and not f_int_changed(secondBar[1], secondBar)
    bool breakoutUpEvent = breakoutUp and geometryStable and close[1] <= upperNow[1] + nz(closeBreakBuffer[1], closeBreakBuffer)
    bool breakoutDownEvent = breakoutDown and geometryStable and close[1] >= lowerNow[1] - nz(closeBreakBuffer[1], closeBreakBuffer)
    // Stateful principal-channel watcher. `ta.barssince()` alone can keep an
    // older opposite breakout alive and let both sides appear eligible. Resolve
    // one watched crossing, then arm the next crossing.
    var int watchedDirection = 0
    var int watchedBar = na
    var int watchedBias = 0
    var int watchedFirstBar = na
    var int watchedSecondBar = na
    int priorWatchedDirection = watchedDirection
    int priorWatchedBar = watchedBar
    int barsSinceWatchedBreak = na(priorWatchedBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - priorWatchedBar
    bool watchedContextInvalid = priorWatchedDirection != 0 and (not channelReady or not ((watchedFirstBar) == firstBar and (watchedSecondBar) == secondBar and (watchedBias) == bias))
    bool upperFakeout = barstate.isconfirmed and channelReady and not watchedContextInvalid and priorWatchedDirection == 1 and barsSinceWatchedBreak > 0 and barsSinceWatchedBreak <= fakeoutBars and close <= upperNow
    bool lowerFakeout = barstate.isconfirmed and channelReady and not watchedContextInvalid and priorWatchedDirection == -1 and barsSinceWatchedBreak > 0 and barsSinceWatchedBreak <= fakeoutBars and close >= lowerNow
    if watchedContextInvalid or (priorWatchedDirection != 0 and barsSinceWatchedBreak > fakeoutBars) or upperFakeout or lowerFakeout
        watchedDirection := 0
        watchedBar := na
        watchedBias := 0
        watchedFirstBar := na
        watchedSecondBar := na
    if breakoutUpEvent
        watchedDirection := 1
        watchedBar := bar_index
        watchedBias := bias
        watchedFirstBar := firstBar
        watchedSecondBar := secondBar
    else if breakoutDownEvent
        watchedDirection := -1
        watchedBar := bar_index
        watchedBias := bias
        watchedFirstBar := firstBar
        watchedSecondBar := secondBar
    bool supportBroken = trendReady and bias == 1 and barstate.isconfirmed and close < baseNow - closeBreakBuffer
    bool resistanceBroken = trendReady and bias == -1 and barstate.isconfirmed and close > baseNow + closeBreakBuffer
    int signal = f_channel_signal(upperFakeout, lowerFakeout, channelReady, breakoutUp, breakoutDown, bias, close, middleNow, trendReady, supportBroken, resistanceBroken, baseNow)
    var float bullishTarget = na
    var int bullishTargetBar = na
    var float bearishTarget = na
    var int bearishTargetBar = na
    int objectiveMaximumAge = math.max(retestBars, forwardBars * 5)
    [nextBullTarget, nextBullTargetBar, nextBearTarget, nextBearTargetBar] = f_update_channel_objectives(bullishTarget, bullishTargetBar, bearishTarget, bearishTargetBar, breakoutUpEvent, breakoutDownEvent, upperFakeout, lowerFakeout, not geometryStable, upperNow, lowerNow, width, objectiveMaximumAge)
    bullishTarget := nextBullTarget
    bullishTargetBar := nextBullTargetBar
    bearishTarget := nextBearTarget
    bearishTargetBar := nextBearTargetBar
    string baseState = upperFakeout ? "الرئيسي: اختراق علوي وهمي ثم عودة داخل القناة" : lowerFakeout ? "الرئيسي: كسر سفلي وهمي ثم عودة داخل القناة" : not trendReady ? "الرئيسي: غير مكتمل" : channelReady ? "الرئيسي: قناة " + (bias == 1 ? "صاعدة" : "هابطة") + " | تجانس " + str.tostring(consistency * 100.0, "#") + "%" + (breakoutUp ? " | اختراق علوي" : breakoutDown ? " | كسر سفلي" : " | داخل المسار") : "الرئيسي: ترند " + (bias == 1 ? "صاعد" : "هابط") + " من ارتكازين"
    string targetState = not na(bullishTarget) ? " | هدف رئيسي علوي " + f_price(bullishTarget) : not na(bearishTarget) ? " | هدف رئيسي سفلي " + f_price(bearishTarget) : ""
    string state = baseState + targetState
    [bias, trendReady, channelReady, baseNow, lowerNow, upperNow, width, firstBar, firstPrice, secondBar, secondPrice, breakoutUpEvent, breakoutDownEvent, breakoutUp, breakoutDown, upperFakeout, lowerFakeout, signal, bullishTarget, bearishTarget, state]

// Update one clean visual layer. The principal layer is solid and slightly
// stronger; the secondary layer is dotted and more transparent.
f_update_channel_visual(line trendLine, line zoneLine, linefill zoneFill, line parallelLine, bool showLayer, bool trendIsReady, bool channelIsReady, int bias, int firstBar, float firstPrice, int secondBar, float secondPrice, float channelWidthValue, float atrSeries, int forwardBars, float maximumProjectionAtr, float zoneAtrPart, color bullishColor, color bearishColor, int trendTransparency, int zoneLineTransparencyValue, int zoneFillTransparencyValue, int parallelTransparency, bool principalLayer) =>
    line nextTrendLine = trendLine
    line nextZoneLine = zoneLine
    linefill nextZoneFill = zoneFill
    line nextParallelLine = parallelLine
    bool anchorsReady = not na(firstBar) and not na(firstPrice) and not na(secondBar) and not na(secondPrice) and secondBar != firstBar
    if showLayer and trendIsReady and anchorsReady
        float slope = (secondPrice - firstPrice) / float(secondBar - firstBar)
        int atrLimitedBars = math.abs(slope) <= syminfo.mintick ? forwardBars : math.max(1, int(math.floor((atrSeries * maximumProjectionAtr) / math.abs(slope))))
        int visualForwardBars = math.min(forwardBars, atrLimitedBars)
        int lastBar = bar_index + visualForwardBars
        float lastPrice = f_project(firstPrice, firstBar, secondPrice, secondBar, lastBar)
        float zoneWidth = math.max(syminfo.mintick * 2.0, atrSeries * zoneAtrPart)
        float zoneFirstPrice = bias == 1 ? firstPrice + zoneWidth : firstPrice - zoneWidth
        float zoneLastPrice = bias == 1 ? lastPrice + zoneWidth : lastPrice - zoneWidth
        color baseColor = bias == 1 ? bullishColor : bearishColor
        color trendColor = color.new(baseColor, principalLayer ? math.max(0, trendTransparency - 12) : math.min(95, trendTransparency + 10))
        color zoneColor = color.new(baseColor, principalLayer ? zoneLineTransparencyValue : math.min(96, zoneLineTransparencyValue + 8))
        color fillColor = color.new(baseColor, principalLayer ? zoneFillTransparencyValue : math.min(98, zoneFillTransparencyValue + 4))
        color parallelColor = color.new(baseColor, principalLayer ? parallelTransparency : math.min(96, parallelTransparency + 8))
        string trendStyle = principalLayer ? line.style_solid : line.style_dotted
        string parallelStyle = principalLayer ? line.style_dashed : line.style_dotted
        int trendWidth = principalLayer ? 2 : 1
        if na(nextTrendLine)
            nextTrendLine := line.new(firstBar, firstPrice, lastBar, lastPrice, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.none, color = trendColor, style = trendStyle, width = trendWidth, force_overlay = true)
        else
            line.set_xy1(nextTrendLine, firstBar, firstPrice)
            line.set_xy2(nextTrendLine, lastBar, lastPrice)
            line.set_color(nextTrendLine, trendColor)
            line.set_style(nextTrendLine, trendStyle)
            line.set_width(nextTrendLine, trendWidth)
        if na(nextZoneLine)
            nextZoneLine := line.new(firstBar, zoneFirstPrice, lastBar, zoneLastPrice, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.none, color = zoneColor, style = trendStyle, width = 1, force_overlay = true)
        else
            line.set_xy1(nextZoneLine, firstBar, zoneFirstPrice)
            line.set_xy2(nextZoneLine, lastBar, zoneLastPrice)
            line.set_color(nextZoneLine, zoneColor)
            line.set_style(nextZoneLine, trendStyle)
            line.set_width(nextZoneLine, 1)
        if na(nextZoneFill)
            nextZoneFill := linefill.new(nextTrendLine, nextZoneLine, fillColor)
        else
            linefill.set_color(nextZoneFill, fillColor)
        if channelIsReady and not na(channelWidthValue)
            float parallelFirstPrice = bias == 1 ? firstPrice + channelWidthValue : firstPrice - channelWidthValue
            float parallelLastPrice = bias == 1 ? lastPrice + channelWidthValue : lastPrice - channelWidthValue
            if na(nextParallelLine)
                nextParallelLine := line.new(firstBar, parallelFirstPrice, lastBar, parallelLastPrice, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.none, color = parallelColor, style = parallelStyle, width = 1, force_overlay = true)
            else
                line.set_xy1(nextParallelLine, firstBar, parallelFirstPrice)
                line.set_xy2(nextParallelLine, lastBar, parallelLastPrice)
                line.set_color(nextParallelLine, parallelColor)
                line.set_style(nextParallelLine, parallelStyle)
                line.set_width(nextParallelLine, 1)
        else if not na(nextParallelLine)
            nextParallelLine := f_clear_line(nextParallelLine)
    else
        if not na(nextZoneFill)
            linefill.delete(nextZoneFill)
            nextZoneFill := na
        nextZoneLine := f_clear_line(nextZoneLine)
        nextTrendLine := f_clear_line(nextTrendLine)
        nextParallelLine := f_clear_line(nextParallelLine)
    [nextTrendLine, nextZoneLine, nextZoneFill, nextParallelLine]

// Build one recent multi-touch cluster from pivots of the same type. Keeping
// highs and lows in separate arrays prevents a support and a resistance from
// being merged merely because their prices happen to be close.
f_strong_sr_cluster(array<float> prices, array<int> pivotBarIndexes, float tolerance, int minimumTouches, int maximumAgeBars) =>
    float selectedLevel = na
    float selectedLow = na
    float selectedHigh = na
    int selectedTouches = 0
    int selectedRecentBar = na
    int availablePoints = array.size(prices)
    if availablePoints > 0 and array.size(pivotBarIndexes) == availablePoints
        for candidateIndex = 0 to availablePoints - 1
            float candidatePrice = array.get(prices, candidateIndex)
            int candidateBar = array.get(pivotBarIndexes, candidateIndex)
            if bar_index - candidateBar <= maximumAgeBars
                float clusterSum = 0.0
                float clusterLow = candidatePrice
                float clusterHigh = candidatePrice
                int clusterTouches = 0
                int clusterRecentBar = candidateBar
                for pointIndex = 0 to availablePoints - 1
                    float pointPrice = array.get(prices, pointIndex)
                    int pointBar = array.get(pivotBarIndexes, pointIndex)
                    if (bar_index - pointBar <= maximumAgeBars) and math.abs(pointPrice - candidatePrice) <= tolerance
                        clusterSum += pointPrice
                        clusterLow := math.min(clusterLow, pointPrice)
                        clusterHigh := math.max(clusterHigh, pointPrice)
                        clusterTouches += 1
                        clusterRecentBar := math.max(clusterRecentBar, pointBar)
                bool betterCluster = clusterTouches > selectedTouches or (clusterTouches == selectedTouches and clusterRecentBar > nz(selectedRecentBar, -1))
                if betterCluster
                    selectedLevel := clusterSum / float(math.max(clusterTouches, 1))
                    selectedLow := clusterLow
                    selectedHigh := clusterHigh
                    selectedTouches := clusterTouches
                    selectedRecentBar := clusterRecentBar
    bool clusterQualified = selectedTouches >= minimumTouches
    [clusterQualified ? selectedLevel : na, clusterQualified ? selectedLow : na, clusterQualified ? selectedHigh : na, clusterQualified ? selectedTouches : 0, clusterQualified ? selectedRecentBar : na]

// Return the first future bar where any stored pivot becomes older than the
// configured window. This schedules an exact refresh without running the nested
// clustering loops on every historical bar.
f_next_sr_expiry(array<int> pivotBarIndexes, int maximumAgeBars) =>
    int nextExpiryBar = na
    int availableBars = array.size(pivotBarIndexes)
    if availableBars > 0
        for pointIndex = 0 to availableBars - 1
            int pointBar = array.get(pivotBarIndexes, pointIndex)
            int pointExpiryBar = pointBar + maximumAgeBars + 1
            if pointExpiryBar > bar_index
                nextExpiryBar := na(nextExpiryBar) ? pointExpiryBar : math.min(nextExpiryBar, pointExpiryBar)
    nextExpiryBar

f_float_changed(float previousValue, float currentValue) =>
    na(previousValue) != na(currentValue) or (not na(previousValue) and not na(currentValue) and math.abs(previousValue - currentValue) > syminfo.mintick * 0.50)

f_update_horizontal_sr_zone(box zoneBox, int firstBar, int lastBar, float rawTop, float rawBottom, color zoneColor, string zoneText) =>
    box result = zoneBox
    float center = (rawTop + rawBottom) * 0.50
    float halfHeight = math.max(math.abs(rawTop - rawBottom) * 0.50, syminfo.mintick * 3.0)
    float zoneTop = center + halfHeight
    float zoneBottom = center - halfHeight
    if na(result)
        result := box.new(left = firstBar, top = zoneTop, right = lastBar, bottom = zoneBottom, xloc = xloc.bar_index, border_color = color.new(zoneColor, 58), border_width = 1, bgcolor = color.new(zoneColor, 94), text = zoneText, text_color = color.new(zoneColor, 8), text_size = size.tiny, text_halign = text.align_right, text_valign = text.align_center, force_overlay = true)
    else
        box.set_lefttop(result, firstBar, zoneTop)
        box.set_rightbottom(result, lastBar, zoneBottom)
        box.set_border_color(result, color.new(zoneColor, 58))
        box.set_border_width(result, 1)
        box.set_bgcolor(result, color.new(zoneColor, 94))
        box.set_text(result, zoneText)
        box.set_text_color(result, color.new(zoneColor, 8))
        box.set_text_size(result, size.tiny)
        box.set_text_halign(result, text.align_right)
        box.set_text_valign(result, text.align_center)
    result


// حالة مضغوطة من آخر شمعة أعلى مكتملة. الأطوال تتكيف مع الأسبوعي/الشهري
// حتى لا يحتاج سياق 6M أو 12M إلى عشرات السنين قبل أن يصبح جاهزًا.
f_closed_htf_state() =>
    int monthMultiple = timeframe.ismonthly ? timeframe.multiplier : 0
    int fastLength = timeframe.ismonthly ? (monthMultiple >= 6 ? 3 : monthMultiple >= 3 ? 5 : 8) : timeframe.isweekly ? 13 : 20
    int slowLength = timeframe.ismonthly ? (monthMultiple >= 6 ? 6 : monthMultiple >= 3 ? 10 : 21) : timeframe.isweekly ? 34 : 50
    int contextLength = timeframe.ismonthly ? (monthMultiple >= 6 ? 5 : monthMultiple >= 3 ? 7 : 10) : timeframe.isweekly ? 10 : 14
    float fast = ta.ema(close, fastLength)
    float slow = ta.ema(close, slowLength)
    float momentum = ta.rsi(close, contextLength)
    float volatility = ta.atr(contextLength)
    bool ready = not na(slow[3]) and not na(momentum[1]) and not na(volatility[1]) and volatility[1] > 0
    float separation = ready ? (fast[1] - slow[1]) / volatility[1] : 0.0
    float slope = ready ? (fast[1] - fast[3]) / volatility[1] : 0.0
    int score = not ready ? 0 : (close[1] > fast[1] ? 1 : close[1] < fast[1] ? -1 : 0) + (separation >= 0.10 ? 1 : separation <= -0.10 ? -1 : 0) + (slope >= 0.05 ? 1 : slope <= -0.05 ? -1 : 0) + (momentum[1] >= 55 ? 1 : momentum[1] <= 45 ? -1 : 0)
    not ready ? 9 : score >= 3 ? 2 : score >= 1 ? 1 : score <= -3 ? -2 : score <= -1 ? -1 : 0

f_bias_text(int state) =>
    state == 9 ? "انتظار" : state == 2 ? "صاعد قوي" : state == 1 ? "صاعد" : state == -2 ? "هابط قوي" : state == -1 ? "هابط" : "محايد"

bool stockSymbolAllowed = syminfo.type == "stock" or syminfo.type == "fund" or syminfo.type == "dr"
int chartSeconds = int(timeframe.in_seconds())
int maximumSupportedSeconds = int(timeframe.in_seconds("D"))
bool supportedTimeframe = timeframe.isintraday and chartSeconds >= 60 and chartSeconds < maximumSupportedSeconds
bool standardChartAllowed = chart.is_standard  // الشموع العادية والمفرغة تستخدم OHLC الحقيقي؛ Heikin Ashi والأنواع الصناعية غير معتمدة للتنفيذ.
bool stockChartAllowed = stockSymbolAllowed and supportedTimeframe and standardChartAllowed
// إشارات الشارت مصدرها الفاصل المفتوح فقط. الفواصل الأعلى للرأي المترابط والجدول ولا يرسمان أحداثا على الشموع.
bool chartSignalDisplayAllowed = stockChartAllowed
bool entrySessionPass = not onlyRegularSessionEntries or session.ismarket
string priceContextText = "سعر السهم"

// Apply a moderate additional horizon factor instead of multiplying time twice.
// daily boundary enough room for swing targets.
f_timeframe_display_profile() =>
    float horizonFactor = chartSeconds <= 60 ? 1.00 : chartSeconds <= 5 * 60 ? 1.05 : chartSeconds <= 15 * 60 ? 1.15 : chartSeconds <= 30 * 60 ? 1.25 : chartSeconds <= 60 * 60 ? 1.35 : chartSeconds <= 4 * 60 * 60 ? 1.55 : 1.75
    string horizonText = chartSeconds <= 60 ? "سكالب لحظي" : chartSeconds <= 5 * 60 ? "لحظي داخل الجلسة" : chartSeconds <= 30 * 60 ? "لحظي يومي" : chartSeconds < 24 * 60 * 60 ? "قصير إلى متوسط" : "سوينغ يومي"
    bool compactDisplay = adaptiveCompactDisplay and chartSeconds <= 30 * 60
    bool fastestDisplay = compactDisplay and chartSeconds <= 5 * 60
    int boxOffset = fastestDisplay ? math.max(planBoxOffsetBars, 14) : compactDisplay ? math.max(planBoxOffsetBars, 10) : math.max(planBoxOffsetBars, 7)
    int trendTransparency = compactDisplay ? 72 : 62
    [horizonFactor, horizonText, compactDisplay, fastestDisplay, boxOffset, trendTransparency, (compactDisplay ? 86 : 78), (compactDisplay ? 95 : 93), (compactDisplay ? 91 : 84), (compactDisplay ? 91 : 82), (compactDisplay ? 97 : 94), (compactDisplay ? 82 : 68), (compactDisplay ? 12 : 5), (compactDisplay ? 12 : 5), (compactDisplay ? 25 : 15), (compactDisplay ? 30 : 20), (compactDisplay ? 35 : 25)]

bool adaptiveTargetsEnabled = targetAdaptationMode == TARGET_AUTO
[automaticTargetHorizonFactor, automaticTargetHorizonText, compactIntradayDisplay, fastestIntradayDisplay, visualPlanBoxOffsetBars, trendLineTransparency, trendZoneLineTransparency, trendZoneFillTransparency, channelLineTransparency, zoneBorderTransparency, zoneFillTransparency, wyckoffRangeLineTransparency, planEntryLineTransparency, planStopLineTransparency, planTarget1LineTransparency, planTarget2LineTransparency, planTarget3LineTransparency] = f_timeframe_display_profile()
float targetHorizonFactor = adaptiveTargetsEnabled ? automaticTargetHorizonFactor : 1.0
string currentTargetHorizonText = adaptiveTargetsEnabled ? automaticTargetHorizonText + " ×" + str.tostring(targetHorizonFactor, "#.##") : "أهداف ثابتة ×1"
// الفواصل اللحظية تحتاج طبقة عرض أخف من اليومي والأسبوعي. لا تتغير
// الحسابات أو الشروط هنا؛ التكيف بصري فقط حتى لا تغطي العلامات الشموع.
// their nearer offset, while entry/stop/target captions use three times the distance.
int visualExplanationOffsetBars = visualPlanBoxOffsetBars * 3

f_new_plan_level_label(float level, color textColor) =>
    label.new(bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars, level, "", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_none, color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = textColor, size = size.tiny, textalign = text.align_left, force_overlay = true)

if barstate.isfirst and not supportedTimeframe
    runtime.error("نسخة اللحظي تعمل على الفواصل اللحظية من دقيقة فما فوق وأقل من اليومي. استخدم النسخة اليومية لليومي والأسبوعي والشهري والربع سنوي.")
if barstate.isfirst and not standardChartAllowed
    runtime.error("استخدم شموعا سعرية قياسية. Heikin-Ashi وRenko وKagi وRange والرسوم الاصطناعية غير مدعومة لأن أسعارها وأحجامها ليست أسعار التنفيذ الفعلية.")
if barstate.isfirst and not stockSymbolAllowed
    runtime.error("هذا المؤشر مخصص لأسهم وصناديق وحقوق إيداع مدرجة فقط، ولا يحلل العقود أو العملات أو المؤشرات الاصطناعية.")
// قاعدة 2+2 هي الحد الأدنى الكلاسيكي للمحور الصحيح. يستطيع المستخدم رفع
// القوة يدويا للحصول على قمم وقيعان هيكلية أبطأ وأقل عددا.
int pivotBars = swingMode == AUTO_MODE ? (2) : manualPivotBars
// محاور الرسم أقوى من محاور بنية 2+2. تتسع تلقائيا على الفواصل السريعة
// لتصفية الضوضاء، وتستخدم قيمة مستقلة لحد اليومي المشترك.
int channelPivotBars = swingMode == MANUAL_MODE ? math.max(manualPivotBars, 3) : chartSeconds <= 60 ? 5 : chartSeconds <= 5 * 60 ? 4 : chartSeconds <= 30 * 60 ? 4 : chartSeconds < 24 * 60 * 60 ? 3 : 4
int majorChannelPivotBars = math.min(20, math.max(channelPivotBars + 2, int(math.round(float(channelPivotBars) * 1.50))))
string channelPivotRuleText = "الفرعي " + str.tostring(channelPivotBars) + "+" + str.tostring(channelPivotBars) + " | الرئيسي " + str.tostring(majorChannelPivotBars) + "+" + str.tostring(majorChannelPivotBars) + " | المنطقة " + str.tostring(trendZoneAtrWidth, "#.##") + " ATR"
string higherTimeframe1 = chartSeconds <= 60 ? "5" : chartSeconds <= 5 * 60 ? "15" : chartSeconds <= 15 * 60 ? "60" : chartSeconds <= 30 * 60 ? "120" : chartSeconds <= 60 * 60 ? "240" : "D"
string higherTimeframe2 = chartSeconds <= 60 ? "15" : chartSeconds <= 5 * 60 ? "60" : chartSeconds <= 30 * 60 ? "240" : chartSeconds <= 60 * 60 ? "D" : "W"
string higherTimeframe3 = chartSeconds <= 60 ? "60" : chartSeconds <= 5 * 60 ? "D" : chartSeconds <= 30 * 60 ? "D" : chartSeconds <= 60 * 60 ? "W" : "M"
// كل طلب يقرأ آخر شمعة أعلى مكتملة فقط؛ الفاصل الحالي يبقى مصدر التنفيذ.
int higherTrendRaw1 = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, higherTimeframe1, f_closed_htf_state(), lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_on)
int higherTrendRaw2 = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, higherTimeframe2, f_closed_htf_state(), lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_on)
int higherTrendRaw3 = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, higherTimeframe3, f_closed_htf_state(), lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_on)
int higherState1 = higherTrendRaw1 == 9 ? 0 : higherTrendRaw1
int higherState2 = higherTrendRaw2 == 9 ? 0 : higherTrendRaw2
int higherState3 = higherTrendRaw3 == 9 ? 0 : higherTrendRaw3
bool higherTimeframeConflict = math.max(higherState1, math.max(higherState2, higherState3)) > 0 and math.min(higherState1, math.min(higherState2, higherState3)) < 0
int higherWeightedScore = higherState1 * 3 + higherState2 * 2 + higherState3
float higherLongPenalty = requireHigherTimeframeAlignment ? math.min(10.0, float(math.max(0, -higherWeightedScore))) : 0.0
float higherShortPenalty = requireHigherTimeframeAlignment ? math.min(10.0, float(math.max(0, higherWeightedScore))) : 0.0
int channelReturnWindowBars = fakeoutWindow

// Otherwise an anchor equal to the chart interval resets every bar and
// degenerates into the bar's own typical price. The daily chart uses a monthly anchor.
string automaticVwapAnchorTimeframe = chartSeconds <= 60 * 60 ? "D" : chartSeconds < 24 * 60 * 60 ? "W" : "M"
string requestedVwapAnchorTimeframe = vwapAnchorMode == ANCHOR_DAY ? "D" : vwapAnchorMode == ANCHOR_WEEK ? "W" : vwapAnchorMode == ANCHOR_MONTH ? "M" : automaticVwapAnchorTimeframe
int requestedVwapAnchorSeconds = int(timeframe.in_seconds(requestedVwapAnchorTimeframe))
string effectiveVwapAnchorTimeframe = requestedVwapAnchorSeconds > chartSeconds ? requestedVwapAnchorTimeframe : higherTimeframe1
bool selectedVwapAnchor = barstate.isfirst or timeframe.change(effectiveVwapAnchorTimeframe)

// =============================================================================
// Core calculations and optional filters
// =============================================================================
float atrValue = ta.atr(atrLength)
float safeAtr = nz(atrValue, math.max(high - low, syminfo.mintick))
// Independent plan-management implementation of a volatility trail. These
// series are calculated globally on every bar, but never create an entry.
float postTargetTrailAtr = ta.atr(postTargetTrailLength)
// ta.lowest over a fixed window could import a pre-entry extreme and jump the
// trail unrealistically close to price immediately after T1.
bool postTargetTrailReady = not na(postTargetTrailAtr)
float ema20 = ta.ema(close, 20)
float ema50 = ta.ema(close, 50)
float rsiValue = ta.rsi(close, rsiLength)

// independent vote. MACD is intentionally omitted because it is derived from
// the same EMA family and would duplicate trend/momentum already represented by
// structure, the higher timeframe and this context.
bool fastIntradayMaContext = timeframe.isintraday and chartSeconds <= 60 * 60
int adaptiveMaFastLength = fastIntradayMaContext ? 9 : timeframe.isintraday ? 20 : 50
int adaptiveMaSlowLength = fastIntradayMaContext ? 20 : timeframe.isintraday ? 50 : 200
[adaptiveFastAverage, adaptiveSlowAverage, adaptiveMaBias, momentumContextState] = f_momentum_context((timeframe.isintraday), adaptiveMaFastLength, adaptiveMaSlowLength, close, rsiValue, rsiLength)
float adaptiveTrendCenter = f_vidya_center(close, adaptiveMaFastLength, adaptiveMaSlowLength)
float adaptiveTrendBand = safeAtr * 1.50
var int adaptiveTrendBias = 0
if barstate.isconfirmed and not na(adaptiveTrendCenter)
    adaptiveTrendBias := close > adaptiveTrendCenter + adaptiveTrendBand ? 1 : close < adaptiveTrendCenter - adaptiveTrendBand ? -1 : adaptiveTrendBias


// Minute and higher intraday RVOL compares the same clock-time slot across prior
// regular sessions. Daily-or-higher charts use closed rolling bars.
var array<float> rvolSlotHistory = array.new<float>(timeframe.isintraday ? 1440 * intradayRvolSessions : 0, na)
var array<float> rvolSlotSums = array.new<float>(timeframe.isintraday ? 1440 : 0, 0.0)
var array<int> rvolSlotCounts = array.new<int>(timeframe.isintraday ? 1440 : 0, 0)
var array<int> rvolSessionTouchedSlots = array.new<int>(timeframe.isintraday ? 1440 * intradayRvolSessions : 0, -1)
var array<int> rvolSessionTouchedCounts = array.new<int>(timeframe.isintraday ? intradayRvolSessions : 0, 0)
var int rvolRingIndex = 0

bool useIntradaySlotRvol = timeframe.isintraday and chartSeconds >= 60
bool newRvolTradingDay = useIntradaySlotRvol and (barstate.isfirst or time_tradingday != time_tradingday[1])
if newRvolTradingDay and not barstate.isfirst
    rvolRingIndex := (rvolRingIndex + 1) % intradayRvolSessions
    // Remove the complete session that just fell out of the rolling window.
    // Clearing only minutes that trade today leaves stale observations behind on
    // halted/illiquid stocks and silently compares against more than N sessions.
    // The touched-slot list avoids scanning all 1440 clock minutes every day.
    int rvolExpiredTouchedCount = array.get(rvolSessionTouchedCounts, rvolRingIndex)
    if rvolExpiredTouchedCount > 0
        for rvolClearPosition = 0 to rvolExpiredTouchedCount - 1
            int rvolTouchedStorageIndex = rvolRingIndex * 1440 + rvolClearPosition
            int rvolClearMinute = array.get(rvolSessionTouchedSlots, rvolTouchedStorageIndex)
            if rvolClearMinute >= 0 and rvolClearMinute < 1440
                int rvolClearHistoryIndex = rvolClearMinute * intradayRvolSessions + rvolRingIndex
                float rvolExpiredVolume = array.get(rvolSlotHistory, rvolClearHistoryIndex)
                if not na(rvolExpiredVolume)
                    float rvolClearedSum = math.max(0.0, array.get(rvolSlotSums, rvolClearMinute) - rvolExpiredVolume)
                    int rvolClearedCount = math.max(0, array.get(rvolSlotCounts, rvolClearMinute) - 1)
                    array.set(rvolSlotSums, rvolClearMinute, rvolClearedSum)
                    array.set(rvolSlotCounts, rvolClearMinute, rvolClearedCount)
                    array.set(rvolSlotHistory, rvolClearHistoryIndex, na)
                array.set(rvolSessionTouchedSlots, rvolTouchedStorageIndex, -1)
    array.set(rvolSessionTouchedCounts, rvolRingIndex, 0)

int rvolMinuteSlot = hour(time, syminfo.timezone) * 60 + minute(time, syminfo.timezone)
int rvolHistoryIndex = rvolMinuteSlot * intradayRvolSessions + rvolRingIndex
float rvolOldSlotVolume = useIntradaySlotRvol ? array.get(rvolSlotHistory, rvolHistoryIndex) : na
float rvolReferenceSum = useIntradaySlotRvol ? array.get(rvolSlotSums, rvolMinuteSlot) - nz(rvolOldSlotVolume, 0.0) : na
int rvolReferenceCount = useIntradaySlotRvol ? array.get(rvolSlotCounts, rvolMinuteSlot) - (na(rvolOldSlotVolume) ? 0 : 1) : 0
float intradayVolumeAverage = rvolReferenceCount >= 3 ? rvolReferenceSum / float(rvolReferenceCount) : na
float rollingVolumeAverage = ta.sma(volume, volumeLength)[1]
bool regularSessionVolumeBar = not timeframe.isintraday or session.ismarket
float volumeAverage = useIntradaySlotRvol ? intradayVolumeAverage : rollingVolumeAverage
bool volumeAvailable = regularSessionVolumeBar and not na(volume) and not na(volumeAverage) and volumeAverage > 0
float relativeVolume = volumeAvailable ? volume / volumeAverage : na
int volumeRegimeLength = timeframe.ismonthly ? 24 : timeframe.isweekly ? 52 : math.max(30, volumeLength * 5)
float volumeRegimeMean = ta.sma(relativeVolume[1], volumeRegimeLength)
float volumeRegimeDeviation = ta.stdev(relativeVolume[1], volumeRegimeLength)
float volumeZScore = volumeAvailable and not na(volumeRegimeMean) and volumeRegimeDeviation > 0 ? (relativeVolume - volumeRegimeMean) / volumeRegimeDeviation : na
bool statisticalVolumeHigh = not na(volumeZScore) and volumeZScore >= 1.0
bool statisticalVolumeExtreme = not na(volumeZScore) and volumeZScore >= 2.0
// طبقات الحجم مبنية على Z-Score للحجم النسبي نفسه. لحظيًا يحافظ ذلك على
// مقارنة وقت الجلسة المماثل بدل تضخيم افتتاح وإغلاق السوق بسبب الحجم الخام.
float heatmapVolumeZ = volumeZScore
int heatmapVolumeTier = na(heatmapVolumeZ) ? 0 : heatmapVolumeZ > 4.0 ? 5 : heatmapVolumeZ > 2.5 ? 4 : heatmapVolumeZ > 1.0 ? 3 : heatmapVolumeZ > -0.5 ? 2 : 1
bool heatmapVolumeMediumOrHigher = heatmapVolumeTier >= 3
bool heatmapVolumeHighOrHigher = heatmapVolumeTier >= 4
bool heatmapVolumeExtreme = heatmapVolumeTier >= 5
bool standardVolumeEffort = relativeVolume >= volumeMultiple or heatmapVolumeMediumOrHigher
bool volumePass = not useVolumeFilter or (volumeAvailable and standardVolumeEffort)

if useIntradaySlotRvol and regularSessionVolumeBar and barstate.isconfirmed and not na(volume) and volume >= 0
    float updatedRvolSum = rvolReferenceSum + volume
    int updatedRvolCount = rvolReferenceCount + 1
    if na(rvolOldSlotVolume)
        int rvolTouchedCount = array.get(rvolSessionTouchedCounts, rvolRingIndex)
        if rvolTouchedCount < 1440
            int rvolTouchedStorageIndex = rvolRingIndex * 1440 + rvolTouchedCount
            array.set(rvolSessionTouchedSlots, rvolTouchedStorageIndex, rvolMinuteSlot)
            array.set(rvolSessionTouchedCounts, rvolRingIndex, rvolTouchedCount + 1)
    array.set(rvolSlotHistory, rvolHistoryIndex, volume)
    array.set(rvolSlotSums, rvolMinuteSlot, updatedRvolSum)
    array.set(rvolSlotCounts, rvolMinuteSlot, updatedRvolCount)

float vwapValue = ta.vwap(hlc3, selectedVwapAnchor)
// Structural VWAP is anchored by events calculated later in the script. The
// entry gate therefore uses its last confirmed value, avoiding a circular same-
// bar dependency. Until the first structural anchor is ready, periodic VWAP is
// the explicit fallback.
var float confirmedAnalysisVwapValue = na
var bool confirmedStructuralVwapReady = false
float entryVwapValue = useStructuralVwap and confirmedStructuralVwapReady and not na(confirmedAnalysisVwapValue) ? confirmedAnalysisVwapValue : vwapValue
bool vwapLongPass = not useVwapFilter or (not na(entryVwapValue) and close > entryVwapValue)
bool vwapShortPass = not useVwapFilter or (not na(entryVwapValue) and close < entryVwapValue)

f_update_fvg_state() =>
    var bool bullishFvgActive = false
    var bool bearishFvgActive = false
    var float bullishFvgBottom = na
    var float bearishFvgTop = na
    var int bullishFvgBar = na
    var int bearishFvgBar = na
    bool fvgBullPass = not useFvgFilter
    bool fvgBearPass = not useFvgFilter
    bool recentBullFvg = false
    bool recentBearFvg = false
    float middleCandleRange = math.max(high[1] - low[1], syminfo.mintick)
    float middleCandleBodyRatio = math.abs(close[1] - open[1]) / middleCandleRange
    float expectedBarMilliseconds = float(chartSeconds) * 1000.0
    bool fvgBarsContinuous = not timeframe.isintraday or (not na(time[2]) and float(time - time[1]) <= expectedBarMilliseconds * 1.5 and float(time[1] - time[2]) <= expectedBarMilliseconds * 1.5)
    bool fvgSameTradingSession = not ignoreOpeningGapFvg or not timeframe.isintraday or (time_tradingday == time_tradingday[2] and fvgBarsContinuous)
    bool fvgDisplacementVolumePass = not confirmFvgDisplacement or (volumeAvailable[1] and relativeVolume[1] >= volumeMultiple)
    bool fvgDisplacementBodyPass = not confirmFvgDisplacement or middleCandleBodyRatio >= 0.55
    bool bullishFvg = bar_index >= 2 and low > high[2] and close[1] > open[1] and fvgSameTradingSession and fvgDisplacementVolumePass and fvgDisplacementBodyPass
    bool bearishFvg = bar_index >= 2 and high < low[2] and close[1] < open[1] and fvgSameTradingSession and fvgDisplacementVolumePass and fvgDisplacementBodyPass

    if bullishFvg
        bullishFvgActive := true
        bullishFvgBottom := high[2]
        bullishFvgBar := bar_index
    if bearishFvg
        bearishFvgActive := true
        bearishFvgTop := low[2]
        bearishFvgBar := bar_index

    // الفجوة تبقى صالحة فقط ما دامت حديثة ولم تُملأ بالكامل بعودة السعر إلى حدها البعيد.
    bool bullishFvgFilled = bullishFvgActive and not na(bullishFvgBar) and bar_index > bullishFvgBar and low <= bullishFvgBottom
    bool bearishFvgFilled = bearishFvgActive and not na(bearishFvgBar) and bar_index > bearishFvgBar and high >= bearishFvgTop
    if bullishFvgFilled or (bullishFvgActive and bar_index - bullishFvgBar > fvgLookback)
        bullishFvgActive := false
        bullishFvgBottom := na
        bullishFvgBar := na
    if bearishFvgFilled or (bearishFvgActive and bar_index - bearishFvgBar > fvgLookback)
        bearishFvgActive := false
        bearishFvgTop := na
        bearishFvgBar := na

    int barsSinceBullFvg = bullishFvgActive and not na(bullishFvgBar) ? bar_index - bullishFvgBar : 100000
    int barsSinceBearFvg = bearishFvgActive and not na(bearishFvgBar) ? bar_index - bearishFvgBar : 100000
    recentBullFvg := bullishFvgActive and barsSinceBullFvg <= fvgLookback
    recentBearFvg := bearishFvgActive and barsSinceBearFvg <= fvgLookback
    fvgBullPass := not useFvgFilter or recentBullFvg
    fvgBearPass := not useFvgFilter or recentBearFvg

    [bullishFvgActive, bearishFvgActive, bullishFvgBottom, bearishFvgTop, bullishFvgBar, bearishFvgBar, fvgBullPass, fvgBearPass, recentBullFvg, recentBearFvg]

[bullishFvgActive, bearishFvgActive, bullishFvgBottom, bearishFvgTop, bullishFvgBar, bearishFvgBar, fvgBullPass, fvgBearPass, recentBullFvg, recentBearFvg] = f_update_fvg_state()
float candleRange = math.max(high - low, syminfo.mintick)
float bodyRatio = math.abs(close - open) / candleRange
float averageSpread = ta.sma(candleRange, volumeLength)
float relativeSpread = not na(averageSpread) and averageSpread > 0 ? candleRange / averageSpread : na
float closeLocation = (close - low) / candleRange
bool vsaHighEffort = useVsaAnalysis and volumeAvailable and standardVolumeEffort
bool vsaWideResult = not na(relativeSpread) and relativeSpread >= 1.20
bool vsaNarrowResult = not na(relativeSpread) and relativeSpread <= 0.75
bool bullishVsaEffortResult = vsaHighEffort and vsaWideResult and closeLocation >= 0.65
bool bearishVsaEffortResult = vsaHighEffort and vsaWideResult and closeLocation <= 0.35

// =============================================================================
// Dow structure: every strength uses a strict symmetric pivot. The candidate
// must be strictly above/below every candle on both sides; equal highs/lows do
// not become separate pivots. Only the latest confirmed pair is shown.
// =============================================================================
f_confirm_symmetric_pivots() =>
    float pivotHigh = na
    float pivotLow = na
    bool newPivotHigh = false
    bool newPivotLow = false
    float structureRightHigh = ta.highest(high, pivotBars)
    float structureLeftHigh = ta.highest(high[pivotBars + 1], pivotBars)
    float structureRightLow = ta.lowest(low, pivotBars)
    float structureLeftLow = ta.lowest(low[pivotBars + 1], pivotBars)
    bool strictPivotHigh = bar_index >= pivotBars * 2 and high[pivotBars] > structureRightHigh and high[pivotBars] > structureLeftHigh
    bool strictPivotLow = bar_index >= pivotBars * 2 and low[pivotBars] < structureRightLow and low[pivotBars] < structureLeftLow
    pivotHigh := strictPivotHigh ? high[pivotBars] : na
    pivotLow := strictPivotLow ? low[pivotBars] : na
    newPivotHigh := stockChartAllowed and not na(pivotHigh) and barstate.isconfirmed
    newPivotLow := stockChartAllowed and not na(pivotLow) and barstate.isconfirmed

    [pivotHigh, pivotLow, newPivotHigh, newPivotLow]

[pivotHigh, pivotLow, newPivotHigh, newPivotLow] = f_confirm_symmetric_pivots()
var float lastSwingHigh = na
var float lastSwingLow = na
var int lastSwingHighBar = na
var int lastSwingLowBar = na
var float lastPivotHighRsi = na
var float lastPivotLowRsi = na
var string lastHighTag = "-"
var string lastLowTag = "-"
var bool highWasBroken = false
var bool lowWasBroken = false
var bool highWasSwept = false
var bool lowWasSwept = false

var bool doubleTopArmed = false
var bool doubleBottomArmed = false
var float doubleTopNeckline = na
var float doubleBottomNeckline = na
var float doubleTopPrice = na
var float doubleBottomPrice = na
var float doubleTopHeight = na
var float doubleBottomHeight = na
var int doubleTopArmedBar = na
var int doubleBottomArmedBar = na

var array<float> srHighPivotPrices = array.new<float>()
var array<int> srHighPivotBars = array.new<int>()
var array<float> srLowPivotPrices = array.new<float>()
var array<int> srLowPivotBars = array.new<int>()
var float strongResistanceLevel = na
var float strongResistanceLow = na
var float strongResistanceHigh = na
var int strongResistanceTouches = 0
var int strongResistanceRecentBar = na
var float strongSupportLevel = na
var float strongSupportLow = na
var float strongSupportHigh = na
var int strongSupportTouches = 0
var int strongSupportRecentBar = na

bool bearishRsiDivergence = false
bool bullishRsiDivergence = false

float pivotTolerance = nz(atrValue[pivotBars], safeAtr) * equalAtrPart
float patternTolerance = math.max(pivotTolerance, nz(atrValue[pivotBars], safeAtr) * 0.25)

if newPivotHigh
    int pivotBar = bar_index - pivotBars
    float confirmedPivotRsi = rsiValue[pivotBars]
    array.unshift(srHighPivotPrices, pivotHigh)
    array.unshift(srHighPivotBars, pivotBar)
    while array.size(srHighPivotPrices) > srStoredPivots
        array.pop(srHighPivotPrices)
        array.pop(srHighPivotBars)
    bearishRsiDivergence := useRsiDivergence and not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastPivotHighRsi) and pivotHigh >= lastSwingHigh - pivotTolerance and confirmedPivotRsi <= lastPivotHighRsi - rsiDivergenceGap
