    bool validDoubleTop = useChartPatterns and not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLow) and not na(lastSwingHighBar) and not na(lastSwingLowBar) and lastSwingLowBar > lastSwingHighBar and lastSwingLowBar < pivotBar and math.abs(pivotHigh - lastSwingHigh) <= patternTolerance and math.min(pivotHigh, lastSwingHigh) - lastSwingLow >= safeAtr
    if validDoubleTop
        doubleTopArmed := true
        doubleTopArmedBar := pivotBar
        doubleTopNeckline := lastSwingLow
        doubleTopPrice := (pivotHigh + lastSwingHigh) * 0.50
        doubleTopHeight := doubleTopPrice - doubleTopNeckline
    else if not na(lastSwingHigh) and pivotHigh > lastSwingHigh + patternTolerance
        doubleTopArmed := false
        doubleTopArmedBar := na
        doubleTopNeckline := na
        doubleTopPrice := na
        doubleTopHeight := na
    string highTag = na(lastSwingHigh) ? "H" : pivotHigh > lastSwingHigh + pivotTolerance ? "HH" : pivotHigh < lastSwingHigh - pivotTolerance ? "LH" : "EH"
    lastSwingHigh := pivotHigh
    lastSwingHighBar := pivotBar
    lastPivotHighRsi := confirmedPivotRsi
    lastHighTag := highTag
    highWasBroken := false
    highWasSwept := false


if newPivotLow
    int pivotBar = bar_index - pivotBars
    float confirmedPivotRsi = rsiValue[pivotBars]
    array.unshift(srLowPivotPrices, pivotLow)
    array.unshift(srLowPivotBars, pivotBar)
    while array.size(srLowPivotPrices) > srStoredPivots
        array.pop(srLowPivotPrices)
        array.pop(srLowPivotBars)
    bullishRsiDivergence := useRsiDivergence and not na(lastSwingLow) and not na(lastPivotLowRsi) and pivotLow <= lastSwingLow + pivotTolerance and confirmedPivotRsi >= lastPivotLowRsi + rsiDivergenceGap
    bool validDoubleBottom = useChartPatterns and not na(lastSwingLow) and not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLowBar) and not na(lastSwingHighBar) and lastSwingHighBar > lastSwingLowBar and lastSwingHighBar < pivotBar and math.abs(pivotLow - lastSwingLow) <= patternTolerance and lastSwingHigh - math.max(pivotLow, lastSwingLow) >= safeAtr
    if validDoubleBottom
        doubleBottomArmed := true
        doubleBottomArmedBar := pivotBar
        doubleBottomNeckline := lastSwingHigh
        doubleBottomPrice := (pivotLow + lastSwingLow) * 0.50
        doubleBottomHeight := doubleBottomNeckline - doubleBottomPrice
    else if not na(lastSwingLow) and pivotLow < lastSwingLow - patternTolerance
        doubleBottomArmed := false
        doubleBottomArmedBar := na
        doubleBottomNeckline := na
        doubleBottomPrice := na
        doubleBottomHeight := na
    string lowTag = na(lastSwingLow) ? "L" : pivotLow > lastSwingLow + pivotTolerance ? "HL" : pivotLow < lastSwingLow - pivotTolerance ? "LL" : "EL"
    lastSwingLow := pivotLow
    lastSwingLowBar := pivotBar
    lastPivotLowRsi := confirmedPivotRsi
    lastLowTag := lowTag
    lowWasBroken := false
    lowWasSwept := false


// Recalculate on a new confirmed pivot or exactly when a stored touch ages out.
// price-clustering loops on every bar.
var int nextHorizontalSrExpiryBar = na
bool horizontalSrAgeRefresh = useHorizontalSr and barstate.isconfirmed and not na(nextHorizontalSrExpiryBar) and bar_index >= nextHorizontalSrExpiryBar
bool horizontalSrRecalculated = useHorizontalSr and barstate.isconfirmed and (newPivotHigh or newPivotLow or horizontalSrAgeRefresh)
bool strongResistanceClusterChanged = false
bool strongSupportClusterChanged = false
if horizontalSrRecalculated
    float horizontalSrTolerance = math.max(safeAtr * srAtrTolerance, syminfo.mintick * 5.0)
    [resistanceLevelCandidate, resistanceLowCandidate, resistanceHighCandidate, resistanceTouchesCandidate, resistanceRecentBarCandidate] = f_strong_sr_cluster(srHighPivotPrices, srHighPivotBars, horizontalSrTolerance, srMinimumTouches, srMaximumAgeBars)
    [supportLevelCandidate, supportLowCandidate, supportHighCandidate, supportTouchesCandidate, supportRecentBarCandidate] = f_strong_sr_cluster(srLowPivotPrices, srLowPivotBars, horizontalSrTolerance, srMinimumTouches, srMaximumAgeBars)
    strongResistanceClusterChanged := f_float_changed(strongResistanceLevel, resistanceLevelCandidate) or f_float_changed(strongResistanceLow, resistanceLowCandidate) or f_float_changed(strongResistanceHigh, resistanceHighCandidate) or strongResistanceTouches != resistanceTouchesCandidate or f_int_changed(strongResistanceRecentBar, resistanceRecentBarCandidate)
    strongSupportClusterChanged := f_float_changed(strongSupportLevel, supportLevelCandidate) or f_float_changed(strongSupportLow, supportLowCandidate) or f_float_changed(strongSupportHigh, supportHighCandidate) or strongSupportTouches != supportTouchesCandidate or f_int_changed(strongSupportRecentBar, supportRecentBarCandidate)
    strongResistanceLevel := resistanceLevelCandidate
    strongResistanceLow := resistanceLowCandidate
    strongResistanceHigh := resistanceHighCandidate
    strongResistanceTouches := resistanceTouchesCandidate
    strongResistanceRecentBar := resistanceRecentBarCandidate
    strongSupportLevel := supportLevelCandidate
    strongSupportLow := supportLowCandidate
    strongSupportHigh := supportHighCandidate
    strongSupportTouches := supportTouchesCandidate
    strongSupportRecentBar := supportRecentBarCandidate
    int nextResistanceExpiryBar = f_next_sr_expiry(srHighPivotBars, srMaximumAgeBars)
    int nextSupportExpiryBar = f_next_sr_expiry(srLowPivotBars, srMaximumAgeBars)
    nextHorizontalSrExpiryBar := na(nextResistanceExpiryBar) ? nextSupportExpiryBar : na(nextSupportExpiryBar) ? nextResistanceExpiryBar : math.min(nextResistanceExpiryBar, nextSupportExpiryBar)

bool strongResistanceExpired = barstate.isconfirmed and not na(strongResistanceRecentBar) and bar_index - strongResistanceRecentBar > srMaximumAgeBars
bool strongSupportExpired = barstate.isconfirmed and not na(strongSupportRecentBar) and bar_index - strongSupportRecentBar > srMaximumAgeBars
if strongResistanceExpired
    strongResistanceLevel := na
    strongResistanceLow := na
    strongResistanceHigh := na
    strongResistanceTouches := 0
    strongResistanceRecentBar := na
if strongSupportExpired
    strongSupportLevel := na
    strongSupportLow := na
    strongSupportHigh := na
    strongSupportTouches := 0
    strongSupportRecentBar := na

bool doubleTopExpired = barstate.isconfirmed and doubleTopArmed and not na(doubleTopArmedBar) and bar_index - doubleTopArmedBar > patternMaxBars
bool doubleBottomExpired = barstate.isconfirmed and doubleBottomArmed and not na(doubleBottomArmedBar) and bar_index - doubleBottomArmedBar > patternMaxBars
if doubleTopExpired
    doubleTopArmed := false
    doubleTopArmedBar := na
    doubleTopNeckline := na
    doubleTopPrice := na
    doubleTopHeight := na
if doubleBottomExpired
    doubleBottomArmed := false
    doubleBottomArmedBar := na
    doubleBottomNeckline := na
    doubleBottomPrice := na
    doubleBottomHeight := na

int dowBias = lastHighTag == "HH" and lastLowTag == "HL" ? 1 : lastHighTag == "LH" and lastLowTag == "LL" ? -1 : 0
string dowState = dowBias == 1 ? "صاعد: قمة أعلى (HH) وقاع أعلى (HL)" : dowBias == -1 ? "هابط: قمة أدنى (LH) وقاع أدنى (LL)" : "عرضي أو انتقالي: بنية داو غير مكتملة"
int barsSinceBullishDivergence = ta.barssince(bullishRsiDivergence)
int barsSinceBearishDivergence = ta.barssince(bearishRsiDivergence)
bool recentBullishDivergence = not na(barsSinceBullishDivergence) and barsSinceBullishDivergence <= contextLookback
bool recentBearishDivergence = not na(barsSinceBearishDivergence) and barsSinceBearishDivergence <= contextLookback
// =============================================================================
// Confirmed breaks, Wyckoff sweeps, and classical retests
// =============================================================================
float breakBuffer = safeAtr * breakAtrBuffer

bool rawStrongResistanceReady = useHorizontalSr and strongResistanceTouches >= srMinimumTouches and not na(strongResistanceLevel) and not na(strongResistanceLow) and not na(strongResistanceHigh)
bool rawStrongSupportReady = useHorizontalSr and strongSupportTouches >= srMinimumTouches and not na(strongSupportLevel) and not na(strongSupportLow) and not na(strongSupportHigh)
bool horizontalSrCompatible = not rawStrongResistanceReady or not rawStrongSupportReady or strongResistanceLow > strongSupportHigh + syminfo.mintick
bool strongResistanceReady = rawStrongResistanceReady and horizontalSrCompatible
bool strongSupportReady = rawStrongSupportReady and horizontalSrCompatible

// A changed level is not itself a breakout. An unrelated pivot or the expiry of
// a touch outside the selected cluster must not suppress a valid crossing.
bool strongResistanceBreak = barstate.isconfirmed and strongResistanceReady and not strongResistanceClusterChanged and close > strongResistanceHigh + breakBuffer and close[1] <= nz(strongResistanceHigh[1], strongResistanceHigh) + nz(breakBuffer[1], breakBuffer)
bool strongSupportBreak = barstate.isconfirmed and strongSupportReady and not strongSupportClusterChanged and close < strongSupportLow - breakBuffer and close[1] >= nz(strongSupportLow[1], strongSupportLow) - nz(breakBuffer[1], breakBuffer)
float horizontalSrRetestTolerance = safeAtr * retestAtrTolerance
// calculated or expired cluster invalidates the old context instead of letting
// ta.barssince() carry evidence from a level that no longer exists.
var int strongResistanceBreakContextBar = na
var int strongSupportBreakContextBar = na
if strongResistanceClusterChanged or strongResistanceExpired
    strongResistanceBreakContextBar := na
if strongSupportClusterChanged or strongSupportExpired
    strongSupportBreakContextBar := na
if strongResistanceBreak
    strongResistanceBreakContextBar := bar_index
if strongSupportBreak
    strongSupportBreakContextBar := bar_index
bool recentStrongResistanceBreak = not na(strongResistanceBreakContextBar) and bar_index - strongResistanceBreakContextBar <= contextLookback
bool recentStrongSupportBreak = not na(strongSupportBreakContextBar) and bar_index - strongSupportBreakContextBar <= contextLookback
bool strongResistanceRoleReversed = not na(strongResistanceBreakContextBar)
bool strongSupportRoleReversed = not na(strongSupportBreakContextBar)
bool touchingStrongResistanceCluster = strongResistanceReady and high >= strongResistanceLow - horizontalSrRetestTolerance and low <= strongResistanceHigh + horizontalSrRetestTolerance
bool touchingStrongSupportCluster = strongSupportReady and low <= strongSupportHigh + horizontalSrRetestTolerance and high >= strongSupportLow - horizontalSrRetestTolerance
// A broken cluster reverses its role only after a real break tied to the same
// still-valid cluster. This prevents a historical high cluster from remaining
// permanently bearish after price has accepted above it (and vice versa).
bool resistanceClusterActsAsSupport = touchingStrongResistanceCluster and strongResistanceRoleReversed and close >= strongResistanceLevel
bool supportClusterActsAsResistance = touchingStrongSupportCluster and strongSupportRoleReversed and close <= strongSupportLevel
bool nearStrongSupport = touchingStrongSupportCluster and not supportClusterActsAsResistance and close >= strongSupportLow - breakBuffer
bool nearStrongResistance = touchingStrongResistanceCluster and not resistanceClusterActsAsSupport and close <= strongResistanceHigh + breakBuffer
int horizontalSrSignal = recentStrongResistanceBreak and not recentStrongSupportBreak ? 1 : recentStrongSupportBreak and not recentStrongResistanceBreak ? -1 : resistanceClusterActsAsSupport ? 1 : supportClusterActsAsResistance ? -1 : nearStrongSupport and not nearStrongResistance ? 1 : nearStrongResistance and not nearStrongSupport ? -1 : 0
// Old clusters that are far from price are context, not current evidence and not
// useful chart drawings. Five ATR keeps nearby structure without cluttering.
float horizontalSrVisualDistance = safeAtr * 5.0
bool strongResistanceVisualRelevant = strongResistanceReady and math.abs(close - strongResistanceLevel) <= horizontalSrVisualDistance and (close <= strongResistanceHigh + horizontalSrRetestTolerance or recentStrongResistanceBreak)
bool strongSupportVisualRelevant = strongSupportReady and math.abs(close - strongSupportLevel) <= horizontalSrVisualDistance and (close >= strongSupportLow - horizontalSrRetestTolerance or recentStrongSupportBreak)
// current-bar line visibility on that same snapshot so a forming candle cannot
// make a level appear/disappear while the table still describes the prior close.
bool confirmedResistanceVisualRelevant = barstate.isconfirmed ? strongResistanceVisualRelevant : strongResistanceVisualRelevant[1]
bool confirmedSupportVisualRelevant = barstate.isconfirmed ? strongSupportVisualRelevant : strongSupportVisualRelevant[1]
f_horizontal_sr_summary() =>
    int stateCode = not useHorizontalSr ? 1 : not horizontalSrCompatible ? 2 : strongResistanceBreak ? 3 : strongSupportBreak ? 4 : resistanceClusterActsAsSupport ? 10 : supportClusterActsAsResistance ? 11 : nearStrongSupport ? 5 : nearStrongResistance ? 6 : strongResistanceVisualRelevant and strongSupportVisualRelevant ? 7 : strongResistanceVisualRelevant ? 8 : strongSupportVisualRelevant ? 9 : 0
    string stateText = stateCode == 1 ? "الدعم والمقاومة الأفقية معطلة" : stateCode == 2 ? "تجمعات الدعم والمقاومة متداخلة؛ أهملت كتأكيد" : stateCode == 3 ? "اختراق تجمع قمم قوي بإغلاق" : stateCode == 4 ? "كسر تجمع قيعان قوي بإغلاق" : stateCode == 5 ? "قرب دعم أفقي قوي " + str.tostring(strongSupportTouches) + " لمسات | " + f_price(strongSupportLevel) : stateCode == 6 ? "قرب مقاومة أفقية قوية " + str.tostring(strongResistanceTouches) + " لمسات | " + f_price(strongResistanceLevel) : stateCode == 7 ? "مقاومة " + f_price(strongResistanceLevel) + " (" + str.tostring(strongResistanceTouches) + ") | دعم " + f_price(strongSupportLevel) + " (" + str.tostring(strongSupportTouches) + ")" : stateCode == 8 ? "مقاومة أفقية " + f_price(strongResistanceLevel) + " | " + str.tostring(strongResistanceTouches) + " لمسات" : stateCode == 9 ? "دعم أفقي " + f_price(strongSupportLevel) + " | " + str.tostring(strongSupportTouches) + " لمسات" : stateCode == 10 ? "إعادة اختبار تجمع قمم مخترق كدعم | " + f_price(strongResistanceLevel) : stateCode == 11 ? "إعادة اختبار تجمع قيعان مكسور كمقاومة | " + f_price(strongSupportLevel) : "لا يوجد مستوى أفقي متعدد اللمسات قريب من السعر"
    [stateCode, stateText]

[horizontalSrStateCode, horizontalSrState] = f_horizontal_sr_summary()

bool confirmedResistanceActsAsSupport = barstate.isconfirmed ? strongResistanceRoleReversed : strongResistanceRoleReversed[1]
bool confirmedSupportActsAsResistance = barstate.isconfirmed ? strongSupportRoleReversed : strongSupportRoleReversed[1]
float confirmedResistanceTop = barstate.isconfirmed ? strongResistanceHigh : strongResistanceHigh[1]
float confirmedResistanceBottom = barstate.isconfirmed ? strongResistanceLow : strongResistanceLow[1]
float confirmedSupportTop = barstate.isconfirmed ? strongSupportHigh : strongSupportHigh[1]
float confirmedSupportBottom = barstate.isconfirmed ? strongSupportLow : strongSupportLow[1]
var box strongResistanceVisualZone = na
var box strongSupportVisualZone = na
if barstate.islast
    if showHorizontalSr and confirmedResistanceVisualRelevant and not na(confirmedResistanceTop) and not na(confirmedResistanceBottom)
        strongResistanceVisualZone := f_update_horizontal_sr_zone(strongResistanceVisualZone, nz(strongResistanceRecentBar, bar_index), bar_index + channelForwardBars, confirmedResistanceTop, confirmedResistanceBottom, (confirmedResistanceActsAsSupport ? bullColor : bearColor), confirmedResistanceActsAsSupport ? "منطقة دعم (مقاومة سابقة)" : "منطقة مقاومة")
    else if not na(strongResistanceVisualZone)
        strongResistanceVisualZone := f_clear_box(strongResistanceVisualZone)
    if showHorizontalSr and confirmedSupportVisualRelevant and not na(confirmedSupportTop) and not na(confirmedSupportBottom)
        strongSupportVisualZone := f_update_horizontal_sr_zone(strongSupportVisualZone, nz(strongSupportRecentBar, bar_index), bar_index + channelForwardBars, confirmedSupportTop, confirmedSupportBottom, (confirmedSupportActsAsResistance ? bearColor : bullColor), confirmedSupportActsAsResistance ? "منطقة مقاومة (دعم سابق)" : "منطقة دعم")
    else if not na(strongSupportVisualZone)
        strongSupportVisualZone := f_clear_box(strongSupportVisualZone)

// not count its internal statements against the main-body compilation limit.
[majorChannelBias, majorTrendReady, majorChannelReady, majorChannelBaseNow, majorChannelLowerNow, majorChannelUpperNow, majorChannelWidth, majorFirstAnchorBar, majorFirstAnchorPrice, majorSecondAnchorBar, majorSecondAnchorPrice, majorBreakoutUpEvent, majorBreakoutDownEvent, majorChannelBreakoutUp, majorChannelBreakoutDown, majorChannelUpperFakeout, majorChannelLowerFakeout, majorChannelSignal, bullishMajorChannelTarget, bearishMajorChannelTarget, majorChannelState] = f_calculate_major_layer(majorChannelPivotBars, channelValidationMaxBars, safeAtr, breakAtrBuffer, trendZoneAtrWidth, channelMaxAtrProjection, channelMaximumSlopeAtr, channelMinimumWidthConsistency, dowBias, breakBuffer, fakeoutWindow, retestWindow, channelForwardBars)

// Secondary trend and channel pivots are confirmed only after their right-side bars close.
// one meaningful trend and at most one parallel boundary on the selected timeframe.
float channelRightHigh = ta.highest(high, channelPivotBars)
float channelLeftHigh = ta.highest(high[channelPivotBars + 1], channelPivotBars)
float channelRightLow = ta.lowest(low, channelPivotBars)
float channelLeftLow = ta.lowest(low[channelPivotBars + 1], channelPivotBars)
bool strictImportantPivotHigh = bar_index >= channelPivotBars * 2 and high[channelPivotBars] > channelRightHigh and high[channelPivotBars] > channelLeftHigh
bool strictImportantPivotLow = bar_index >= channelPivotBars * 2 and low[channelPivotBars] < channelRightLow and low[channelPivotBars] < channelLeftLow
float importantPivotHighCandidate = strictImportantPivotHigh ? high[channelPivotBars] : na
float importantPivotLowCandidate = strictImportantPivotLow ? low[channelPivotBars] : na
int importantPivotCandidateBar = bar_index - channelPivotBars

var float previousImportantHigh = na
var float lastImportantHigh = na
var int previousImportantHighBar = na
var int lastImportantHighBar = na
var float previousImportantLow = na
var float lastImportantLow = na
var int previousImportantLowBar = na
var int lastImportantLowBar = na

if barstate.isconfirmed and not na(importantPivotHighCandidate)
    previousImportantHigh := lastImportantHigh
    previousImportantHighBar := lastImportantHighBar
    lastImportantHigh := importantPivotHighCandidate
    lastImportantHighBar := importantPivotCandidateBar

if barstate.isconfirmed and not na(importantPivotLowCandidate)
    previousImportantLow := lastImportantLow
    previousImportantLowBar := lastImportantLowBar
    lastImportantLow := importantPivotLowCandidate
    lastImportantLowBar := importantPivotCandidateBar

// Same-side channel anchors require explicit temporal separation. The pivot
// function normally enforces this implicitly; the guard protects the geometry
// if pivot behavior or manual strengths change later.
int minimumChannelAnchorBars = channelPivotBars + 1
bool importantHighTrendReady = not na(previousImportantHigh) and not na(lastImportantHigh) and not na(previousImportantHighBar) and not na(lastImportantHighBar) and lastImportantHighBar - previousImportantHighBar >= minimumChannelAnchorBars
bool importantLowTrendReady = not na(previousImportantLow) and not na(lastImportantLow) and not na(previousImportantLowBar) and not na(lastImportantLowBar) and lastImportantLowBar - previousImportantLowBar >= minimumChannelAnchorBars
float importantHighSlope = importantHighTrendReady ? (lastImportantHigh - previousImportantHigh) / float(lastImportantHighBar - previousImportantHighBar) : na
float importantLowSlope = importantLowTrendReady ? (lastImportantLow - previousImportantLow) / float(lastImportantLowBar - previousImportantLowBar) : na

// Validate a new rising support line or falling resistance line against every
// confirmed close since its older anchor. The bounded historical loop runs only
// when a same-side pivot changes. Later closes remain the responsibility of the
// existing break/fakeout state machine, so a real break can still be recorded
// and watched for a return inside the same channel. Wicks alone are allowed.
var bool risingAnchorLineUnbroken = false
var bool fallingAnchorLineUnbroken = false

bool newImportantLowAnchor = barstate.isconfirmed and not na(importantPivotLowCandidate)
bool newImportantHighAnchor = barstate.isconfirmed and not na(importantPivotHighCandidate)

if newImportantLowAnchor
    bool risingLowPair = importantLowTrendReady and lastImportantLow > previousImportantLow + syminfo.mintick
    risingAnchorLineUnbroken := risingLowPair
    if risingLowPair
        int risingValidationSpan = bar_index - previousImportantLowBar
        if risingValidationSpan > channelValidationMaxBars
            risingAnchorLineUnbroken := false
        else
            for historyOffset = 0 to risingValidationSpan
                int historicalBarIndex = bar_index - historyOffset
                float risingLineAtHistory = f_project(previousImportantLow, previousImportantLowBar, lastImportantLow, lastImportantLowBar, historicalBarIndex)
                // A trend is a narrow reaction zone, not an infinitely thin
                // price. Allow a small ATR-scaled overshoot while validating
                // the anchors; confirmed breaks are still evaluated below.
                float historicalBreakBuffer = nz(atrValue[historyOffset], safeAtr) * math.max(breakAtrBuffer, trendZoneAtrWidth * 0.50)
                if close[historyOffset] < risingLineAtHistory - historicalBreakBuffer
                    risingAnchorLineUnbroken := false
                    break
if newImportantHighAnchor
    bool fallingHighPair = importantHighTrendReady and lastImportantHigh < previousImportantHigh - syminfo.mintick
    fallingAnchorLineUnbroken := fallingHighPair
    if fallingHighPair
        int fallingValidationSpan = bar_index - previousImportantHighBar
        if fallingValidationSpan > channelValidationMaxBars
            fallingAnchorLineUnbroken := false
        else
            for historyOffset = 0 to fallingValidationSpan
                int historicalBarIndex = bar_index - historyOffset
                float fallingLineAtHistory = f_project(previousImportantHigh, previousImportantHighBar, lastImportantHigh, lastImportantHighBar, historicalBarIndex)
                float historicalBreakBuffer = nz(atrValue[historyOffset], safeAtr) * math.max(breakAtrBuffer, trendZoneAtrWidth * 0.50)
                if close[historyOffset] > fallingLineAtHistory + historicalBreakBuffer
                    fallingAnchorLineUnbroken := false
                    break
bool risingAnchorCandidate = importantLowTrendReady and lastImportantLow > previousImportantLow + syminfo.mintick
bool fallingAnchorCandidate = importantHighTrendReady and lastImportantHigh < previousImportantHigh - syminfo.mintick
bool risingCandidateViolated = risingAnchorCandidate and not risingAnchorLineUnbroken
bool fallingCandidateViolated = fallingAnchorCandidate and not fallingAnchorLineUnbroken

bool risingImportantStructure = importantHighTrendReady and importantLowTrendReady and risingAnchorLineUnbroken and lastImportantHigh > previousImportantHigh + syminfo.mintick and lastImportantLow > previousImportantLow + syminfo.mintick
bool fallingImportantStructure = importantHighTrendReady and importantLowTrendReady and fallingAnchorLineUnbroken and lastImportantHigh < previousImportantHigh - syminfo.mintick and lastImportantLow < previousImportantLow - syminfo.mintick
float risingWidthAtPreviousHigh = risingImportantStructure ? previousImportantHigh - f_project(previousImportantLow, previousImportantLowBar, lastImportantLow, lastImportantLowBar, previousImportantHighBar) : na
float risingWidthAtLastHigh = risingImportantStructure ? lastImportantHigh - f_project(previousImportantLow, previousImportantLowBar, lastImportantLow, lastImportantLowBar, lastImportantHighBar) : na
float fallingWidthAtPreviousLow = fallingImportantStructure ? f_project(previousImportantHigh, previousImportantHighBar, lastImportantHigh, lastImportantHighBar, previousImportantLowBar) - previousImportantLow : na
float fallingWidthAtLastLow = fallingImportantStructure ? f_project(previousImportantHigh, previousImportantHighBar, lastImportantHigh, lastImportantHighBar, lastImportantLowBar) - lastImportantLow : na
float risingMaximumWidth = risingImportantStructure ? math.max(risingWidthAtPreviousHigh, risingWidthAtLastHigh) : na
float risingMinimumWidth = risingImportantStructure ? math.min(risingWidthAtPreviousHigh, risingWidthAtLastHigh) : na
float fallingMaximumWidth = fallingImportantStructure ? math.max(fallingWidthAtPreviousLow, fallingWidthAtLastLow) : na
float fallingMinimumWidth = fallingImportantStructure ? math.min(fallingWidthAtPreviousLow, fallingWidthAtLastLow) : na
float risingWidthConsistency = not na(risingMaximumWidth) and risingMaximumWidth > syminfo.mintick ? risingMinimumWidth / risingMaximumWidth : na
float fallingWidthConsistency = not na(fallingMaximumWidth) and fallingMaximumWidth > syminfo.mintick ? fallingMinimumWidth / fallingMaximumWidth : na
int completeChannelBias = (risingImportantStructure and risingMinimumWidth > syminfo.mintick and risingWidthConsistency >= channelMinimumWidthConsistency) ? 1 : (fallingImportantStructure and fallingMinimumWidth > syminfo.mintick and fallingWidthConsistency >= channelMinimumWidthConsistency) ? -1 : 0
bool risingTrendOnly = risingAnchorCandidate and risingAnchorLineUnbroken and dowBias != -1
bool fallingTrendOnly = fallingAnchorCandidate and fallingAnchorLineUnbroken and dowBias != 1
// Rising lows plus falling highs describe compression/triangle geometry, not a
// directional trend. Do not choose a direction merely from the newest pivot.
bool convergingImportantStructure = risingTrendOnly and fallingTrendOnly
int channelBias = completeChannelBias != 0 ? completeChannelBias : (risingTrendOnly and not fallingTrendOnly ? 1 : fallingTrendOnly and not risingTrendOnly ? -1 : 0)
float rawChannelWidth = completeChannelBias == 1 ? risingMaximumWidth : completeChannelBias == -1 ? fallingMaximumWidth : na
bool validChannelWidth = not na(rawChannelWidth) and rawChannelWidth > syminfo.mintick and rawChannelWidth <= safeAtr * channelMaxAtrProjection * 2.0
float channelWidth = validChannelWidth ? math.max(rawChannelWidth, safeAtr * 0.50) : na

float channelBaseNow = channelBias == 1 ? f_project(previousImportantLow, previousImportantLowBar, lastImportantLow, lastImportantLowBar, bar_index) : channelBias == -1 ? f_project(previousImportantHigh, previousImportantHighBar, lastImportantHigh, lastImportantHighBar, bar_index) : na
// Reject an analytically stale/near-vertical projection when its base has moved
// too far from current price. Previously only the visual line was ATR-limited.
bool channelBaseRelevant = not na(channelBaseNow) and math.abs(close - channelBaseNow) <= safeAtr * channelMaxAtrProjection * 2.0
float selectedChannelSlope = channelBias == 1 ? importantLowSlope : channelBias == -1 ? importantHighSlope : na
bool channelSlopeRelevant = not na(selectedChannelSlope) and math.abs(selectedChannelSlope) <= safeAtr * channelMaximumSlopeAtr
bool trendReady = channelBias != 0 and channelBaseRelevant and channelSlopeRelevant
bool channelReady = completeChannelBias == channelBias and validChannelWidth and trendReady
float channelLowerNow = channelReady ? (channelBias == 1 ? channelBaseNow : channelBaseNow - channelWidth) : na
float channelUpperNow = channelReady ? (channelBias == 1 ? channelBaseNow + channelWidth : channelBaseNow) : na
float channelMiddleNow = channelReady ? (channelLowerNow + channelUpperNow) * 0.50 : na
float channelTolerance = safeAtr * retestAtrTolerance
bool channelBreakoutUp = channelReady and barstate.isconfirmed and close > channelUpperNow + breakBuffer
bool channelBreakoutDown = channelReady and barstate.isconfirmed and close < channelLowerNow - breakBuffer
bool channelGeometryStable = channelReady and channelReady[1] and channelBias == channelBias[1] and not f_int_changed(lastImportantHighBar[1], lastImportantHighBar) and not f_int_changed(lastImportantLowBar[1], lastImportantLowBar)
bool channelBreakoutUpEvent = channelBreakoutUp and channelGeometryStable and close[1] <= channelUpperNow[1] + nz(breakBuffer[1], breakBuffer)
bool channelBreakoutDownEvent = channelBreakoutDown and channelGeometryStable and close[1] >= channelLowerNow[1] - nz(breakBuffer[1], breakBuffer)
bool channelNearLower = channelReady and low <= channelLowerNow + channelTolerance and close >= channelLowerNow - breakBuffer
bool channelNearUpper = channelReady and high >= channelUpperNow - channelTolerance and close <= channelUpperNow + breakBuffer
bool trendSupportBroken = trendReady and channelBias == 1 and barstate.isconfirmed and close < channelBaseNow - breakBuffer
bool trendResistanceBroken = trendReady and channelBias == -1 and barstate.isconfirmed and close > channelBaseNow + breakBuffer

var int additionalTrendReactions = 0
var bool trendReactionArmed = false
var int lastTrendReactionBar = na
bool trendGeometryChanged = trendReady and (not trendReady[1] or channelBias != channelBias[1] or f_int_changed(lastImportantHighBar[1], lastImportantHighBar) or f_int_changed(lastImportantLowBar[1], lastImportantLowBar))
float trendTouchTolerance = safeAtr * math.max(trendZoneAtrWidth, retestAtrTolerance)
[nextTrendReactions, nextTrendReactionArmed, nextTrendReactionBar, trendRejectionEvent] = f_update_trend_reactions(trendReady, channelBias, channelBaseNow, trendTouchTolerance, breakBuffer, trendGeometryChanged, additionalTrendReactions, trendReactionArmed, lastTrendReactionBar, math.max(2, channelPivotBars))
additionalTrendReactions := nextTrendReactions
trendReactionArmed := nextTrendReactionArmed
lastTrendReactionBar := nextTrendReactionBar
int totalTrendTouches = (channelReady ? 4 : trendReady ? 2 : 0) + additionalTrendReactions
bool trendTouchesConfirmed = trendReady and totalTrendTouches >= trendMinimumTouches
float selectedWidthConsistency = completeChannelBias == 1 ? risingWidthConsistency : completeChannelBias == -1 ? fallingWidthConsistency : na
bool channelQualityConfirmed = channelReady and trendTouchesConfirmed and not na(selectedWidthConsistency) and selectedWidthConsistency >= channelMinimumWidthConsistency

// A throw-over is confirmed only after a later closed bar returns inside the
// same unchanged channel. Resolve the previous watcher before a new crossing is
// allowed to arm the next cycle.
var int watchedChannelBreakDirection = 0
var float watchedChannelBreakLevel = na
var int watchedChannelBreakBar = na
var int watchedChannelBias = 0
var int watchedChannelHighAnchorBar = na
var int watchedChannelLowAnchorBar = na

int priorChannelWatchDirection = watchedChannelBreakDirection
float priorChannelWatchLevel = watchedChannelBreakLevel
int priorChannelWatchBar = watchedChannelBreakBar
bool watchedChannelGeometryChanged = priorChannelWatchDirection != 0 and ((watchedChannelHighAnchorBar) != lastImportantHighBar or (watchedChannelLowAnchorBar) != lastImportantLowBar)
bool watchedChannelContextInvalid = priorChannelWatchDirection != 0 and ((watchedChannelBias) != channelBias or watchedChannelGeometryChanged)
int barsFromChannelBreak = na(priorChannelWatchBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - priorChannelWatchBar
bool watchedChannelBreakExpired = priorChannelWatchDirection != 0 and barsFromChannelBreak > channelReturnWindowBars
// A sloped channel moves after the breakout. Confirm the return against the
// current projection of the unchanged channel, not against a frozen old price.
bool channelUpperFakeout = barstate.isconfirmed and channelReady and not watchedChannelContextInvalid and priorChannelWatchDirection == 1 and barsFromChannelBreak > 0 and barsFromChannelBreak <= channelReturnWindowBars and close <= channelUpperNow
bool channelLowerFakeout = barstate.isconfirmed and channelReady and not watchedChannelContextInvalid and priorChannelWatchDirection == -1 and barsFromChannelBreak > 0 and barsFromChannelBreak <= channelReturnWindowBars and close >= channelLowerNow
float channelFakeoutLevel = channelUpperFakeout or channelLowerFakeout ? priorChannelWatchLevel : na

if watchedChannelContextInvalid or watchedChannelBreakExpired or channelUpperFakeout or channelLowerFakeout
    watchedChannelBreakDirection := 0
    watchedChannelBreakLevel := na
    watchedChannelBreakBar := na
    watchedChannelBias := 0
    watchedChannelHighAnchorBar := na
    watchedChannelLowAnchorBar := na

// Staying outside is a state, not a fresh event. Only a new crossing can arm.
if channelBreakoutUpEvent
    watchedChannelBreakDirection := 1
    watchedChannelBreakLevel := channelUpperNow
    watchedChannelBreakBar := bar_index
    watchedChannelBias := channelBias
    watchedChannelHighAnchorBar := lastImportantHighBar
    watchedChannelLowAnchorBar := lastImportantLowBar
else if channelBreakoutDownEvent
    watchedChannelBreakDirection := -1
    watchedChannelBreakLevel := channelLowerNow
    watchedChannelBreakBar := bar_index
    watchedChannelBias := channelBias
    watchedChannelHighAnchorBar := lastImportantHighBar
    watchedChannelLowAnchorBar := lastImportantLowBar
bool qualifiedChannelBreakUpEvent = channelBreakoutUpEvent and channelQualityConfirmed
bool qualifiedChannelBreakDownEvent = channelBreakoutDownEvent and channelQualityConfirmed
f_update_channel_runtime_state() =>
    var float bullishChannelMeasuredTarget = na
    var int bullishChannelMeasuredTargetBar = na
    var float bearishChannelMeasuredTarget = na
    var int bearishChannelMeasuredTargetBar = na
    int channelSignal = 0
    bool channelLayerConflict = false
    bool channelEvidenceReady = false
    int effectiveChannelSignal = 0
    int effectiveChannelBias = 0
    string channelState = "القناة غير جاهزة"
    int channelObjectiveMaximumAge = math.max(retestWindow, channelForwardBars * 3)
    [nextBullishChannelTarget, nextBullishChannelTargetBar, nextBearishChannelTarget, nextBearishChannelTargetBar] = f_update_channel_objectives(bullishChannelMeasuredTarget, bullishChannelMeasuredTargetBar, bearishChannelMeasuredTarget, bearishChannelMeasuredTargetBar, qualifiedChannelBreakUpEvent, qualifiedChannelBreakDownEvent, channelUpperFakeout, channelLowerFakeout, not channelGeometryStable, channelUpperNow, channelLowerNow, channelWidth, channelObjectiveMaximumAge)
    bullishChannelMeasuredTarget := nextBullishChannelTarget
    bullishChannelMeasuredTargetBar := nextBullishChannelTargetBar
    bearishChannelMeasuredTarget := nextBearishChannelTarget
    bearishChannelMeasuredTargetBar := nextBearishChannelTargetBar
    channelSignal := f_channel_signal(channelUpperFakeout, channelLowerFakeout, channelReady, channelBreakoutUp, channelBreakoutDown, channelBias, close, channelMiddleNow, trendReady, trendSupportBroken, trendResistanceBroken, channelBaseNow)
    [nextChannelLayerConflict, nextChannelEvidenceReady, nextEffectiveChannelSignal, nextEffectiveChannelBias] = f_merge_channel_layers(majorTrendReady, majorChannelBias, majorChannelSignal, trendReady, channelBias, channelSignal)
    channelLayerConflict := nextChannelLayerConflict
    channelEvidenceReady := nextChannelEvidenceReady
    effectiveChannelSignal := nextEffectiveChannelSignal
    effectiveChannelBias := nextEffectiveChannelBias
    int channelStateCode = ((channelUpperFakeout) ? 1 : (channelLowerFakeout) ? 2 : (convergingImportantStructure) ? 3 : not (trendReady) and (risingCandidateViolated) and (fallingCandidateViolated) ? 4 : not (trendReady) and (risingCandidateViolated) ? 5 : not (trendReady) and (fallingCandidateViolated) ? 6 : (channelBias) != 0 and not (channelBaseRelevant) ? 7 : not (trendReady) ? 8 : not (channelReady) ? ((channelBias) == 1 ? ((trendSupportBroken) ? 9 : 10) : ((trendResistanceBroken) ? 11 : 12)) : (channelBias) == 1 ? ((channelBreakoutDown) ? 13 : (channelBreakoutUp) ? 14 : (channelNearLower) ? 15 : (channelNearUpper) ? 16 : 17) : ((channelBreakoutUp) ? 18 : (channelBreakoutDown) ? 19 : (channelNearUpper) ? 20 : (channelNearLower) ? 21 : 22))
    string trendTouchState = trendReady ? (channelReady ? "القناة: " : "الترند: ") + str.tostring(totalTrendTouches) + " ارتكازات" + (trendTouchesConfirmed ? " مؤكدة" : " أولية") + (trendRejectionEvent ? " | رفض ذيل/عودة إغلاق" : "") : channelBias != 0 and not channelSlopeRelevant ? "أهمل الإسقاط: الميل أكبر من الحد المتكيف" : ""
    string channelConsistencyState = channelReady and not na(selectedWidthConsistency) ? " | تجانس العرض " + str.tostring(selectedWidthConsistency * 100.0, "#") + "%" : ""
    string channelObjectiveState = not na(bullishChannelMeasuredTarget) ? " | هدف اختراق علوي بعرض القناة " + f_price(bullishChannelMeasuredTarget) : not na(bearishChannelMeasuredTarget) ? " | هدف كسر سفلي بعرض القناة " + f_price(bearishChannelMeasuredTarget) : ""
    channelState := f_channel_state_text(channelStateCode, channelPivotRuleText) + (trendTouchState == "" ? "" : "\n" + trendTouchState + channelConsistencyState + channelObjectiveState)

    [bullishChannelMeasuredTarget, bullishChannelMeasuredTargetBar, bearishChannelMeasuredTarget, bearishChannelMeasuredTargetBar, channelSignal, channelLayerConflict, channelEvidenceReady, effectiveChannelSignal, effectiveChannelBias, channelState]

[bullishChannelMeasuredTarget, bullishChannelMeasuredTargetBar, bearishChannelMeasuredTarget, bearishChannelMeasuredTargetBar, channelSignal, channelLayerConflict, channelEvidenceReady, effectiveChannelSignal, effectiveChannelBias, channelState] = f_update_channel_runtime_state()
// Secondary channel watcher is resolved before re-arming.

// secondary = dotted/lighter. Both are frozen to the last confirmed close while
// the current candle is forming, matching the dashboard.
var line activeMajorTrendLine = na
var line activeMajorTrendZoneLine = na
var linefill activeMajorTrendZoneFill = na
var line activeMajorChannelLine = na
var line activeTrendLine = na
var line activeTrendZoneLine = na
var linefill activeTrendZoneFill = na
var line activeChannelLine = na

if barstate.islast
    bool confirmedMajorTrendReady = barstate.isconfirmed ? majorTrendReady : majorTrendReady[1]
    bool confirmedMajorChannelReady = barstate.isconfirmed ? majorChannelReady : majorChannelReady[1]
    int confirmedMajorBias = barstate.isconfirmed ? majorChannelBias : nz(majorChannelBias[1], 0)
    float confirmedMajorWidth = barstate.isconfirmed ? majorChannelWidth : majorChannelWidth[1]
    bool confirmedVisualTrendReady = barstate.isconfirmed ? trendReady : trendReady[1]
    bool confirmedVisualChannelReady = barstate.isconfirmed ? channelReady : channelReady[1]
    int confirmedVisualChannelBias = barstate.isconfirmed ? channelBias : nz(channelBias[1], 0)
    float confirmedVisualChannelWidth = barstate.isconfirmed ? channelWidth : channelWidth[1]
    float confirmedVisualAtr = barstate.isconfirmed ? safeAtr : nz(safeAtr[1], safeAtr)
    int secondaryFirstAnchorBar = confirmedVisualChannelBias == 1 ? previousImportantLowBar : confirmedVisualChannelBias == -1 ? previousImportantHighBar : na
    float secondaryFirstAnchorPrice = confirmedVisualChannelBias == 1 ? previousImportantLow : confirmedVisualChannelBias == -1 ? previousImportantHigh : na
    int secondarySecondAnchorBar = confirmedVisualChannelBias == 1 ? lastImportantLowBar : confirmedVisualChannelBias == -1 ? lastImportantHighBar : na
    float secondarySecondAnchorPrice = confirmedVisualChannelBias == 1 ? lastImportantLow : confirmedVisualChannelBias == -1 ? lastImportantHigh : na
    [nextMajorTrendLine, nextMajorZoneLine, nextMajorZoneFill, nextMajorParallelLine] = f_update_channel_visual(activeMajorTrendLine, activeMajorTrendZoneLine, activeMajorTrendZoneFill, activeMajorChannelLine, showTrendChannel, confirmedMajorTrendReady, confirmedMajorChannelReady, confirmedMajorBias, majorFirstAnchorBar, majorFirstAnchorPrice, majorSecondAnchorBar, majorSecondAnchorPrice, confirmedMajorWidth, confirmedVisualAtr, channelForwardBars, channelMaxAtrProjection, trendZoneAtrWidth, bullColor, bearColor, trendLineTransparency, trendZoneLineTransparency, trendZoneFillTransparency, channelLineTransparency, true)
    activeMajorTrendLine := nextMajorTrendLine
    activeMajorTrendZoneLine := nextMajorZoneLine
    activeMajorTrendZoneFill := nextMajorZoneFill
    activeMajorChannelLine := nextMajorParallelLine
    [nextMinorTrendLine, nextMinorZoneLine, nextMinorZoneFill, nextMinorParallelLine] = f_update_channel_visual(activeTrendLine, activeTrendZoneLine, activeTrendZoneFill, activeChannelLine, showTrendChannel, confirmedVisualTrendReady, confirmedVisualChannelReady, confirmedVisualChannelBias, secondaryFirstAnchorBar, secondaryFirstAnchorPrice, secondarySecondAnchorBar, secondarySecondAnchorPrice, confirmedVisualChannelWidth, confirmedVisualAtr, channelForwardBars, channelMaxAtrProjection, trendZoneAtrWidth, bullColor, bearColor, trendLineTransparency, trendZoneLineTransparency, trendZoneFillTransparency, channelLineTransparency, false)
    activeTrendLine := nextMinorTrendLine
    activeTrendZoneLine := nextMinorZoneLine
    activeTrendZoneFill := nextMinorZoneFill
    activeChannelLine := nextMinorParallelLine

// الكسر الصحيح يحتاج إغلاقا واضحا خارج المستوى، لا مجرد ذيل. مكان الإغلاق
// وقوة الجسم يقللان كسور الافتتاح الضعيفة، بينما يبقى الحجم/VWAP/FVG حسب الإعدادات.
bool bullishBreakCandleQuality = closeLocation >= 0.55 and (bodyRatio >= 0.30 or vsaWideResult)
bool bearishBreakCandleQuality = closeLocation <= 0.45 and (bodyRatio >= 0.30 or vsaWideResult)
// A swing high and swing low confirmed from the same outside candle do not
// reveal which extreme occurred first. Treat that pair as temporally ambiguous
// until a later distinct pivot resolves the chronology.
bool swingChronologyCompatible = na(lastSwingHighBar) or na(lastSwingLowBar) or lastSwingHighBar != lastSwingLowBar
bool swingLevelsCompatible = (na(lastSwingHigh) or na(lastSwingLow) or lastSwingHigh > lastSwingLow + syminfo.mintick) and swingChronologyCompatible

// Market structure is defined only by a confirmed close beyond the swing.
// Volume, VWAP and FVG are optional ENTRY qualifiers and must never rewrite
// BOS/CHoCH, zone creation, break watchers or the underlying Dow state.
bool bullishBreak = barstate.isconfirmed and swingLevelsCompatible and not na(lastSwingHigh) and not highWasBroken and close > lastSwingHigh + breakBuffer
bool bearishBreak = barstate.isconfirmed and swingLevelsCompatible and not na(lastSwingLow) and not lowWasBroken and close < lastSwingLow - breakBuffer

// جودة الرفض موحدة لسحب السيولة، وهمي القناة، وفشل الكسر.
f_rejection_quality(bool bullishSide, float wickShare, bool bodyAlternative, bool participationOkay) =>
    (bullishSide ? closeLocation >= 0.55 : closeLocation <= 0.45) and (bodyAlternative ? bodyRatio >= 0.25 or wickShare >= 0.35 : wickShare >= 0.30) and (participationOkay or wickShare >= 0.55)

f_liquidity_sweep_state() =>
    float lowerWickShare = math.max(math.min(open, close) - low, 0.0) / candleRange
    float upperWickShare = math.max(high - math.max(open, close), 0.0) / candleRange
    float reclaimBuffer = math.max(syminfo.mintick * 2.0, safeAtr * 0.04)
    float maximumPenetration = safeAtr
    float lowerPenetration = not na(lastSwingLow) ? lastSwingLow - low : na
    float upperPenetration = not na(lastSwingHigh) ? high - lastSwingHigh : na
    bool participationOkay = not volumeAvailable or heatmapVolumeMediumOrHigher or statisticalVolumeHigh or relativeVolume >= 0.85
    bool bullishRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and swingLevelsCompatible and not na(lastSwingLow) and not lowWasBroken and not lowWasSwept and lowerPenetration >= breakBuffer and lowerPenetration <= maximumPenetration and close >= lastSwingLow + reclaimBuffer
    bool bearishRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and swingLevelsCompatible and not na(lastSwingHigh) and not highWasBroken and not highWasSwept and upperPenetration >= breakBuffer and upperPenetration <= maximumPenetration and close <= lastSwingHigh - reclaimBuffer
    bool bullishQualified = bullishRaw and not bearishRaw and f_rejection_quality(true, lowerWickShare, false, participationOkay)
    bool bearishQualified = bearishRaw and not bullishRaw and f_rejection_quality(false, upperWickShare, false, participationOkay)
    [lowerWickShare, upperWickShare, participationOkay, (bullishRaw and bearishRaw), bullishQualified, bearishQualified]

[sweepLowerWickShare, sweepUpperWickShare, sweepVolumeAcceptance, twoSidedLiquiditySweep, bullishSweep, bearishSweep] = f_liquidity_sweep_state()

// التنفيذ المباشر أشد من مجرد اكتشاف حدث الكسر: جسم واضح، إغلاق قريب
// من طرف الشمعة، وذيل معاكس محدود. الكسر الأضعف يبقى مرشح اختبار.
bool bullishDirectBreakQuality = bullishBreakCandleQuality and sweepUpperWickShare <= 0.25 and bodyRatio >= 0.40 and closeLocation >= 0.65
bool bearishDirectBreakQuality = bearishBreakCandleQuality and sweepLowerWickShare <= 0.25 and bodyRatio >= 0.40 and closeLocation <= 0.35

var int structureBias = 0
var int lastStructureBreakBar = na
if structureBias == 0 and dowBias != 0
    structureBias := dowBias

int structureBreakGraceBars = math.max(2, pivotBars * 2 + 1)
bool recentStructureBreakBeforeCurrent = not na(lastStructureBreakBar) and bar_index - lastStructureBreakBar <= structureBreakGraceBars
int structureBiasBeforeBreak = not swingLevelsCompatible ? 0 : (not recentStructureBreakBeforeCurrent and structureBias != 0 and dowBias != 0 and structureBias != dowBias) ? dowBias : structureBias != 0 ? structureBias : dowBias
bool bullishChoch = bullishBreak and structureBiasBeforeBreak == -1
bool bearishChoch = bearishBreak and structureBiasBeforeBreak == 1
bool bullishBos = bullishBreak and not bullishChoch
bool bearishBos = bearishBreak and not bearishChoch

if bullishBreak
    highWasBroken := true
    structureBias := 1
    lastStructureBreakBar := bar_index
else if bearishBreak
    lowWasBroken := true
    structureBias := -1
    lastStructureBreakBar := bar_index

// A close-confirmed break bias and the latest Dow pivot sequence can disagree
// temporarily. Treat that state as a conflict rather than awarding both sides
// a structural point or presenting one of them as the sole market direction.
bool recentStructureBreak = not na(lastStructureBreakBar) and bar_index - lastStructureBreakBar <= structureBreakGraceBars
bool structureDirectionConflict = not recentStructureBreak and structureBias != 0 and dowBias != 0 and structureBias != dowBias
int resolvedStructureBias = not swingLevelsCompatible or structureDirectionConflict ? 0 : structureBias != 0 ? structureBias : dowBias

bool doubleBottomConfirmed = useChartPatterns and bullishBreak and doubleBottomArmed and not na(doubleBottomNeckline) and math.abs(lastSwingHigh - doubleBottomNeckline) <= patternTolerance
bool doubleTopConfirmed = useChartPatterns and bearishBreak and doubleTopArmed and not na(doubleTopNeckline) and math.abs(lastSwingLow - doubleTopNeckline) <= patternTolerance
// موضع اسم النموذج يثبت على المحور الثاني، بينما يبقى تأكيد الصفقة عند كسر خط العنق.
int doubleBottomPatternBar = doubleBottomConfirmed ? doubleBottomArmedBar : na
float doubleBottomPatternLevel = doubleBottomConfirmed ? doubleBottomPrice : na
int doubleTopPatternBar = doubleTopConfirmed ? doubleTopArmedBar : na
float doubleTopPatternLevel = doubleTopConfirmed ? doubleTopPrice : na
// الرأس والكتفين: ثلاثة محاور مرتبة، كتفان متقاربان، ثم كسر خط العنق بإغلاق.
f_head_shoulders_state(array<float> shoulderPrices, array<int> shoulderBars, array<float> neckPrices, array<int> neckBars, bool inverse) =>
    bool dataReady = useChartPatterns and array.size(shoulderPrices) >= 3 and array.size(shoulderBars) >= 3 and array.size(neckPrices) >= 2 and array.size(neckBars) >= 2
    float rightShoulder = dataReady ? array.get(shoulderPrices, 0) : na
    float head = dataReady ? array.get(shoulderPrices, 1) : na
    float leftShoulder = dataReady ? array.get(shoulderPrices, 2) : na
    int rightShoulderBar = dataReady ? array.get(shoulderBars, 0) : na
    int headBar = dataReady ? array.get(shoulderBars, 1) : na
    int leftShoulderBar = dataReady ? array.get(shoulderBars, 2) : na
    float rightNeck = dataReady ? array.get(neckPrices, 0) : na
    float leftNeck = dataReady ? array.get(neckPrices, 1) : na
    int rightNeckBar = dataReady ? array.get(neckBars, 0) : na
    int leftNeckBar = dataReady ? array.get(neckBars, 1) : na
    bool chronology = dataReady and leftShoulderBar < leftNeckBar and leftNeckBar < headBar and headBar < rightNeckBar and rightNeckBar < rightShoulderBar
    float neckAtHead = chronology ? f_project(leftNeck, leftNeckBar, rightNeck, rightNeckBar, headBar) : na
    float height = chronology ? (inverse ? neckAtHead - head : head - neckAtHead) : na
    bool shouldersOk = chronology and math.abs(leftShoulder - rightShoulder) <= math.max(patternTolerance, safeAtr * 0.50)
    bool shouldersOnCorrectSide = chronology and (inverse ? math.max(leftShoulder, rightShoulder) < math.min(leftNeck, rightNeck) : math.min(leftShoulder, rightShoulder) > math.max(leftNeck, rightNeck))
    bool headOk = chronology and (inverse ? head < math.min(leftShoulder, rightShoulder) - safeAtr * 0.50 : head > math.max(leftShoulder, rightShoulder) + safeAtr * 0.50)
    int leftDuration = chronology ? headBar - leftShoulderBar : 0
    int rightDuration = chronology ? rightShoulderBar - headBar : 0
    float durationRatio = chronology and math.min(leftDuration, rightDuration) > 0 ? math.max(leftDuration, rightDuration) / math.min(leftDuration, rightDuration) : 100.0
    bool ready = shouldersOk and shouldersOnCorrectSide and headOk and (chronology and durationRatio <= 3.0) and height > syminfo.mintick and bar_index - rightShoulderBar <= patternMaxBars
    float necklineNow = ready ? f_project(leftNeck, leftNeckBar, rightNeck, rightNeckBar, bar_index) : na
    float necklinePrevious = ready ? f_project(leftNeck, leftNeckBar, rightNeck, rightNeckBar, bar_index - 1) : na
    [ready, necklineNow, necklinePrevious, ready ? height : na, rightShoulder]

[headShouldersReady, hsNecklineNow, hsNecklinePrevious, headShouldersHeight, hsRightShoulder] = f_head_shoulders_state(srHighPivotPrices, srHighPivotBars, srLowPivotPrices, srLowPivotBars, false)
[inverseHeadShouldersReady, ihsNecklineNow, ihsNecklinePrevious, inverseHeadShouldersHeight, ihsRightShoulder] = f_head_shoulders_state(srLowPivotPrices, srLowPivotBars, srHighPivotPrices, srHighPivotBars, true)
bool headShouldersConfirmed = barstate.isconfirmed and headShouldersReady and close < hsNecklineNow - breakBuffer and close[1] >= hsNecklinePrevious - nz(breakBuffer[1], breakBuffer)
bool inverseHeadShouldersConfirmed = barstate.isconfirmed and inverseHeadShouldersReady and close > ihsNecklineNow + breakBuffer and close[1] <= ihsNecklinePrevious + nz(breakBuffer[1], breakBuffer)
float bullishMeasuredTarget = doubleBottomConfirmed ? doubleBottomNeckline + doubleBottomHeight : inverseHeadShouldersConfirmed ? ihsNecklineNow + inverseHeadShouldersHeight : na
float bearishMeasuredTarget = doubleTopConfirmed ? doubleTopNeckline - doubleTopHeight : headShouldersConfirmed ? hsNecklineNow - headShouldersHeight : na

if doubleBottomConfirmed or (doubleBottomArmed and not na(doubleBottomPrice) and close < doubleBottomPrice - breakBuffer)
    doubleBottomArmed := false
    doubleBottomArmedBar := na
    doubleBottomNeckline := na
    doubleBottomPrice := na
    doubleBottomHeight := na
if doubleTopConfirmed or (doubleTopArmed and not na(doubleTopPrice) and close > doubleTopPrice + breakBuffer)
    doubleTopArmed := false
    doubleTopArmedBar := na
    doubleTopNeckline := na
    doubleTopPrice := na
    doubleTopHeight := na

// A zone now requires both a close-confirmed structure break and a displacement
// candle. Its base follows proximal/distal rules from the supplied references.
// Only one subtle box per side is retained, so the chart stays readable.
bool bullishZoneDisplacement = bullishBreak and bodyRatio >= zoneImpulseBodyRatio and closeLocation >= 0.65
bool bearishZoneDisplacement = bearishBreak and bodyRatio >= zoneImpulseBodyRatio and closeLocation <= 0.35
[demandOriginReady, demandFarthestOffset, demandProximalCandidate, demandDistalCandidate, demandBaseCandidateCount, demandPatternCandidate] = f_zone_origin(true, zoneLookback, zoneBaseBodyRatio)
[supplyOriginReady, supplyFarthestOffset, supplyProximalCandidate, supplyDistalCandidate, supplyBaseCandidateCount, supplyPatternCandidate] = f_zone_origin(false, zoneLookback, zoneBaseBodyRatio)
var bool demandZoneActive = false
var bool supplyZoneActive = false
var bool demandZoneRetested = false
var bool supplyZoneRetested = false
var float demandZoneHigh = na
var float demandZoneLow = na
var float supplyZoneHigh = na
var float supplyZoneLow = na
var int demandZoneCreatedBar = na
var int supplyZoneCreatedBar = na
var int demandZoneBaseCount = 0
var int supplyZoneBaseCount = 0
var int demandZonePatternCode = 0
var int supplyZonePatternCode = 0
var box demandZoneBox = na
var box supplyZoneBox = na
var int demandZoneQuality = 0
var int supplyZoneQuality = 0
var bool bullishBreakerActive = false
var bool bearishBreakerActive = false
var bool bullishBreakerRetested = false
var bool bearishBreakerRetested = false
var float bullishBreakerHigh = na
var float bullishBreakerLow = na
var float bearishBreakerHigh = na
var float bearishBreakerLow = na
var int bullishBreakerCreatedBar = na
var int bearishBreakerCreatedBar = na

bool validDemandCandidate = demandOriginReady and not na(demandProximalCandidate) and not na(demandDistalCandidate) and demandProximalCandidate > demandDistalCandidate
bool validSupplyCandidate = supplyOriginReady and not na(supplyProximalCandidate) and not na(supplyDistalCandidate) and supplyDistalCandidate > supplyProximalCandidate

if bullishZoneDisplacement and validDemandCandidate
    demandZoneActive := true
    demandZoneRetested := false
    demandZoneHigh := demandProximalCandidate
    demandZoneLow := demandDistalCandidate
    demandZoneCreatedBar := bar_index
    demandZoneBaseCount := demandBaseCandidateCount
    demandZonePatternCode := demandPatternCandidate
    demandZoneQuality := (demandBaseCandidateCount <= 3 ? 1 : 0) + ((demandProximalCandidate - demandDistalCandidate) <= safeAtr * 1.50 ? 1 : 0) + (volumeAvailable and (relativeVolume >= 1.0 or heatmapVolumeMediumOrHigher) ? 1 : 0) + (bodyRatio >= 0.70 ? 1 : 0)
    demandZoneBox := f_clear_box(demandZoneBox)
    demandZoneBox := stockChartAllowed and showSupplyDemandZones ? box.new(left = bar_index - demandFarthestOffset, top = demandZoneHigh, right = bar_index + visualPlanBoxOffsetBars, bottom = demandZoneLow, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, border_color = color.new(bullColor, zoneBorderTransparency), border_width = 1, bgcolor = color.new(bullColor, zoneFillTransparency), text = "منطقة طلب", text_color = color.new(bullColor, 8), text_size = size.tiny, text_halign = text.align_right, text_valign = text.align_center, force_overlay = true) : na

if bearishZoneDisplacement and validSupplyCandidate
    supplyZoneActive := true
    supplyZoneRetested := false
    supplyZoneHigh := supplyDistalCandidate
    supplyZoneLow := supplyProximalCandidate
    supplyZoneCreatedBar := bar_index
    supplyZoneBaseCount := supplyBaseCandidateCount
    supplyZonePatternCode := supplyPatternCandidate
    supplyZoneQuality := (supplyBaseCandidateCount <= 3 ? 1 : 0) + ((supplyDistalCandidate - supplyProximalCandidate) <= safeAtr * 1.50 ? 1 : 0) + (volumeAvailable and (relativeVolume >= 1.0 or heatmapVolumeMediumOrHigher) ? 1 : 0) + (bodyRatio >= 0.70 ? 1 : 0)
    supplyZoneBox := f_clear_box(supplyZoneBox)
    supplyZoneBox := stockChartAllowed and showSupplyDemandZones ? box.new(left = bar_index - supplyFarthestOffset, top = supplyZoneHigh, right = bar_index + visualPlanBoxOffsetBars, bottom = supplyZoneLow, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, border_color = color.new(bearColor, zoneBorderTransparency), border_width = 1, bgcolor = color.new(bearColor, zoneFillTransparency), text = "منطقة عرض", text_color = color.new(bearColor, 8), text_size = size.tiny, text_halign = text.align_right, text_valign = text.align_center, force_overlay = true) : na

bool demandZoneInvalidated = barstate.isconfirmed and demandZoneActive and close < demandZoneLow - breakBuffer
bool supplyZoneInvalidated = barstate.isconfirmed and supplyZoneActive and close > supplyZoneHigh + breakBuffer
bool demandZoneExpired = barstate.isconfirmed and demandZoneActive and not na(demandZoneCreatedBar) and bar_index - demandZoneCreatedBar > zoneMaxBars
bool supplyZoneExpired = barstate.isconfirmed and supplyZoneActive and not na(supplyZoneCreatedBar) and bar_index - supplyZoneCreatedBar > zoneMaxBars
bool bullishDemandRetestRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and demandZoneActive and not demandZoneRetested and not demandZoneInvalidated and not demandZoneExpired and bar_index > demandZoneCreatedBar and low <= demandZoneHigh and high >= demandZoneLow and close > demandZoneHigh and close > open
bool bearishSupplyRetestRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and supplyZoneActive and not supplyZoneRetested and not supplyZoneInvalidated and not supplyZoneExpired and bar_index > supplyZoneCreatedBar and high >= supplyZoneLow and low <= supplyZoneHigh and close < supplyZoneLow and close < open
bool bullishDemandRetest = bullishDemandRetestRaw and demandZoneQuality >= 2
bool bearishSupplyRetest = bearishSupplyRetestRaw and supplyZoneQuality >= 2

if bullishDemandRetestRaw
    demandZoneRetested := true
if bearishSupplyRetestRaw
    supplyZoneRetested := true

// المنطقة الجيدة التي تُكسر بإغلاق تتحول إلى بريكر واحد خفيف بدل إنشاء محرك مناطق مكرر.
if supplyZoneInvalidated and supplyZoneQuality >= 2
    bullishBreakerActive := true
    bullishBreakerRetested := false
    bullishBreakerHigh := supplyZoneHigh
    bullishBreakerLow := supplyZoneLow
    bullishBreakerCreatedBar := bar_index
if demandZoneInvalidated and demandZoneQuality >= 2
    bearishBreakerActive := true
    bearishBreakerRetested := false
    bearishBreakerHigh := demandZoneHigh
    bearishBreakerLow := demandZoneLow
    bearishBreakerCreatedBar := bar_index

if demandZoneInvalidated or demandZoneExpired
    demandZoneActive := false
    demandZoneRetested := false
    demandZoneBox := f_clear_box(demandZoneBox)
    demandZoneHigh := na
    demandZoneLow := na
    demandZoneCreatedBar := na
    demandZoneBaseCount := 0
    demandZonePatternCode := 0
    demandZoneQuality := 0
if supplyZoneInvalidated or supplyZoneExpired
    supplyZoneActive := false
    supplyZoneRetested := false
    supplyZoneBox := f_clear_box(supplyZoneBox)
    supplyZoneHigh := na
    supplyZoneLow := na
    supplyZoneCreatedBar := na
    supplyZoneBaseCount := 0
    supplyZonePatternCode := 0
    supplyZoneQuality := 0

bool bullishBreakerExpired = bullishBreakerActive and not na(bullishBreakerCreatedBar) and bar_index - bullishBreakerCreatedBar > zoneMaxBars
bool bearishBreakerExpired = bearishBreakerActive and not na(bearishBreakerCreatedBar) and bar_index - bearishBreakerCreatedBar > zoneMaxBars
bool bullishBreakerFailed = barstate.isconfirmed and bullishBreakerActive and close < bullishBreakerLow - breakBuffer
bool bearishBreakerFailed = barstate.isconfirmed and bearishBreakerActive and close > bearishBreakerHigh + breakBuffer
bool bullishBreakerRetest = barstate.isconfirmed and bullishBreakerActive and not bullishBreakerRetested and not bullishBreakerExpired and not bullishBreakerFailed and bar_index > bullishBreakerCreatedBar and low <= bullishBreakerHigh and high >= bullishBreakerLow and close > bullishBreakerHigh and close > open
bool bearishBreakerRetest = barstate.isconfirmed and bearishBreakerActive and not bearishBreakerRetested and not bearishBreakerExpired and not bearishBreakerFailed and bar_index > bearishBreakerCreatedBar and high >= bearishBreakerLow and low <= bearishBreakerHigh and close < bearishBreakerLow and close < open
if bullishBreakerRetest
    bullishBreakerRetested := true
if bearishBreakerRetest
    bearishBreakerRetested := true
if bullishBreakerExpired or bullishBreakerFailed
    bullishBreakerActive := false
    bullishBreakerRetested := false
    bullishBreakerHigh := na
    bullishBreakerLow := na
    bullishBreakerCreatedBar := na
if bearishBreakerExpired or bearishBreakerFailed
    bearishBreakerActive := false
    bearishBreakerRetested := false
    bearishBreakerHigh := na
    bearishBreakerLow := na
    bearishBreakerCreatedBar := na

f_refresh_supply_demand_captions(barstate.islast, demandZoneBox, supplyZoneBox, bar_index + visualPlanBoxOffsetBars, bullColor, bearColor)

// An old valid zone remains a drawing/stop/target reference, but it must not
// contribute a permanent location vote when price has travelled far away.
float zoneEvidenceMaximumDistance = safeAtr * zoneEvidenceMaxDistanceAtr
bool demandZoneEvidenceRelevant = demandZoneActive and not na(demandZoneHigh) and close >= demandZoneLow - breakBuffer and (bullishDemandRetest or math.max(close - demandZoneHigh, 0.0) <= zoneEvidenceMaximumDistance)
bool supplyZoneEvidenceRelevant = supplyZoneActive and not na(supplyZoneLow) and close <= supplyZoneHigh + breakBuffer and (bearishSupplyRetest or math.max(supplyZoneLow - close, 0.0) <= zoneEvidenceMaximumDistance)
bool bullishBreakerEvidenceRelevant = bullishBreakerActive and close >= bullishBreakerLow - breakBuffer and math.max(close - bullishBreakerHigh, 0.0) <= zoneEvidenceMaximumDistance
bool bearishBreakerEvidenceRelevant = bearishBreakerActive and close <= bearishBreakerHigh + breakBuffer and math.max(bearishBreakerLow - close, 0.0) <= zoneEvidenceMaximumDistance
bool conflictingZoneLocation = (demandZoneEvidenceRelevant or bullishBreakerEvidenceRelevant) and (supplyZoneEvidenceRelevant or bearishBreakerEvidenceRelevant) and not bullishDemandRetest and not bearishSupplyRetest and not bullishBreakerRetest and not bearishBreakerRetest
int zoneSignal = bullishBreakerRetest or bullishDemandRetest ? 1 : bearishBreakerRetest or bearishSupplyRetest ? -1 : conflictingZoneLocation ? 0 : (demandZoneEvidenceRelevant or bullishBreakerEvidenceRelevant) and resolvedStructureBias == 1 ? 1 : (supplyZoneEvidenceRelevant or bearishBreakerEvidenceRelevant) and resolvedStructureBias == -1 ? -1 : 0
float effectiveChannelFakeoutLevel = channelUpperFakeout or channelLowerFakeout ? channelFakeoutLevel : majorChannelUpperFakeout ? majorChannelUpperNow : majorChannelLowerFakeout ? majorChannelLowerNow : na
bool qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout = (majorChannelUpperFakeout or channelUpperFakeout) and f_rejection_quality(false, sweepUpperWickShare, true, sweepVolumeAcceptance)
bool qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout = (majorChannelLowerFakeout or channelLowerFakeout) and f_rejection_quality(true, sweepLowerWickShare, true, sweepVolumeAcceptance)

f_zone_opinion_summary() =>
    int stateCode = bullishDemandRetest ? 1 : bearishSupplyRetest ? 2 : demandZoneInvalidated ? 3 : supplyZoneInvalidated ? 4 : demandZoneExpired ? 5 : supplyZoneExpired ? 6 : demandZoneActive and supplyZoneActive ? 7 : demandZoneActive ? 8 : supplyZoneActive ? 9 : 0
    string demandOrigin = demandZoneActive ? f_zone_pattern_text(demandZonePatternCode, demandZoneBaseCount) : ""
    string supplyOrigin = supplyZoneActive ? f_zone_pattern_text(supplyZonePatternCode, supplyZoneBaseCount) : ""
    string activeOrigin = demandZoneActive and supplyZoneActive ? demandOrigin + " | " + supplyOrigin : demandZoneActive ? demandOrigin : supplyZoneActive ? supplyOrigin : ""
    string breakerText = bullishBreakerActive and bearishBreakerActive ? "بريكر صاعد وهابط تحت المراقبة" : bullishBreakerActive ? "بريكر صاعد تحت المراقبة" : bearishBreakerActive ? "بريكر هابط تحت المراقبة" : ""
    string stateText = f_text_code(TXT_ZONE_STATE, stateCode) + (activeOrigin == "" ? "" : "\n" + activeOrigin + " | جودة " + str.tostring(math.max(demandZoneQuality, supplyZoneQuality)) + "/4") + (breakerText == "" ? "" : "\n" + breakerText)
    int opinionCode = structureDirectionConflict ? 1 : channelLayerConflict ? 12 : convergingImportantStructure ? 2 : qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout ? 3 : qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout ? 4 : not (majorChannelReady or channelReady) ? 5 : effectiveChannelBias == 1 and (majorChannelBreakoutDown or channelBreakoutDown) ? 6 : effectiveChannelBias == -1 and (majorChannelBreakoutUp or channelBreakoutUp) ? 7 : resolvedStructureBias == 1 and effectiveChannelSignal == 1 ? 8 : resolvedStructureBias == -1 and effectiveChannelSignal == -1 ? 9 : effectiveChannelBias == 1 ? 10 : 11
    string opinionText = f_text_code(TXT_TECHNICAL_OPINION, opinionCode)
    [stateCode, stateText, opinionCode, opinionText]

[zoneStateCode, zoneState, technicalOpinionCode, technicalOpinion] = f_zone_opinion_summary()

if bullishSweep
    lowWasSwept := true
if bearishSweep
    highWasSwept := true

// Watch a confirmed break for either a successful retest or a multi-bar failure.
var int watchedBreakDirection = 0
var float watchedBreakLevel = na
var int watchedBreakBar = na

// Resolve the previously armed break before arming a new one. The old code
// overwrote the watched level first, which could hide a same-bar failure when
// an opposite structural break appeared.
int priorWatchedBreakDirection = watchedBreakDirection
float priorWatchedBreakLevel = watchedBreakLevel
int priorWatchedBreakBar = watchedBreakBar
int barsFromBreak = na(priorWatchedBreakBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - priorWatchedBreakBar
bool watchAfterBreak = priorWatchedBreakDirection != 0 and barsFromBreak > 0
bool insideFakeoutWindow = watchAfterBreak and barsFromBreak <= fakeoutWindow
bool insideRetestWindow = watchAfterBreak and barsFromBreak <= retestWindow
float retestTolerance = safeAtr * retestAtrTolerance
float failureConfirmationBuffer = math.max(breakBuffer * 0.50, safeAtr * 0.04)
bool bullishBreakFailureRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and insideFakeoutWindow and priorWatchedBreakDirection == 1 and close < priorWatchedBreakLevel - failureConfirmationBuffer and close[1] >= priorWatchedBreakLevel - failureConfirmationBuffer
bool bearishBreakFailureRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and insideFakeoutWindow and priorWatchedBreakDirection == -1 and close > priorWatchedBreakLevel + failureConfirmationBuffer and close[1] <= priorWatchedBreakLevel + failureConfirmationBuffer
bool bullishBreakFailed = bullishBreakFailureRaw and f_rejection_quality(false, sweepUpperWickShare, true, sweepVolumeAcceptance)
bool bearishBreakFailed = bearishBreakFailureRaw and f_rejection_quality(true, sweepLowerWickShare, true, sweepVolumeAcceptance)
bool bullishRetest = barstate.isconfirmed and insideRetestWindow and priorWatchedBreakDirection == 1 and not bullishBreakFailed and low >= priorWatchedBreakLevel - retestTolerance and low <= priorWatchedBreakLevel + retestTolerance and close > priorWatchedBreakLevel and closeLocation >= 0.55
bool bearishRetest = barstate.isconfirmed and insideRetestWindow and priorWatchedBreakDirection == -1 and not bearishBreakFailed and high <= priorWatchedBreakLevel + retestTolerance and high >= priorWatchedBreakLevel - retestTolerance and close < priorWatchedBreakLevel and closeLocation <= 0.45
float resolvedBreakEventLevel = bullishBreakFailed or bearishBreakFailed or bullishRetest or bearishRetest ? priorWatchedBreakLevel : na
bool breakWatcherExpired = priorWatchedBreakDirection != 0 and barsFromBreak > math.max(fakeoutWindow, retestWindow)

if (bullishBreakFailed or bearishBreakFailed or bullishRetest or bearishRetest or breakWatcherExpired)
    watchedBreakDirection := 0
    watchedBreakLevel := na
    watchedBreakBar := na

// A current raw BOS/CHoCH now arms the next monitoring cycle after the old
// cycle's events have been preserved in resolvedBreakEventLevel.
if bullishBreak
    watchedBreakDirection := 1
    watchedBreakLevel := lastSwingHigh
    watchedBreakBar := bar_index
else if bearishBreak
    watchedBreakDirection := -1
    watchedBreakLevel := lastSwingLow
    watchedBreakBar := bar_index

// Wyckoff spring/upthrust tests require a later revisit with a smaller result
// and without renewed high effort. The raw sweep alone remains a warning.
var int watchedSweepDirection = 0
var float watchedSweepLevel = na
var float watchedSweepExtreme = na
var int watchedSweepBar = na
if twoSidedLiquiditySweep
    watchedSweepDirection := 0
    watchedSweepLevel := na
    watchedSweepExtreme := na
    watchedSweepBar := na
else if bullishSweep
    watchedSweepDirection := 1
    watchedSweepLevel := lastSwingLow
    watchedSweepExtreme := low
    watchedSweepBar := bar_index
else if bearishSweep
    watchedSweepDirection := -1
    watchedSweepLevel := lastSwingHigh
    watchedSweepExtreme := high
    watchedSweepBar := bar_index

int barsFromSweep = na(watchedSweepBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - watchedSweepBar
bool insideSweepTestWindow = watchedSweepDirection != 0 and barsFromSweep > 0 and barsFromSweep <= retestWindow
bool quieterTestVolume = volumeAvailable and relativeVolume <= 1.0
bool quieterTestSpread = not na(relativeSpread) and relativeSpread <= 1.0
bool bullishWyckoffTest = barstate.isconfirmed and insideSweepTestWindow and watchedSweepDirection == 1 and low <= watchedSweepLevel + retestTolerance and low > watchedSweepExtreme and close > watchedSweepLevel and close > open and quieterTestVolume and quieterTestSpread
bool bearishWyckoffTest = barstate.isconfirmed and insideSweepTestWindow and watchedSweepDirection == -1 and high >= watchedSweepLevel - retestTolerance and high < watchedSweepExtreme and close < watchedSweepLevel and close < open and quieterTestVolume and quieterTestSpread
float wyckoffTestLevel = bullishWyckoffTest or bearishWyckoffTest ? watchedSweepLevel : na
float wyckoffTestExtreme = bullishWyckoffTest or bearishWyckoffTest ? watchedSweepExtreme : na
if bullishWyckoffTest or bearishWyckoffTest or barsFromSweep > retestWindow
    watchedSweepDirection := 0
    watchedSweepLevel := na
    watchedSweepExtreme := na
    watchedSweepBar := na

// =============================================================================
// Wyckoff accumulation/distribution sequence
// =============================================================================
// by one entity. Phase A needs a prior trend plus effort; AR/ST build the range;
// Phase C is optional; Phase D needs a confirmed SOS/SOW and its retest.
var int wyckoffMode = 0              // 1 accumulation, -1 distribution, 0 none
var int wyckoffStage = 0             // event progression inside the active mode
var int wyckoffPhase = 0             // 1=A, 2=B, 3=C, 4=D, 5=E
var int wyckoffSequenceStartBar = na
var int wyckoffClimaxBar = na
var int wyckoffAutomaticBar = na
var int wyckoffSignBar = na
var int wyckoffPhaseEBar = na
var float wyckoffRangeSupport = na
var float wyckoffRangeResistance = na
var float wyckoffClimaxRvol = na
var float wyckoffClimaxSpread = na
var bool wyckoffResistanceWasBroken = false
var bool wyckoffSupportWasBroken = false
// Phase-C tests must remain tied to the exact Spring/UTAD that created them.
var int wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection = 0
var float wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel = na
var float wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme = na
var int wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar = na
var array<label> wyckoffModelLabels = array.new<label>()
var array<label> chartEventLabels = array.new<label>()
var label activeStageLabel = na
var line wyckoffSupportLine = na
var line wyckoffResistanceLine = na

// Original accumulation must stop an extended downtrend; original distribution
// must stop an extended uptrend. Re-accumulation/re-distribution are intentionally
// not labelled by this state machine because their prior-trend rules are different.
int wyckoffPriorTrendBars = math.max(wyckoffTrendLookback, (chartSeconds <= 5 * 60 ? 60 : chartSeconds <= 30 * 60 ? 40 : chartSeconds < 24 * 60 * 60 ? 30 : 40))
int wyckoffTrendSlopeBars = math.max(3, int(math.round(float(wyckoffPriorTrendBars) * 0.25)))
float wyckoffMinimumTrendMoveAtr = chartSeconds < 24 * 60 * 60 ? 3.0 : 4.0
float priorWyckoffLow = ta.lowest(low[1], wyckoffPriorTrendBars)
float priorWyckoffHigh = ta.highest(high[1], wyckoffPriorTrendBars)
float wyckoffTrendStartClose = close[wyckoffPriorTrendBars + 1]
float wyckoffTrendReferenceAtr = nz(ta.sma(safeAtr[1], wyckoffPriorTrendBars), safeAtr)
float wyckoffLowerWick = math.min(open, close) - low
float wyckoffUpperWick = high - math.max(open, close)
bool wyckoffTrendHistoryReady = not na(wyckoffTrendStartClose) and not na(ema20[1]) and not na(ema50[1]) and not na(ema20[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1]) and not na(ema50[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1]) and not na(priorWyckoffLow) and not na(priorWyckoffHigh)
// Qualify the trend from bars preceding the stopping event. The climax candle
// may itself reverse sharply, so requiring its close to remain below/above the
// moving averages would contradict the purpose of SC/BC detection.
bool extendedDownwardMove = wyckoffTrendHistoryReady and wyckoffTrendStartClose - low >= wyckoffTrendReferenceAtr * wyckoffMinimumTrendMoveAtr
bool extendedUpwardMove = wyckoffTrendHistoryReady and high - wyckoffTrendStartClose >= wyckoffTrendReferenceAtr * wyckoffMinimumTrendMoveAtr
bool persistentDowntrend = wyckoffTrendHistoryReady and ema20[1] < ema50[1] and close[1] < ema20[1] and ema20[1] < ema20[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1] and ema50[1] <= ema50[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1]
bool persistentUptrend = wyckoffTrendHistoryReady and ema20[1] > ema50[1] and close[1] > ema20[1] and ema20[1] > ema20[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1] and ema50[1] >= ema50[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1]
bool priorDowntrendForWyckoff = extendedDownwardMove and persistentDowntrend
bool priorUptrendForWyckoff = extendedUpwardMove and persistentUptrend
bool preliminaryWyckoffEffort = volumeAvailable and (relativeVolume >= wyckoffPreliminaryVolume or statisticalVolumeHigh or heatmapVolumeHighOrHigher and relativeVolume >= math.max(volumeMultiple, wyckoffPreliminaryVolume * 0.90))
bool climaxWyckoffEffort = volumeAvailable and (relativeVolume >= wyckoffClimaxVolume or statisticalVolumeExtreme or heatmapVolumeExtreme and relativeVolume >= math.max(volumeMultiple, wyckoffClimaxVolume * 0.80))
bool preliminarySupportRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and priorDowntrendForWyckoff and preliminaryWyckoffEffort and close < open and (vsaWideResult or wyckoffLowerWick >= candleRange * 0.15) and (na(priorWyckoffLow) or low <= priorWyckoffLow)
bool sellingClimaxRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and priorDowntrendForWyckoff and climaxWyckoffEffort and vsaWideResult and closeLocation >= 0.30 and wyckoffLowerWick >= candleRange * 0.15 and (na(priorWyckoffLow) or low <= priorWyckoffLow)
bool preliminarySupplyRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and priorUptrendForWyckoff and preliminaryWyckoffEffort and close > open and (vsaWideResult or wyckoffUpperWick >= candleRange * 0.15) and (na(priorWyckoffHigh) or high >= priorWyckoffHigh)
bool buyingClimaxRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and priorUptrendForWyckoff and climaxWyckoffEffort and vsaWideResult and closeLocation <= 0.70 and wyckoffUpperWick >= candleRange * 0.15 and (na(priorWyckoffHigh) or high >= priorWyckoffHigh)

bool wyckoffBuildingExpired = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode != 0 and wyckoffPhase < 5 and not na(wyckoffSequenceStartBar) and bar_index - wyckoffSequenceStartBar > wyckoffMaxSequenceBars
bool wyckoffPhaseEOppositeBreak = wyckoffPhase == 5 and ((wyckoffMode == 1 and (bearishChoch or (not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and close < wyckoffRangeSupport - breakBuffer))) or (wyckoffMode == -1 and (bullishChoch or (not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and close > wyckoffRangeResistance + breakBuffer))))
bool wyckoffPhaseEExpired = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode != 0 and wyckoffPhase == 5 and not na(wyckoffPhaseEBar) and (bar_index - wyckoffPhaseEBar > contextLookback or wyckoffPhaseEOppositeBreak)
if (wyckoffBuildingExpired or wyckoffPhaseEExpired)
    f_clear_label_array(wyckoffModelLabels)
    wyckoffSupportLine := f_clear_line(wyckoffSupportLine)
    wyckoffResistanceLine := f_clear_line(wyckoffResistanceLine)
    wyckoffMode := 0
    wyckoffStage := 0
    wyckoffPhase := 0
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := na
    wyckoffClimaxBar := na
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := na
    wyckoffSignBar := na
    wyckoffPhaseEBar := na
    wyckoffRangeSupport := na
    wyckoffRangeResistance := na
    wyckoffClimaxRvol := na
    wyckoffClimaxSpread := na
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := false
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := false
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na

bool preliminarySupportEvent = preliminarySupportRaw and not sellingClimaxRaw and (wyckoffMode == 0 or (wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffPhase == 5))
bool sellingClimaxEvent = sellingClimaxRaw and (wyckoffMode != 1 or wyckoffStage <= 1 or wyckoffPhase == 5)
bool preliminarySupplyEvent = preliminarySupplyRaw and not buyingClimaxRaw and not preliminarySupportEvent and not sellingClimaxEvent and (wyckoffMode == 0 or (wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffPhase == 5))
bool buyingClimaxEvent = buyingClimaxRaw and not sellingClimaxEvent and (wyckoffMode != -1 or wyckoffStage <= 1 or wyckoffPhase == 5)
bool startNewWyckoffModel = preliminarySupportEvent or preliminarySupplyEvent or (sellingClimaxEvent and (wyckoffMode != 1 or wyckoffPhase == 5)) or (buyingClimaxEvent and (wyckoffMode != -1 or wyckoffPhase == 5))

if startNewWyckoffModel
    f_clear_label_array(wyckoffModelLabels)
    wyckoffSupportLine := f_clear_line(wyckoffSupportLine)
    wyckoffResistanceLine := f_clear_line(wyckoffResistanceLine)
    // Fully clear the previous model before assigning the new event. Without
    // this reset, a fresh PS/PSY could inherit the old range boundary or climax
    // statistics and display a false completed range.
    wyckoffStage := 0
    wyckoffPhase := 0
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := na
    wyckoffClimaxBar := na
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := na
    wyckoffSignBar := na
    wyckoffPhaseEBar := na
    wyckoffRangeSupport := na
    wyckoffRangeResistance := na
    wyckoffClimaxRvol := na
    wyckoffClimaxSpread := na
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := false
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := false
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na

int confirmedPivotBar = bar_index - pivotBars
float wyckoffRangeHeight = not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) ? math.max(wyckoffRangeResistance - wyckoffRangeSupport, safeAtr) : safeAtr
float wyckoffRangeTolerance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.35, wyckoffRangeHeight * 0.20)
float confirmedPivotAtr = nz(safeAtr[pivotBars], safeAtr)
bool pivotVolumeQuieterThanClimax = volumeAvailable[pivotBars] and not na(wyckoffClimaxRvol) and relativeVolume[pivotBars] <= math.min(1.10, wyckoffClimaxRvol * 0.80)
bool pivotSpreadQuieterThanClimax = not na(relativeSpread[pivotBars]) and not na(wyckoffClimaxSpread) and relativeSpread[pivotBars] <= math.min(1.0, wyckoffClimaxSpread * 0.80)
// Compare a confirmed pivot with the ATR that existed at that pivot, not with
// the current bar's ATR which may have changed materially during confirmation.
bool accumulationAutomaticRallyEvent = wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage == 2 and newPivotHigh and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffClimaxBar and pivotHigh - wyckoffRangeSupport >= confirmedPivotAtr
bool distributionAutomaticReactionEvent = wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage == 2 and newPivotLow and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffClimaxBar and wyckoffRangeResistance - pivotLow >= confirmedPivotAtr
