bool accumulationSecondaryTestEvent = wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage >= 3 and wyckoffStage <= 4 and newPivotLow and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffAutomaticBar and math.abs(pivotLow - wyckoffRangeSupport) <= wyckoffRangeTolerance and pivotVolumeQuieterThanClimax and pivotSpreadQuieterThanClimax
bool distributionSecondaryTestEvent = wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage >= 3 and wyckoffStage <= 4 and newPivotHigh and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffAutomaticBar and math.abs(pivotHigh - wyckoffRangeResistance) <= wyckoffRangeTolerance and pivotVolumeQuieterThanClimax and pivotSpreadQuieterThanClimax
// Spring/UTAD belong to the active Wyckoff range. Do not require the latest
// generic swing to be the same level, because that coupling can miss a valid
// range-boundary sweep. A bar sweeping both range sides is ambiguous and is
// excluded from directional Phase C classification.
bool wyckoffTwoSidedRangeSweep = wyckoffMode != 0 and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and low < wyckoffRangeSupport - breakBuffer and high > wyckoffRangeResistance + breakBuffer
bool accumulationSpringEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage >= 4 and wyckoffStage < 6 and not wyckoffTwoSidedRangeSweep and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and low < wyckoffRangeSupport - breakBuffer and close > wyckoffRangeSupport
bool distributionUtadEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage >= 4 and wyckoffStage < 6 and not wyckoffTwoSidedRangeSweep and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and high > wyckoffRangeResistance + breakBuffer and close < wyckoffRangeResistance
int barsFromWyckoffPhaseCSweep = na(wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar
bool insideWyckoffPhaseCTestWindow = wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection != 0 and barsFromWyckoffPhaseCSweep > 0 and barsFromWyckoffPhaseCSweep <= retestWindow
bool accumulationSpringTestEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage == 5 and insideWyckoffPhaseCTestWindow and wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection == 1 and low <= wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel + retestTolerance and low > wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme and close > wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel and close > open and quieterTestVolume and quieterTestSpread
bool distributionUtadTestEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage == 5 and insideWyckoffPhaseCTestWindow and wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection == -1 and high >= wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel - retestTolerance and high < wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme and close < wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel and close < open and quieterTestVolume and quieterTestSpread
float wyckoffPhaseCTestLevel = accumulationSpringTestEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent ? wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel : na
float wyckoffPhaseCTestExtreme = accumulationSpringTestEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent ? wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme : na
bool wyckoffResistanceCrossEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage >= 4 and wyckoffStage < 6 and not wyckoffResistanceWasBroken and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and close > wyckoffRangeResistance + breakBuffer and close[1] <= nz(wyckoffRangeResistance[1], wyckoffRangeResistance) + nz(breakBuffer[1], breakBuffer)
bool wyckoffSupportCrossEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage >= 4 and wyckoffStage < 6 and not wyckoffSupportWasBroken and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and close < wyckoffRangeSupport - breakBuffer and close[1] >= nz(wyckoffRangeSupport[1], wyckoffRangeSupport) - nz(breakBuffer[1], breakBuffer)
bool accumulationSosEvent = (wyckoffResistanceCrossEvent and bullishBreakCandleQuality) and volumeAvailable and standardVolumeEffort and vsaWideResult and closeLocation >= 0.60
bool distributionSowEvent = (wyckoffSupportCrossEvent and bearishBreakCandleQuality) and volumeAvailable and standardVolumeEffort and vsaWideResult and closeLocation <= 0.40
bool quietWyckoffBackupPivot = volumeAvailable[pivotBars] and relativeVolume[pivotBars] <= 1.10 and not na(relativeSpread[pivotBars]) and relativeSpread[pivotBars] <= 1.0
bool accumulationLpsEvent = wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage >= 6 and wyckoffStage <= 7 and newPivotLow and not na(wyckoffSignBar) and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffSignBar and quietWyckoffBackupPivot and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and math.abs(pivotLow - wyckoffRangeResistance) <= wyckoffRangeTolerance and close[pivotBars] > wyckoffRangeResistance and closeLocation[pivotBars] >= 0.55
bool distributionLpsyEvent = wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage >= 6 and wyckoffStage <= 7 and newPivotHigh and not na(wyckoffSignBar) and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffSignBar and quietWyckoffBackupPivot and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and math.abs(pivotHigh - wyckoffRangeSupport) <= wyckoffRangeTolerance and close[pivotBars] < wyckoffRangeSupport and closeLocation[pivotBars] <= 0.45
bool accumulationPhaseEEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage == 7 and wyckoffPhase < 5 and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and close > wyckoffRangeResistance + safeAtr and ema20 > ema20[1]
bool distributionPhaseEEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage == 7 and wyckoffPhase < 5 and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and close < wyckoffRangeSupport - safeAtr and ema20 < ema20[1]

if wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection != 0 and barsFromWyckoffPhaseCSweep > retestWindow
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na

if sellingClimaxEvent
    int accumulationStartBar = wyckoffMode == 1 and not na(wyckoffSequenceStartBar) ? wyckoffSequenceStartBar : bar_index
    wyckoffMode := 1
    wyckoffStage := 2
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := accumulationStartBar
    wyckoffClimaxBar := bar_index
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := na
    wyckoffPhaseEBar := na
    wyckoffRangeSupport := low
    wyckoffRangeResistance := na
    wyckoffClimaxRvol := relativeVolume
    wyckoffClimaxSpread := relativeSpread
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := false
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := false
else if buyingClimaxEvent
    int distributionStartBar = wyckoffMode == -1 and not na(wyckoffSequenceStartBar) ? wyckoffSequenceStartBar : bar_index
    wyckoffMode := -1
    wyckoffStage := 2
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := distributionStartBar
    wyckoffClimaxBar := bar_index
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := na
    wyckoffPhaseEBar := na
    wyckoffRangeSupport := na
    wyckoffRangeResistance := high
    wyckoffClimaxRvol := relativeVolume
    wyckoffClimaxSpread := relativeSpread
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := false
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := false
else if preliminarySupportEvent
    wyckoffMode := 1
    wyckoffStage := 1
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := bar_index
    wyckoffRangeSupport := low
else if preliminarySupplyEvent
    wyckoffMode := -1
    wyckoffStage := 1
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := bar_index
    wyckoffRangeResistance := high
else if accumulationAutomaticRallyEvent
    wyckoffStage := 3
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := confirmedPivotBar
    wyckoffRangeResistance := pivotHigh
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := false
else if distributionAutomaticReactionEvent
    wyckoffStage := 3
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := confirmedPivotBar
    wyckoffRangeSupport := pivotLow
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := false
else if accumulationSecondaryTestEvent
    wyckoffStage := 4
    wyckoffPhase := 2
    wyckoffRangeSupport := math.min(wyckoffRangeSupport, pivotLow)
else if distributionSecondaryTestEvent
    wyckoffStage := 4
    wyckoffPhase := 2
    wyckoffRangeResistance := math.max(wyckoffRangeResistance, pivotHigh)
else if accumulationSpringEvent
    wyckoffStage := 5
    wyckoffPhase := 3
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 1
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := wyckoffRangeSupport
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := low
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := bar_index
else if accumulationSpringTestEvent
    wyckoffStage := 5
    wyckoffPhase := 3
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na
else if distributionUtadEvent
    wyckoffStage := 5
    wyckoffPhase := 3
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := -1
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := wyckoffRangeResistance
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := high
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := bar_index
else if distributionUtadTestEvent
    wyckoffStage := 5
    wyckoffPhase := 3
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na
else if accumulationSosEvent
    wyckoffStage := 6
    wyckoffPhase := 4
    wyckoffSignBar := bar_index
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := true
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na
else if distributionSowEvent
    wyckoffStage := 6
    wyckoffPhase := 4
    wyckoffSignBar := bar_index
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := true
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na
else if accumulationLpsEvent
    wyckoffStage := 7
    wyckoffPhase := 4
else if distributionLpsyEvent
    wyckoffStage := 7
    wyckoffPhase := 4
else if accumulationPhaseEEvent or distributionPhaseEEvent
    wyckoffStage := 8
    wyckoffPhase := 5
    wyckoffPhaseEBar := bar_index

string wyckoffPhaseState = f_wyckoff_phase_state(wyckoffMode, wyckoffPhase)

f_wyckoff_event_summary() =>
    string trendContext = wyckoffMode == 1 ? "سياق البداية: هبوط ممتد مؤكد" : wyckoffMode == -1 ? "سياق البداية: صعود ممتد مؤكد" : priorDowntrendForWyckoff ? "سياق مؤهل لتجميع: هبوط ممتد" : priorUptrendForWyckoff ? "سياق مؤهل لتصريف: صعود ممتد" : "لا يوجد اتجاه سابق ممتد ومؤهل"
    bool phaseERecent = wyckoffPhase == 5 and not na(wyckoffPhaseEBar) and bar_index - wyckoffPhaseEBar <= contextLookback
    int contextSignal = wyckoffMode == 1 and (wyckoffPhase == 3 or wyckoffPhase == 4 or phaseERecent) ? 1 : wyckoffMode == -1 and (wyckoffPhase == 3 or wyckoffPhase == 4 or phaseERecent) ? -1 : 0
    int eventCode = preliminarySupportEvent ? 1 : sellingClimaxEvent ? 2 : accumulationAutomaticRallyEvent ? 3 : accumulationSecondaryTestEvent ? 4 : accumulationSpringEvent ? 5 : accumulationSpringTestEvent ? 6 : accumulationSosEvent ? 7 : accumulationLpsEvent ? 8 : accumulationPhaseEEvent ? 9 : preliminarySupplyEvent ? 10 : buyingClimaxEvent ? 11 : distributionAutomaticReactionEvent ? 12 : distributionSecondaryTestEvent ? 13 : distributionUtadEvent ? 14 : distributionUtadTestEvent ? 15 : distributionSowEvent ? 16 : distributionLpsyEvent ? 17 : distributionPhaseEEvent ? 18 : 0
    bool eventNow = eventCode != 0
    string eventText = f_text_code(TXT_WYCKOFF_EVENT, eventCode)
    float eventLevel = eventCode == 1 or eventCode == 2 ? low : eventCode == 3 ? pivotHigh : eventCode == 4 ? pivotLow : eventCode == 5 ? low : eventCode == 6 ? wyckoffPhaseCTestLevel : eventCode == 7 ? high : eventCode == 8 ? pivotLow : eventCode == 9 ? high : eventCode == 10 or eventCode == 11 ? high : eventCode == 12 ? pivotLow : eventCode == 13 ? pivotHigh : eventCode == 14 ? high : eventCode == 15 ? wyckoffPhaseCTestLevel : eventCode == 16 ? low : eventCode == 17 ? pivotHigh : eventCode == 18 ? low : na
    bool pivotTimed = accumulationAutomaticRallyEvent or accumulationSecondaryTestEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or distributionAutomaticReactionEvent or distributionSecondaryTestEvent or distributionLpsyEvent
    int eventBar = pivotTimed ? confirmedPivotBar : bar_index
    float eventAtr = pivotTimed ? nz(safeAtr[pivotBars], safeAtr) : safeAtr
    string markerText = f_text_code(TXT_WYCKOFF_MARKER_SHORT, eventCode)
    color markerColor = (preliminarySupportEvent or sellingClimaxEvent or preliminarySupplyEvent or buyingClimaxEvent) ? warningColor : (accumulationAutomaticRallyEvent or accumulationSecondaryTestEvent or distributionAutomaticReactionEvent or distributionSecondaryTestEvent) ? neutralColor : (preliminarySupportEvent or sellingClimaxEvent or accumulationAutomaticRallyEvent or accumulationSecondaryTestEvent or accumulationSpringEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or accumulationSosEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or accumulationPhaseEEvent) ? bullColor : bearColor
    [trendContext, phaseERecent, contextSignal, eventCode, eventNow, eventText, eventLevel, eventBar, eventAtr, (preliminarySupportEvent or sellingClimaxEvent or accumulationSecondaryTestEvent or accumulationSpringEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or distributionAutomaticReactionEvent or distributionSowEvent or distributionPhaseEEvent), markerText, markerColor]

[wyckoffTrendContextState, wyckoffPhaseERecent, wyckoffContextSignal, wyckoffEventCode, wyckoffEventNow, wyckoffEventText, wyckoffEventLevel, wyckoffEventBar, wyckoffEventAtr, wyckoffLabelBelow, compactWyckoffMarkerText, wyckoffMarkerColor] = f_wyckoff_event_summary()

if wyckoffEventNow and chartSignalDisplayAllowed and showWyckoffModelLabels
    int effectiveMaxWyckoffLabels = fastestIntradayDisplay ? math.min(maxWyckoffModelLabels, 5) : compactIntradayDisplay ? math.min(maxWyckoffModelLabels, 7) : math.min(maxWyckoffModelLabels, 12)
    f_push_chart_marker(wyckoffModelLabels, effectiveMaxWyckoffLabels, wyckoffEventBar, wyckoffEventLevel, compactWyckoffMarkerText, wyckoffLabelBelow, wyckoffMarkerColor, wyckoffEventAtr, compactIntradayDisplay ? 0.60 : 0.40, compactIntradayDisplay ? 0.22 : 0.18)

bool wyckoffRangeReady = wyckoffMode != 0 and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and not na(wyckoffSequenceStartBar)
string wyckoffRangeState = wyckoffRangeReady ? "دعم النطاق " + f_price(wyckoffRangeSupport) + " | مقاومة النطاق " + f_price(wyckoffRangeResistance) : "حدود نطاق وايكوف غير مكتملة"
if barstate.isconfirmed
    if stockChartAllowed and showWyckoffRangeLines and wyckoffRangeReady
        int wyckoffRangeLeftBar = wyckoffSequenceStartBar
        if na(wyckoffSupportLine)
            wyckoffSupportLine := line.new(wyckoffRangeLeftBar, wyckoffRangeSupport, bar_index + 1, wyckoffRangeSupport, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(bullColor, wyckoffRangeLineTransparency), style = line.style_dotted, width = 1, force_overlay = true)
        else
            line.set_xy1(wyckoffSupportLine, wyckoffRangeLeftBar, wyckoffRangeSupport)
            line.set_xy2(wyckoffSupportLine, bar_index + 1, wyckoffRangeSupport)
            line.set_color(wyckoffSupportLine, color.new(bullColor, wyckoffRangeLineTransparency))
        if na(wyckoffResistanceLine)
            wyckoffResistanceLine := line.new(wyckoffRangeLeftBar, wyckoffRangeResistance, bar_index + 1, wyckoffRangeResistance, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(bearColor, wyckoffRangeLineTransparency), style = line.style_dotted, width = 1, force_overlay = true)
        else
            line.set_xy1(wyckoffResistanceLine, wyckoffRangeLeftBar, wyckoffRangeResistance)
            line.set_xy2(wyckoffResistanceLine, bar_index + 1, wyckoffRangeResistance)
            line.set_color(wyckoffResistanceLine, color.new(bearColor, wyckoffRangeLineTransparency))
    else
        wyckoffSupportLine := f_clear_line(wyckoffSupportLine)
        wyckoffResistanceLine := f_clear_line(wyckoffResistanceLine)

// failed breaks. They share one capped auction/trigger vote later, but the
// dashboard must not label every generic sweep as a Wyckoff event.
int barsSinceBullishWyckoff = ta.barssince(accumulationSpringEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or accumulationSosEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or accumulationPhaseEEvent)
int barsSinceBearishWyckoff = ta.barssince(distributionUtadEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent or distributionSowEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or distributionPhaseEEvent)
int barsSinceBullishLiquidityBehavior = ta.barssince(bullishSweep or bearishBreakFailed or bullishWyckoffTest)
int barsSinceBearishLiquidityBehavior = ta.barssince(bearishSweep or bullishBreakFailed or bearishWyckoffTest)
bool recentBullishWyckoff = (not na(barsSinceBullishWyckoff) and barsSinceBullishWyckoff <= contextLookback) or wyckoffContextSignal == 1
bool recentBearishWyckoff = (not na(barsSinceBearishWyckoff) and barsSinceBearishWyckoff <= contextLookback) or wyckoffContextSignal == -1
bool recentBullishAuctionTrigger = recentBullishWyckoff or (not na(barsSinceBullishLiquidityBehavior) and barsSinceBullishLiquidityBehavior <= contextLookback)
bool recentBearishAuctionTrigger = recentBearishWyckoff or (not na(barsSinceBearishLiquidityBehavior) and barsSinceBearishLiquidityBehavior <= contextLookback)

// نماذج الشموع: الشكل يقرأ سياقه السابق، ولا يصبح انعكاسًا مؤكدًا إلا بإغلاق الشمعة التالية.
float realBody = math.max(math.abs(close - open), syminfo.mintick)
float upperWick = high - math.max(open, close)
float lowerWick = math.min(open, close) - low
bool precedingDownContext = resolvedStructureBias[1] == -1 or (close[1] < ema20[1] and ema20[1] <= ema50[1])
bool precedingUpContext = resolvedStructureBias[1] == 1 or (close[1] > ema20[1] and ema20[1] >= ema50[1])

f_candle_pattern_code() =>
    bool doji = bodyRatio <= 0.10
    int code = precedingDownContext and close > open and close[1] < open[1] and open <= close[1] and close >= open[1] and bodyRatio >= 0.50 ? 1 :
      precedingUpContext and close < open and close[1] > open[1] and open >= close[1] and close <= open[1] and bodyRatio >= 0.50 ? 2 :
      doji and lowerWick >= candleRange * 0.60 and upperWick <= candleRange * 0.10 ? 23 :
      doji and upperWick >= candleRange * 0.60 and lowerWick <= candleRange * 0.10 ? 24 :
      doji and upperWick >= candleRange * 0.35 and lowerWick >= candleRange * 0.35 ? 25 :
      doji ? 5 :
      precedingDownContext and bodyRatio <= 0.40 and lowerWick >= realBody * 2.0 and upperWick <= realBody ? 3 :
      precedingUpContext and bodyRatio <= 0.40 and upperWick >= realBody * 2.0 and lowerWick <= realBody ? 4 :
      precedingDownContext and bodyRatio <= 0.40 and upperWick >= realBody * 2.0 and lowerWick <= realBody ? 13 :
      precedingUpContext and bodyRatio <= 0.40 and lowerWick >= realBody * 2.0 and upperWick <= realBody ? 14 :
      precedingDownContext and close > open and close[1] < open[1] and open <= close[1] + syminfo.mintick and close > (open[1] + close[1]) * 0.50 and close < open[1] ? 15 :
      precedingUpContext and close < open and close[1] > open[1] and open >= close[1] - syminfo.mintick and close < (open[1] + close[1]) * 0.50 and close > open[1] ? 16 :
      precedingDownContext[2] and close[2] < open[2] and bodyRatio[2] >= 0.55 and bodyRatio[1] <= 0.30 and math.max(open[1], close[1]) <= (open[2] + close[2]) * 0.50 + nz(safeAtr[1], safeAtr) * 0.10 and close > open and bodyRatio >= 0.50 and close > (open[2] + close[2]) * 0.50 ? 17 :
      precedingUpContext[2] and close[2] > open[2] and bodyRatio[2] >= 0.55 and bodyRatio[1] <= 0.30 and math.min(open[1], close[1]) >= (open[2] + close[2]) * 0.50 - nz(safeAtr[1], safeAtr) * 0.10 and close < open and bodyRatio >= 0.50 and close < (open[2] + close[2]) * 0.50 ? 18 :
      precedingDownContext and close > open and close[1] < open[1] and math.max(open, close) <= open[1] and math.min(open, close) >= close[1] and bodyRatio <= bodyRatio[1] * 0.75 ? 19 :
      precedingUpContext and close < open and close[1] > open[1] and math.max(open, close) <= close[1] and math.min(open, close) >= open[1] and bodyRatio <= bodyRatio[1] * 0.75 ? 20 :
      precedingDownContext and close > open and close[1] < open[1] and math.abs(low - low[1]) <= math.max(safeAtr * 0.05, syminfo.mintick * 3.0) ? 21 :
      precedingUpContext and close < open and close[1] > open[1] and math.abs(high - high[1]) <= math.max(safeAtr * 0.05, syminfo.mintick * 3.0) ? 22 :
      high < high[1] and low > low[1] ? 12 :
      bodyRatio > 0.10 and bodyRatio <= 0.30 and upperWick >= realBody and lowerWick >= realBody ? 26 :
      bodyRatio <= 0.45 and lowerWick >= realBody * 2.0 and upperWick <= realBody * 1.25 ? 6 :
      bodyRatio <= 0.45 and upperWick >= realBody * 2.0 and lowerWick <= realBody * 1.25 ? 7 :
      close > open and bodyRatio >= 0.85 and upperWick <= candleRange * 0.08 and lowerWick <= candleRange * 0.08 ? 8 :
      close < open and bodyRatio >= 0.85 and upperWick <= candleRange * 0.08 and lowerWick <= candleRange * 0.08 ? 9 :
      close > open and bodyRatio >= 0.70 ? 27 : close < open and bodyRatio >= 0.70 ? 28 : close > open ? 10 : close < open ? 11 : 0
    code
int candlePatternCode = f_candle_pattern_code()
bool bullishReversalPatternRaw = candlePatternCode == 1 or candlePatternCode == 3 or candlePatternCode == 13 or candlePatternCode == 15 or candlePatternCode == 17 or candlePatternCode == 19 or candlePatternCode == 21 or (candlePatternCode == 23 and precedingDownContext)
bool bearishReversalPatternRaw = candlePatternCode == 2 or candlePatternCode == 4 or candlePatternCode == 14 or candlePatternCode == 16 or candlePatternCode == 18 or candlePatternCode == 20 or candlePatternCode == 22 or (candlePatternCode == 24 and precedingUpContext)
[insideBarRaw, bullishInsideBarSetup, bearishInsideBarSetup, volumeRisingAfterDecline, volumeRisingAfterAdvanceWithWeakResult, sidewaysRangeHigh, sidewaysRangeLow, sidewaysRangeReady, bullishInsideBarBreakRaw, bearishInsideBarBreakRaw, bullishRangeBreakRaw, bearishRangeBreakRaw, bullishInsideBarBreakout, bearishInsideBarBreakout, bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline, bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance] = f_price_action_breakouts(usePriceActionBreakouts, sidewaysRangeBars, sidewaysRangeAtrWidth, breakBuffer, safeAtr, volumePass, volumeAvailable, relativeVolume, closeLocation, precedingDownContext, precedingUpContext, bullishBreakCandleQuality, bearishBreakCandleQuality)
bool bullishCandleConfirmation = useCandleConfirmation and barstate.isconfirmed and (bullishReversalPatternRaw[1] and close > high[1] or bullishInsideBarBreakout)
bool bearishCandleConfirmation = useCandleConfirmation and barstate.isconfirmed and (bearishReversalPatternRaw[1] and close < low[1] or bearishInsideBarBreakout)
bool bullishImportantCandleConfirmation = bullishCandleConfirmation and (bullishWyckoffTest or bullishSweep or bullishBreakFailed or bullishDemandRetest or bullishRetest or bullishChoch or bullishBos or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline or accumulationSpringEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or accumulationSosEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or accumulationPhaseEEvent)
bool bearishImportantCandleConfirmation = bearishCandleConfirmation and (bearishWyckoffTest or bearishSweep or bearishBreakFailed or bearishSupplyRetest or bearishRetest or bearishChoch or bearishBos or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance or distributionUtadEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent or distributionSowEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or distributionPhaseEEvent)
int barsSinceBullishCandle = ta.barssince(bullishCandleConfirmation)
int barsSinceBearishCandle = ta.barssince(bearishCandleConfirmation)
bool recentBullishCandle = not na(barsSinceBullishCandle) and barsSinceBullishCandle <= contextLookback
bool recentBearishCandle = not na(barsSinceBearishCandle) and barsSinceBearishCandle <= contextLookback
string confirmedCandleName = f_text_code(TXT_CANDLE_TYPE, candlePatternCode[1])
var string candleState = "لا يوجد نموذج شموعي مؤكد"
if bullishCandleConfirmation
    candleState := bullishInsideBarBreakout ? "انسايد بار صاعد مؤكد" : confirmedCandleName + " مؤكد"
else if bearishCandleConfirmation
    candleState := bearishInsideBarBreakout ? "انسايد بار هابط مؤكد" : confirmedCandleName + " مؤكد"

f_update_candle_volume_vsa_state() =>
    var float lastKeyCandleHigh = na
    var float lastKeyCandleLow = na
    var int lastKeyCandleBar = na
    var float highVolumeResistanceHigh = na
    var float highVolumeResistanceLow = na
    var int highVolumeResistanceBar = na
    var float highVolumeSupportHigh = na
    var float highVolumeSupportLow = na
    var int highVolumeSupportBar = na
    bool bullishHorizontalRetestSetup = false
    bool bearishHorizontalRetestSetup = false
    string currentCandleDescription = "لا توجد قراءة وصفية"
    string priceActionState = "لا يوجد إعداد برايس أكشن"
    string volumeCandleLevelsState = "لا توجد منطقة حجم مهمة"
    int vsaSignal = 0
    string vsaState = "لا إشارة حجم/سلوك قوية"
    // وصف آخر شمعة مغلقة: مختصر، ولا يمثل دخولًا منفردًا.
    bool strongBullCandle = candlePatternCode == 8 or candlePatternCode == 27
    bool strongBearCandle = candlePatternCode == 9 or candlePatternCode == 28
    bullishHorizontalRetestSetup := barstate.isconfirmed and resistanceClusterActsAsSupport and (bullishCandleConfirmation or strongBullCandle) and vwapLongPass
    bearishHorizontalRetestSetup := barstate.isconfirmed and supportClusterActsAsResistance and (bearishCandleConfirmation or strongBearCandle) and vwapShortPass
    int currentCandleBias = bullishReversalPatternRaw or ((bodyRatio <= 0.45 and lowerWick >= realBody * 2.0 and upperWick <= realBody * 1.25) and close >= open) or strongBullCandle ? 1 : bearishReversalPatternRaw or ((bodyRatio <= 0.45 and upperWick >= realBody * 2.0 and lowerWick <= realBody * 1.25) and close <= open) or strongBearCandle ? -1 : 0
    string currentCandleType = f_text_code(TXT_CANDLE_TYPE, candlePatternCode)
    string currentCandleMeaning = bullishInsideBarSetup ? "تجميع بعد هبوط؛ ينتظر كسر الأم" : bearishInsideBarSetup ? "تجميع بعد صعود؛ ينتظر كسر الأم" : insideBarRaw ? "تجميع؛ ينتظر كسر الأم" : bullishReversalPatternRaw ? "انعكاس صاعد؛ ينتظر تأكيد" : bearishReversalPatternRaw ? "انعكاس هابط؛ ينتظر تأكيد" : candlePatternCode == 8 ? "سيطرة شراء" : candlePatternCode == 9 ? "سيطرة بيع" : bodyRatio <= 0.10 or candlePatternCode == 26 ? "تردد" : currentCandleBias == 1 ? "ضغط شراء" : currentCandleBias == -1 ? "ضغط بيع" : "محايد"
    string currentCandleVolumeText = volumeAvailable ? f_ratio(relativeVolume) : "غير متاح"
    currentCandleDescription := currentCandleType + " | " + currentCandleMeaning + " | حجم " + currentCandleVolumeText
    bool highVolumeCandle = volumeAvailable and (relativeVolume >= volumeMultiple or heatmapVolumeHighOrHigher)
    bool keyRangeCandleNow = barstate.isconfirmed and (vsaWideResult and (strongBullCandle or strongBearCandle) or highVolumeCandle and (vsaWideResult or vsaNarrowResult))
    if keyRangeCandleNow
        lastKeyCandleHigh := high
        lastKeyCandleLow := low
        lastKeyCandleBar := bar_index
    else if not na(lastKeyCandleBar) and bar_index - lastKeyCandleBar > keyCandleMaximumAge
        lastKeyCandleHigh := na
        lastKeyCandleLow := na
        lastKeyCandleBar := na

    bool highVolumePeakZoneEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and highVolumeCandle and precedingUpContext and (nearStrongResistance or high >= nz(lastSwingHigh, high) - safeAtr * 0.25) and (closeLocation <= 0.55 or upperWick >= realBody)
    bool highVolumeBottomZoneEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and highVolumeCandle and precedingDownContext and (nearStrongSupport or low <= nz(lastSwingLow, low) + safeAtr * 0.25) and (closeLocation >= 0.45 or lowerWick >= realBody)
    bool highVolumeResistanceExpired = not na(highVolumeResistanceBar) and (bar_index - highVolumeResistanceBar > keyCandleMaximumAge or close > highVolumeResistanceHigh + breakBuffer)
    bool highVolumeSupportExpired = not na(highVolumeSupportBar) and (bar_index - highVolumeSupportBar > keyCandleMaximumAge or close < highVolumeSupportLow - breakBuffer)
    if highVolumeResistanceExpired
        highVolumeResistanceHigh := na
        highVolumeResistanceLow := na
        highVolumeResistanceBar := na
    if highVolumeSupportExpired
        highVolumeSupportHigh := na
        highVolumeSupportLow := na
        highVolumeSupportBar := na
    if highVolumePeakZoneEvent
        highVolumeResistanceHigh := high
        highVolumeResistanceLow := low
        highVolumeResistanceBar := bar_index
    if highVolumeBottomZoneEvent
        highVolumeSupportHigh := high
        highVolumeSupportLow := low
        highVolumeSupportBar := bar_index
    bool highVolumeResistanceActive = not na(highVolumeResistanceHigh) and not na(highVolumeResistanceLow)
    bool highVolumeSupportActive = not na(highVolumeSupportHigh) and not na(highVolumeSupportLow)
    priceActionState := ((bullishInsideBarBreakout) ? "انسايد بار بعد هبوط: إغلاق مؤكد فوق الشمعة الأم" : (bearishInsideBarBreakout) ? "انسايد بار بعد صعود: إغلاق مؤكد أسفل الشمعة الأم" : (bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline) ? "اختراق نطاق بإغلاق وحجم مؤهل" : (bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance) ? "كسر نطاق بإغلاق وحجم مؤهل" : (bullishInsideBarSetup) ? "انسايد بار بعد هبوط: انتظار إغلاق فوق أعلى الشمعة الأم" : (bearishInsideBarSetup) ? "انسايد بار بعد صعود: انتظار إغلاق أسفل أدنى الشمعة الأم" : (insideBarRaw) ? "انسايد بار محايد: لا دخول قبل كسر الشمعة الأم" : (sidewaysRangeReady) ? "نطاق ضيق تحت المراقبة " + f_price((sidewaysRangeLow)) + " - " + f_price((sidewaysRangeHigh)) : "لا يوجد انكماش سعري مؤهل")
    volumeCandleLevelsState := f_volume_candle_levels_text(volumeRisingAfterDecline, volumeRisingAfterAdvanceWithWeakResult, highVolumeResistanceActive, highVolumeResistanceLow, highVolumeResistanceHigh, highVolumeSupportActive, highVolumeSupportLow, highVolumeSupportHigh)
    bool bullishVsaAbsorption = vsaHighEffort and vsaNarrowResult and closeLocation >= 0.55 and (bullishSweep or bearishBreakFailed)
    bool bearishVsaAbsorption = vsaHighEffort and vsaNarrowResult and closeLocation <= 0.45 and (bearishSweep or bullishBreakFailed)
    bool bullishVsaConfirmation = useVsaAnalysis and barstate.isconfirmed and vsaHighEffort[1] and vsaNarrowResult[1] and close > high[1] and (resolvedStructureBias[1] == -1 or bullishSweep[1] or bearishBreakFailed[1])
    bool bearishVsaConfirmation = useVsaAnalysis and barstate.isconfirmed and vsaHighEffort[1] and vsaNarrowResult[1] and close < low[1] and (resolvedStructureBias[1] == 1 or bearishSweep[1] or bullishBreakFailed[1])
    vsaSignal := not useVsaAnalysis ? 0 : (bullishVsaConfirmation or bullishVsaAbsorption or bullishVsaEffortResult) ? 1 : (bearishVsaConfirmation or bearishVsaAbsorption or bearishVsaEffortResult) ? -1 : 0
    int vsaStateCode = (not (useVsaAnalysis) ? 1 : (bullishVsaConfirmation) ? 2 : (bearishVsaConfirmation) ? 3 : not (volumeAvailable) ? 4 : (bullishVsaAbsorption) ? 5 : (bearishVsaAbsorption) ? 6 : (bullishVsaEffortResult) ? 7 : (bearishVsaEffortResult) ? 8 : (vsaHighEffort) and (vsaNarrowResult) ? 9 : (vsaHighEffort) ? 10 : 0)
    vsaState := (f_text_code(TXT_VSA_STATE, (vsaStateCode)))

    [lastKeyCandleHigh, lastKeyCandleLow, lastKeyCandleBar, highVolumeResistanceHigh, highVolumeResistanceLow, highVolumeResistanceBar, highVolumeSupportHigh, highVolumeSupportLow, highVolumeSupportBar, bullishHorizontalRetestSetup, bearishHorizontalRetestSetup, currentCandleDescription, priceActionState, volumeCandleLevelsState, vsaSignal, vsaState]

[lastKeyCandleHigh, lastKeyCandleLow, lastKeyCandleBar, highVolumeResistanceHigh, highVolumeResistanceLow, highVolumeResistanceBar, highVolumeSupportHigh, highVolumeSupportLow, highVolumeSupportBar, bullishHorizontalRetestSetup, bearishHorizontalRetestSetup, currentCandleDescription, priceActionState, volumeCandleLevelsState, vsaSignal, vsaState] = f_update_candle_volume_vsa_state()
// Structural AVWAP starts from a confirmed change, liquidity event, or tested zone.
f_structural_vwap_anchor_event() =>
    barstate.isconfirmed and (bullishChoch or bearishChoch or bullishSweep or bearishSweep or bullishBreakFailed or bearishBreakFailed or bullishWyckoffTest or bearishWyckoffTest or accumulationSosEvent or distributionSowEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or bullishDemandRetest or bearishSupplyRetest or qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout or strongResistanceBreak or strongSupportBreak) or bullishInsideBarBreakout or bearishInsideBarBreakout or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance

bool structuralVwapAnchorEvent = f_structural_vwap_anchor_event()
float structuralVwapBarVolume = nz(volume, 0.0)
var float structuralVwapPriceVolume = na
var float structuralVwapVolume = na

if not useStructuralVwap
    structuralVwapPriceVolume := na
    structuralVwapVolume := na
else if structuralVwapAnchorEvent
    // A new anchor with no valid volume must invalidate the old structural
    // VWAP. Keeping the previous accumulator would falsely label it as anchored
    // to the newest event.
    structuralVwapPriceVolume := structuralVwapBarVolume > 0 ? hlc3 * structuralVwapBarVolume : na
    structuralVwapVolume := structuralVwapBarVolume > 0 ? structuralVwapBarVolume : na
else if not na(structuralVwapVolume) and structuralVwapVolume > 0 and structuralVwapBarVolume > 0
    structuralVwapPriceVolume += hlc3 * structuralVwapBarVolume
    structuralVwapVolume += structuralVwapBarVolume

bool structuralVwapReady = useStructuralVwap and not na(structuralVwapVolume) and structuralVwapVolume > 0
float structuralVwapValue = structuralVwapReady ? structuralVwapPriceVolume / structuralVwapVolume : na
string analysisVwapLabel = structuralVwapReady ? "هيكلي" : "دوري " + effectiveVwapAnchorTimeframe
if barstate.isconfirmed
    confirmedAnalysisVwapValue := (structuralVwapReady ? structuralVwapValue : vwapValue)
    confirmedStructuralVwapReady := structuralVwapReady
// Preserve the exact broken level before the break watcher is reset below.
float confirmedRetestLevel = bullishRetest or bearishRetest ? resolvedBreakEventLevel : na
f_general_event_direction(int code) =>
    code == 3 ? -1 : code == 4 ? 1 : code >= 5 and code <= 26 ? (code % 2 == 1 ? 1 : -1) : code == 0 and bullishRsiDivergence ? 1 : code == 0 and bearishRsiDivergence ? -1 : 0


f_chart_event_summary() =>
    int eventCode = inverseHeadShouldersConfirmed ? 25 : headShouldersConfirmed ? 26 : doubleBottomConfirmed ? 13 : doubleTopConfirmed ? 14 : bullishChoch ? 19 : bearishChoch ? 20 : bullishBos ? 21 : bearishBos ? 22 : twoSidedLiquiditySweep ? 2 : bullishBreakFailed ? 3 : bearishBreakFailed ? 4 : bullishSweep ? 7 : bearishSweep ? 8 : qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout ? 9 : qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout ? 10 : bullishWyckoffTest ? 5 : bearishWyckoffTest ? 6 : bullishDemandRetest ? 11 : bearishSupplyRetest ? 12 : bullishRetest ? 17 : bearishRetest ? 18 : bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline ? 23 : bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance ? 24 : bullishImportantCandleConfirmation ? 15 : bearishImportantCandleConfirmation ? 16 : wyckoffEventNow ? 1 : 0
    int eventDirection = f_general_event_direction(eventCode)
    string eventText = eventCode == 2 ? "سحب مزدوج" : eventCode == 3 ? "اختراق وهمي" : eventCode == 4 ? "كسر وهمي" : eventCode == 5 or eventCode == 6 ? "اختبار سحب" : eventCode == 7 or eventCode == 8 ? "سحب سيولة" : eventCode == 9 ? "كسر وهمي" : eventCode == 10 ? "اختراق وهمي" : eventCode == 11 ? "اختبار طلب" : eventCode == 12 ? "اختبار عرض" : eventCode == 13 ? "قاعان" : eventCode == 14 ? "قمتان" : eventCode == 15 or eventCode == 16 ? confirmedCandleName : eventCode == 17 or eventCode == 18 ? "إعادة اختبار" : eventCode == 19 ? "CHoCH صاعد" : eventCode == 20 ? "CHoCH هابط" : eventCode == 21 ? "BOS صاعد" : eventCode == 22 ? "BOS هابط" : eventCode == 23 ? "اختراق نطاق" : eventCode == 24 ? "كسر نطاق" : eventCode == 25 ? "رأس وكتفين مقلوب" : eventCode == 26 ? "رأس وكتفين" : eventCode == 0 and bullishRsiDivergence ? "دايفرجنس صاعد" : eventCode == 0 and bearishRsiDivergence ? "دايفرجنس هابط" : ""
    int eventBar = eventCode == 13 ? doubleBottomPatternBar : eventCode == 14 ? doubleTopPatternBar : eventCode == 15 or eventCode == 16 ? bar_index - 1 : eventCode == 0 and (bullishRsiDivergence or bearishRsiDivergence) ? bar_index - pivotBars : bar_index
    float eventLevel = eventCode == 25 ? ihsNecklineNow : eventCode == 26 ? hsNecklineNow : eventCode == 2 ? close : eventCode == 3 or eventCode == 4 or eventCode == 17 or eventCode == 18 ? resolvedBreakEventLevel : eventCode == 5 or eventCode == 6 ? wyckoffTestLevel : eventCode == 7 ? lastSwingLow : eventCode == 8 ? lastSwingHigh : eventCode == 9 or eventCode == 10 ? effectiveChannelFakeoutLevel : eventCode == 11 ? demandZoneHigh : eventCode == 12 ? supplyZoneLow : eventCode == 13 ? doubleBottomPatternLevel : eventCode == 14 ? doubleTopPatternLevel : eventCode == 19 or eventCode == 21 ? lastSwingHigh : eventCode == 20 or eventCode == 22 ? lastSwingLow : eventCode == 15 ? low[1] : eventCode == 16 ? high[1] : eventCode == 23 ? sidewaysRangeHigh : eventCode == 24 ? sidewaysRangeLow : eventCode == 0 and bullishRsiDivergence ? pivotLow : eventCode == 0 and bearishRsiDivergence ? pivotHigh : na
    float eventAtr = eventCode == 15 or eventCode == 16 ? nz(safeAtr[1], safeAtr) : eventCode == 0 and (bullishRsiDivergence or bearishRsiDivergence) ? nz(safeAtr[pivotBars], safeAtr) : safeAtr
    bool eventWarning = eventCode >= 2 and eventCode <= 4 or eventCode >= 7 and eventCode <= 10
    bool importantEvent = eventCode == 1 or eventCode >= 3 and eventCode <= 10 or eventCode >= 13 and eventCode <= 16 or eventCode >= 19 and eventCode <= 26
    bool markerEnabled = importantEvent and eventText != "" and (eventCode == 15 or eventCode == 16 ? showImportantCandleLabel : showEventLabel)
    [eventCode, eventDirection, eventText, eventBar, eventLevel, eventAtr, eventWarning, (eventWarning ? warningColor : eventDirection == 1 ? bullColor : eventDirection == -1 ? bearColor : neutralColor), importantEvent, markerEnabled]

[generalEventCode, unifiedEventDirection, unifiedEventText, unifiedEventBar, unifiedEventLevel, unifiedEventAtr, unifiedEventWarning, unifiedEventColor, importantGeneralEvent, unifiedEventMarkerEnabled] = f_chart_event_summary()

f_pattern_state_summary() =>
    float flatSlope = safeAtr * 0.03
    string compressionState = not useChartPatterns ? "معطل" : importantHighTrendReady and importantLowTrendReady and importantHighSlope < -flatSlope and importantLowSlope > flatSlope ? "مثلث متماثل | ضغط" : importantHighTrendReady and importantLowTrendReady and math.abs(importantHighSlope) <= flatSlope and importantLowSlope > flatSlope ? "مثلث صاعد | ميل صاعد" : importantHighTrendReady and importantLowTrendReady and importantHighSlope < -flatSlope and math.abs(importantLowSlope) <= flatSlope ? "مثلث هابط | ميل هابط" : importantHighTrendReady and importantLowTrendReady and importantHighSlope > flatSlope and importantLowSlope > importantHighSlope ? "وتد صاعد | ضعف محتمل" : importantHighTrendReady and importantLowTrendReady and importantHighSlope < importantLowSlope and importantLowSlope < -flatSlope ? "وتد هابط | تعافٍ محتمل" : convergingImportantStructure ? "تضيق سعري | قرب حركة" : sidewaysRangeReady ? "نطاق عرضي | انتظار كسر" : "لا نموذج"
    string technicalState = not useChartPatterns ? "النماذج: معطلة" : inverseHeadShouldersConfirmed ? "رأس وكتفين مقلوب | صاعد مؤكد" : headShouldersConfirmed ? "رأس وكتفين | هابط مؤكد" : doubleBottomConfirmed ? "قاعان | صاعد مؤكد" : doubleTopConfirmed ? "قمتان | هابط مؤكد" : inverseHeadShouldersReady ? "رأس وكتفين مقلوب | قيد التكوين" : headShouldersReady ? "رأس وكتفين | قيد التكوين" : doubleBottomArmed ? "قاعان | قيد التكوين" : doubleTopArmed ? "قمتان | قيد التكوين" : compressionState
    [flatSlope, compressionState, technicalState]

[patternFlatSlope, compressionPatternState, technicalPatternState] = f_pattern_state_summary()


// Break watcher lifecycle is resolved before re-arming near its definition.
var string lastWyckoffEvent = "لا يوجد"
var string lastClassicEvent = "لا يوجد"
var int lastWyckoffEventBar = na
var int lastClassicEventBar = na

if wyckoffEventNow
    lastWyckoffEvent := wyckoffEventText
    if accumulationSosEvent
        lastClassicEvent := "اختراق نطاق"
    else if distributionSowEvent
        lastClassicEvent := "كسر نطاق"
    else if accumulationLpsEvent
        lastClassicEvent := "إعادة اختبار دعم"
    else if distributionLpsyEvent
        lastClassicEvent := "إعادة اختبار مقاومة"
else if bullishWyckoffTest
    lastWyckoffEvent := "اختبار سحب سيولة صاعد"
    lastClassicEvent := "اختبار دعم ناجح"
else if bearishWyckoffTest
    lastWyckoffEvent := "اختبار سحب سيولة هابط"
    lastClassicEvent := "اختبار مقاومة ناجح"
else if twoSidedLiquiditySweep
    lastWyckoffEvent := "سحب سيولة مزدوج"
    lastClassicEvent := "سحب سيولة مزدوج"
else if bullishSweep
    lastWyckoffEvent := "كسر وهمي / سحب سيولة"
    lastClassicEvent := "كسر وهمي للدعم"
else if bearishSweep
    lastWyckoffEvent := "اختراق وهمي / سحب سيولة"
    lastClassicEvent := "اختراق وهمي للمقاومة"
else if bearishBreakFailed
    lastWyckoffEvent := "كسر وهمي"
    lastClassicEvent := "كسر وهمي"
else if bullishBreakFailed
    lastWyckoffEvent := "اختراق وهمي"
    lastClassicEvent := "اختراق وهمي"
else if inverseHeadShouldersConfirmed
    lastClassicEvent := "رأس وكتفين مقلوب"
else if headShouldersConfirmed
    lastClassicEvent := "رأس وكتفين"
else if doubleBottomConfirmed
    lastClassicEvent := "قاعان | اختراق العنق"
else if doubleTopConfirmed
    lastClassicEvent := "قمتان | كسر العنق"
else if qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout
    lastClassicEvent := majorChannelLowerFakeout ? "كسر وهمي قناة رئيسية" : "كسر وهمي قناة"
else if qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout
    lastClassicEvent := majorChannelUpperFakeout ? "اختراق وهمي قناة رئيسية" : "اختراق وهمي قناة"
else if bullishDemandRetest
    lastClassicEvent := "اختبار طلب"
else if bearishSupplyRetest
    lastClassicEvent := "اختبار عرض"
else if bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline
    lastClassicEvent := "اختراق نطاق"
else if bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance
    lastClassicEvent := "كسر نطاق"
else if bullishCandleConfirmation or bearishCandleConfirmation
    lastClassicEvent := candleState
else if bullishRetest
    lastClassicEvent := "إعادة اختبار صاعد"
else if bearishRetest
    lastClassicEvent := "إعادة اختبار هابط"
else if bullishBreak
    lastClassicEvent := "اختراق مقاومة"
else if bearishBreak
    lastClassicEvent := "كسر دعم"

bool classicDescriptionUpdatedNow = accumulationSosEvent or distributionSowEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or (generalEventCode != 0 and generalEventCode != 1)
if (wyckoffEventNow or bullishWyckoffTest or bearishWyckoffTest or bullishSweep or bearishSweep or bullishBreakFailed or bearishBreakFailed)
    lastWyckoffEventBar := bar_index
if classicDescriptionUpdatedNow
    lastClassicEventBar := bar_index
int barsSinceLastWyckoffDescription = na(lastWyckoffEventBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - lastWyckoffEventBar
int barsSinceLastClassicDescription = na(lastClassicEventBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - lastClassicEventBar
string activeWyckoffEventDescription = barsSinceLastWyckoffDescription <= contextLookback ? lastWyckoffEvent + " | " + f_bar_age(barsSinceLastWyckoffDescription) : "لا يوجد حدث وايكوف حديث داخل النموذج"
string activeClassicEventDescription = barsSinceLastClassicDescription <= contextLookback ? lastClassicEvent + " | " + f_bar_age(barsSinceLastClassicDescription) : "لا يوجد حدث كلاسيكي حديث"

// شروحات الأحداث العامة والشموع تُعرض في اللوحة الثابتة؛ لا توضع نصوص طويلة فوق السعر.

// =============================================================================
// Latest trade plan only
// =============================================================================
// A technical event is snapshotted before all optional participation filters.
// A plan still requires the original qualifiers or a later confirmed follow-through.
type SetupCandidateState
    int dir = 0
    int eventBar = na
    float ref = na
    float rng = na
    float stop = na
    int stopKind = 0
    bool usesGuard = false
    float guardLevel = na
    string source = "-"
    bool directBreak = false
    bool needsQualifiers = false
    bool needsFvg = false
    bool qualified = false
    bool reversalEligible = false
    float tolerance = na

var SetupCandidateState setupCandidateState = SetupCandidateState.new()

f_clear_setup_candidate(SetupCandidateState candidate) =>
    candidate.dir := 0
    candidate.eventBar := na
    candidate.ref := na
    candidate.rng := na
    candidate.stop := na
    candidate.stopKind := 0
    candidate.usesGuard := false
    candidate.guardLevel := na
    candidate.source := "-"
    candidate.directBreak := false
    candidate.needsQualifiers := false
    candidate.needsFvg := false
    candidate.qualified := false
    candidate.reversalEligible := false
    candidate.tolerance := na
    candidate

f_set_setup_candidate(SetupCandidateState candidate, int direction, float reference, float measuredRange, float stopAnchor, int stopKind, bool usesGuard, float guardLevel, string source, bool directBreak, bool needsQualifiers, bool needsFvg, bool qualified, bool reversalEligible, float tolerance) =>
    candidate.dir := direction
    candidate.eventBar := bar_index
    candidate.ref := reference
    candidate.rng := measuredRange
    candidate.stop := stopAnchor
    candidate.stopKind := stopKind
    candidate.usesGuard := usesGuard
    candidate.guardLevel := guardLevel
    candidate.source := source
    candidate.directBreak := directBreak
    candidate.needsQualifiers := needsQualifiers
    candidate.needsFvg := needsFvg
    candidate.qualified := qualified
    candidate.reversalEligible := reversalEligible
    candidate.tolerance := tolerance
    candidate

f_update_setup_candidate_state(SetupCandidateState candidate) =>
    int maximumBars = chartSeconds <= 5 * 60 ? math.max(retestWindow, 16) : chartSeconds <= 15 * 60 ? math.max(retestWindow, 12) : chartSeconds <= 60 * 60 ? math.max(retestWindow, 8) : math.max(retestWindow, 6)
    int storedCandidateMaximumBars = candidate.directBreak ? math.max(retestWindow, maximumBars) : maximumBars
    bool bullStructure = bullishBreak
    bool bearStructure = bearishBreak
    bool bullChannel = qualifiedChannelBreakUpEvent
    bool bearChannel = qualifiedChannelBreakDownEvent
    bool bullMajor = majorBreakoutUpEvent
    bool bearMajor = majorBreakoutDownEvent
    bool bullHorizontal = strongResistanceBreak
    bool bearHorizontal = strongSupportBreak
    bool bullPattern = inverseHeadShouldersConfirmed
    bool bearPattern = headShouldersConfirmed
    bool bullGenericBreak = bullStructure or bullChannel or bullMajor or bullHorizontal
    bool bearGenericBreak = bearStructure or bearChannel or bearMajor or bearHorizontal
    bool bullBreak = bullGenericBreak or bullPattern or accumulationSosEvent or bullishInsideBarBreakRaw or bullishRangeBreakRaw
    bool bearBreak = bearGenericBreak or bearPattern or distributionSowEvent or bearishInsideBarBreakRaw or bearishRangeBreakRaw
    bool bullRetestSetup = bullishRetest or bullishHorizontalRetestSetup or bullishDemandRetest or bullishBreakerRetest or accumulationSpringTestEvent or bullishWyckoffTest or accumulationLpsEvent or qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout or bearishBreakFailed
    bool bearRetestSetup = bearishSupplyRetest or bearishBreakerRetest or bearishHorizontalRetestSetup or bearishRetest or distributionUtadTestEvent or bearishWyckoffTest or distributionLpsyEvent or qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or bullishBreakFailed
    bool useBreak = entryMode != ENTRY_RETEST
    bool useRetest = entryMode != ENTRY_BREAK
    bool bullEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and ((useBreak and bullBreak) or (useRetest and bullRetestSetup))
    bool bearEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and ((useBreak and bearBreak) or (useRetest and bearRetestSetup))
    int eventDirection = bullEvent == bearEvent ? 0 : bullEvent ? 1 : -1

    float activeRetestTolerance = nz(candidate.tolerance, retestTolerance)
    bool expired = candidate.dir != 0 and not na(candidate.eventBar) and bar_index - candidate.eventBar > storedCandidateMaximumBars
    bool stopFailed = candidate.dir == 1 ? not na(candidate.stop) and barstate.isconfirmed and close < candidate.stop - breakBuffer : candidate.dir == -1 ? not na(candidate.stop) and barstate.isconfirmed and close > candidate.stop + breakBuffer : false
    bool breakFailed = candidate.directBreak and not na(candidate.ref) and barstate.isconfirmed and (candidate.dir == 1 ? close < candidate.ref - activeRetestTolerance * 1.5 : close > candidate.ref + activeRetestTolerance * 1.5)
    if barstate.isconfirmed and (expired or stopFailed or breakFailed)
        f_clear_setup_candidate(candidate)

    bool storedBullRetest = candidate.dir == 1 and candidate.directBreak and not na(candidate.ref) and low <= candidate.ref + activeRetestTolerance * 1.5 and close >= candidate.ref - activeRetestTolerance and close > open and closeLocation >= 0.55 and vsaSignal != -1
    bool storedBearRetest = candidate.dir == -1 and candidate.directBreak and not na(candidate.ref) and high >= candidate.ref - activeRetestTolerance * 1.5 and close <= candidate.ref + activeRetestTolerance and close < open and closeLocation <= 0.45 and vsaSignal != 1
    if barstate.isconfirmed and (storedBullRetest or storedBearRetest)
        candidate.directBreak := false
        candidate.needsQualifiers := false
        candidate.needsFvg := false
        candidate.qualified := true
        candidate.reversalEligible := true
        candidate.eventBar := bar_index
        candidate.stop := candidate.dir == 1 ? math.min(nz(candidate.stop, low), low) : math.max(nz(candidate.stop, high), high)
        candidate.stopKind := 2
        candidate.source := candidate.source + " | إعادة اختبار لاحقة"

    if barstate.isconfirmed and eventDirection != 0
        bool isLong = eventDirection == 1
        bool directNow = useBreak and (isLong ? bullBreak : bearBreak)
        bool needsParticipation = (isLong ? bullGenericBreak : bearGenericBreak) or (isLong ? bullPattern : bearPattern) or (isLong ? bullishInsideBarBreakRaw or bullishRangeBreakRaw : bearishInsideBarBreakRaw or bearishRangeBreakRaw) or directNow and useFvgFilter
        bool needsFvg = directNow and useFvgFilter
        bool qualifiedNow = isLong ? accumulationSosEvent and fvgBullPass or ((bullGenericBreak or bullPattern) and bullishDirectBreakQuality and volumePass and fvgBullPass) or bullishInsideBarBreakout and fvgBullPass or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline and fvgBullPass : distributionSowEvent and fvgBearPass or ((bearGenericBreak or bearPattern) and bearishDirectBreakQuality and volumePass and fvgBearPass) or bearishInsideBarBreakout and fvgBearPass or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance and fvgBearPass
        bool reversalNow = isLong ? bullishChoch or bullMajor or bullChannel or bullHorizontal or bullPattern or bullishRangeBreakRaw or accumulationSosEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or bullishWyckoffTest or accumulationLpsEvent or bullishRetest or bullishBreakerRetest or qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout or bearishBreakFailed : bearishChoch or bearMajor or bearChannel or bearHorizontal or bearPattern or bearishRangeBreakRaw or distributionSowEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent or bearishWyckoffTest or distributionLpsyEvent or bearishRetest or bearishBreakerRetest or qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or bullishBreakFailed
        float genericSwingRange = not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLow) ? math.abs(lastSwingHigh - lastSwingLow) : na
        float reference = isLong ? bullPattern ? ihsNecklineNow : bullishInsideBarBreakRaw ? high[2] : bullishRangeBreakRaw ? sidewaysRangeHigh : bullMajor ? majorChannelUpperNow : bullChannel ? channelUpperNow : bullHorizontal or bullishHorizontalRetestSetup ? strongResistanceLevel : bullishBreakerRetest ? bullishBreakerHigh : accumulationSosEvent or accumulationLpsEvent ? wyckoffRangeResistance : bullishDemandRetest ? demandZoneHigh : accumulationSpringTestEvent ? wyckoffRangeSupport : bullishWyckoffTest ? wyckoffTestLevel : bullishRetest ? confirmedRetestLevel : qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout ? effectiveChannelFakeoutLevel : bearishBreakFailed ? resolvedBreakEventLevel : nz(lastSwingHigh, close) : bearPattern ? hsNecklineNow : bearishInsideBarBreakRaw ? low[2] : bearishRangeBreakRaw ? sidewaysRangeLow : bearMajor ? majorChannelLowerNow : bearChannel ? channelLowerNow : bearHorizontal or bearishHorizontalRetestSetup ? strongSupportLevel : bearishBreakerRetest ? bearishBreakerLow : distributionSowEvent or distributionLpsyEvent ? wyckoffRangeSupport : bearishSupplyRetest ? supplyZoneLow : distributionUtadTestEvent ? wyckoffRangeResistance : bearishWyckoffTest ? wyckoffTestLevel : bearishRetest ? confirmedRetestLevel : qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout ? effectiveChannelFakeoutLevel : bullishBreakFailed ? resolvedBreakEventLevel : nz(lastSwingLow, close)
        float measuredRange = bullPattern ? inverseHeadShouldersHeight : bearPattern ? headShouldersHeight : bullishInsideBarBreakRaw or bearishInsideBarBreakRaw ? math.abs(high[2] - low[2]) : bullishRangeBreakRaw or bearishRangeBreakRaw ? math.abs(sidewaysRangeHigh - sidewaysRangeLow) : bullMajor or bearMajor ? majorChannelWidth : bullChannel or bearChannel ? channelWidth : bullishBreakerRetest ? math.abs(bullishBreakerHigh - bullishBreakerLow) : bearishBreakerRetest ? math.abs(bearishBreakerHigh - bearishBreakerLow) : accumulationSosEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or distributionSowEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent ? math.abs(nz(wyckoffRangeResistance, close) - nz(wyckoffRangeSupport, close)) : genericSwingRange
        float fallbackDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 1.5, syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
        float baseContextStop = ((isLong) ? ((bullishDemandRetest) and (demandZoneActive) ? (demandZoneLow) : (accumulationLpsEvent) ? math.min((pivotLow), nz((wyckoffRangeResistance), (pivotLow))) : (accumulationSpringTestEvent) and not na((wyckoffPhaseCTestExtreme)) ? (wyckoffPhaseCTestExtreme) : (bullishWyckoffTest) and not na((wyckoffTestExtreme)) ? (wyckoffTestExtreme) : (bullishRetest) and not na((confirmedRetestLevel)) ? math.min((low), (confirmedRetestLevel)) : nz((lastSwingLow), ((isLong) ? close - fallbackDistance : close + fallbackDistance))) : ((bearishSupplyRetest) and (supplyZoneActive) ? (supplyZoneHigh) : (distributionLpsyEvent) ? math.max((pivotHigh), nz((wyckoffRangeSupport), (pivotHigh))) : (distributionUtadTestEvent) and not na((wyckoffPhaseCTestExtreme)) ? (wyckoffPhaseCTestExtreme) : (bearishWyckoffTest) and not na((wyckoffTestExtreme)) ? (wyckoffTestExtreme) : (bearishRetest) and not na((confirmedRetestLevel)) ? math.max((high), (confirmedRetestLevel)) : nz((lastSwingHigh), ((isLong) ? close - fallbackDistance : close + fallbackDistance))))
        float contextStop = isLong and bullishBreakerRetest ? bullishBreakerLow : not isLong and bearishBreakerRetest ? bearishBreakerHigh : baseContextStop
        bool protectedStop = isLong ? bullishDemandRetest or bullishBreakerRetest or accumulationLpsEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or bullishWyckoffTest or bullishRetest : bearishSupplyRetest or bearishBreakerRetest or distributionLpsyEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent or bearishWyckoffTest or bearishRetest
        float specificStop = isLong ? bullPattern ? ihsRightShoulder : qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout or bearishBreakFailed ? low : bullishInsideBarBreakRaw ? low[2] : bullishRangeBreakRaw ? sidewaysRangeLow : bullMajor ? math.min(low, majorChannelUpperNow) : bullChannel ? math.min(low, channelUpperNow) : bullHorizontal or bullishHorizontalRetestSetup ? math.min(low, strongResistanceLow) : accumulationSosEvent ? math.min(low, wyckoffRangeResistance) : bullStructure ? math.min(low, lastSwingHigh) : na : bearPattern ? hsRightShoulder : qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or bullishBreakFailed ? high : bearishInsideBarBreakRaw ? high[2] : bearishRangeBreakRaw ? sidewaysRangeHigh : bearMajor ? math.max(high, majorChannelLowerNow) : bearChannel ? math.max(high, channelLowerNow) : bearHorizontal or bearishHorizontalRetestSetup ? math.max(high, strongSupportHigh) : distributionSowEvent ? math.max(high, wyckoffRangeSupport) : bearStructure ? math.max(high, lastSwingLow) : na
        float stopAnchor = protectedStop ? contextStop : not na(specificStop) ? specificStop : contextStop
        int stopKind = protectedStop ? 2 : not na(specificStop) ? 1 : 0
        string suffix = isLong ? bullishBreakerRetest ? " | بريكر صاعد" : qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout ? " | كسر وهمي قناة" : bearishBreakFailed ? " | كسر وهمي" : bullMajor ? " | اختراق قناة رئيسية" : bullChannel ? " | اختراق قناة" : bullHorizontal ? " | اختراق مقاومة" : bullishHorizontalRetestSetup ? " | مقاومة أصبحت دعم" : "" : bearishBreakerRetest ? " | بريكر هابط" : qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout ? " | اختراق وهمي قناة" : bullishBreakFailed ? " | اختراق وهمي" : bearMajor ? " | كسر قناة رئيسية" : bearChannel ? " | كسر قناة" : bearHorizontal ? " | كسر دعم" : bearishHorizontalRetestSetup ? " | دعم أصبح مقاومة" : ""
        string source = bullPattern ? "رأس وكتفين مقلوب" : bearPattern ? "رأس وكتفين" : ((isLong) ? ((bullishInsideBarBreakRaw) ? "انسايد بار" : (bullishRangeBreakRaw) ? "اختراق نطاق" : (accumulationLpsEvent) ? "آخر دعم (LPS)" : (bullishDemandRetest) ? "منطقة طلب" : (accumulationSpringTestEvent) ? "اختبار كسر وهمي" : (bullishWyckoffTest) ? "اختبار سحب سيولة" : (bullishRetest) ? "إعادة اختبار كسر" : (doubleBottomConfirmed) ? "قاعان" : (accumulationSosEvent) ? "قوة (SOS)" : (bullishChoch) ? "تغير صاعد (CHoCH)" : "كسر صاعد (BOS)") : ((bearishInsideBarBreakRaw) ? "انسايد بار" : (bearishRangeBreakRaw) ? "كسر نطاق" : (distributionLpsyEvent) ? "آخر عرض (LPSY)" : (bearishSupplyRetest) ? "منطقة عرض" : (distributionUtadTestEvent) ? "اختبار اختراق وهمي" : (bearishWyckoffTest) ? "اختبار سحب سيولة" : (bearishRetest) ? "إعادة اختبار كسر" : (doubleTopConfirmed) ? "قمتان" : (distributionSowEvent) ? "ضعف (SOW)" : (bearishChoch) ? "تغير هابط (CHoCH)" : "كسر هابط (BOS)")) + suffix
        bool preserveQualified = candidate.dir != 0 and candidate.qualified and (candidate.dir == eventDirection or candidate.dir != eventDirection and needsParticipation and not qualifiedNow)
        if not preserveQualified or not needsParticipation or qualifiedNow
            bool usesGuard = isLong ? bullStructure or bullishRetest : bearStructure or bearishRetest
            float guardLevel = isLong ? bullStructure ? lastSwingHigh : bullishRetest ? confirmedRetestLevel : na : bearStructure ? lastSwingLow : bearishRetest ? confirmedRetestLevel : na
            f_set_setup_candidate(candidate, eventDirection, reference, measuredRange, stopAnchor, stopKind, usesGuard, guardLevel, source, directNow, needsParticipation, needsFvg, (candidate.dir == eventDirection and candidate.qualified) or qualifiedNow, (candidate.dir == eventDirection and candidate.reversalEligible) or reversalNow, retestTolerance)
            activeRetestTolerance := nz(candidate.tolerance, retestTolerance)
    else if barstate.isconfirmed and bullEvent and bearEvent and candidate.dir == 0
        candidate.source := "تعارض إعداد شراء وبيع على الشمعة نفسها"

    int activeMaximumBars = candidate.directBreak ? math.max(retestWindow, maximumBars) : maximumBars
    bool withinWindow = candidate.dir != 0 and not na(candidate.eventBar) and bar_index - candidate.eventBar <= activeMaximumBars
    bool stopHolds = candidate.dir == 1 ? na(candidate.stop) or close >= candidate.stop - breakBuffer : candidate.dir == -1 ? na(candidate.stop) or close <= candidate.stop + breakBuffer : false
    bool breakHolds = not candidate.directBreak or na(candidate.ref) or (candidate.dir == 1 ? close >= candidate.ref - activeRetestTolerance * 1.5 : close <= candidate.ref + activeRetestTolerance * 1.5)
    bool fvgFilterPass = not candidate.needsFvg or (candidate.dir == 1 ? fvgBullPass : candidate.dir == -1 ? fvgBearPass : false)
    if barstate.isconfirmed and breakHolds and (candidate.directBreak ? (candidate.dir == 1 ? bullishDirectBreakQuality : candidate.dir == -1 ? bearishDirectBreakQuality : false) : (candidate.dir == 1 ? bullishBreakCandleQuality : candidate.dir == -1 ? bearishBreakCandleQuality : false)) and (not candidate.needsQualifiers or volumePass) and fvgFilterPass
        candidate.qualified := true
    bool entryQualified = not candidate.needsQualifiers or candidate.qualified
    bool active = withinWindow and stopHolds and breakHolds
    float entryReferenceRange = math.max(nz(candidate.rng, safeAtr * 2.0), syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    float directMaximumExtension = useAdaptiveEntryQuality ? math.min(safeAtr * 1.25, math.max(safeAtr * 0.60, entryReferenceRange * 0.25)) : math.min(safeAtr * 1.75, math.max(safeAtr * 0.85, entryReferenceRange * 0.30))
    float retestMaximumDistance = useAdaptiveEntryQuality ? math.min(safeAtr, math.max(safeAtr * 0.35, entryReferenceRange * 0.18)) : math.min(safeAtr * 1.25, math.max(safeAtr * 0.50, entryReferenceRange * 0.20))
    bool minimumBreakLevelPass = not candidate.directBreak or na(candidate.ref) or (candidate.dir == 1 ? close >= candidate.ref - activeRetestTolerance : close <= candidate.ref + activeRetestTolerance)
    bool extensionPass = not preventExtendedEntries or na(candidate.ref) or (candidate.directBreak ? (candidate.dir == 1 ? close <= candidate.ref + directMaximumExtension : close >= candidate.ref - directMaximumExtension) : math.abs(close - candidate.ref) <= retestMaximumDistance)
    bool entryLevelPass = minimumBreakLevelPass and extensionPass
    bool directQualityPass = not candidate.directBreak or (candidate.dir == 1 ? bullishDirectBreakQuality : candidate.dir == -1 ? bearishDirectBreakQuality : false)
    int localGateCode = not entryQualified ? 1 : not entryLevelPass ? 2 : not directQualityPass ? 3 : 0
    [activeMaximumBars, active, (stockChartAllowed and entrySessionPass and active and entryQualified and entryLevelPass and directQualityPass and candidate.dir == 1 and vwapLongPass), (stockChartAllowed and entrySessionPass and active and entryQualified and entryLevelPass and directQualityPass and candidate.dir == -1 and vwapShortPass), localGateCode]
[setupCandidateMaximumBars, setupCandidateActive, actionableLongSetup, actionableShortSetup, setupCandidateLocalGateCode] = f_update_setup_candidate_state(setupCandidateState)
int setupCandidateDirection = setupCandidateState.dir
int setupCandidateBar = setupCandidateState.eventBar
float setupCandidateReference = setupCandidateState.ref
float setupCandidateRange = setupCandidateState.rng
float setupCandidateStopAnchor = setupCandidateState.stop
int setupCandidateStopKind = setupCandidateState.stopKind
bool setupCandidateUsesGuard = setupCandidateState.usesGuard
float setupCandidateGuardLevel = setupCandidateState.guardLevel
string setupCandidateSource = setupCandidateState.source
bool setupCandidateIsDirectBreak = setupCandidateState.directBreak
bool setupCandidateReversalEligible = setupCandidateState.reversalEligible
// ICT premium/discount is tied to the ordered, confirmed swing leg. It is a
// location preference for retests only, never an independent breakout trigger.
[dealingRangeEvidenceReady, dealingRangeSignal, dealingRangeState] = f_dealing_range_context(usePremiumDiscountContext, setupCandidateActive and not setupCandidateIsDirectBreak, swingLevelsCompatible, lastSwingHigh, lastSwingLow, lastSwingHighBar, lastSwingLowBar, close)
[fibRetracementReady, fibBullishLeg, fibBearishLeg, fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, fibRetracement1000, fibRetracementState] = f_fib_retracement_context(useFibRetracement, swingLevelsCompatible, lastSwingHigh, lastSwingLow, lastSwingHighBar, lastSwingLowBar, close)
float fibStopMinimumDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.35, syminfo.mintick * 2.0)
float fibTargetMinimumDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.25, syminfo.mintick * 4.0)
array<float> fibRetracementLevels = array.from(fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, fibRetracement1000)
float nearestFibSupportForStop = f_nearest_price_level(false, close, fibStopMinimumDistance, fibRetracementLevels)
float nearestFibResistanceForStop = f_nearest_price_level(true, close, fibStopMinimumDistance, fibRetracementLevels)
float nearestFibSupportForTarget = f_nearest_price_level(false, close, fibTargetMinimumDistance, fibRetracementLevels)
float nearestFibResistanceForTarget = f_nearest_price_level(true, close, fibTargetMinimumDistance, fibRetracementLevels)

// =============================================================================
// التوافق مقسم إلى مجموعات مستقلة قدر الإمكان، ويستخدم مقاما ديناميكيا حسب
// الأدوات المتاحة والمفعلة بدلا من عد الحدث السعري نفسه عدة مرات.
f_calculate_evidence_aggregates() =>
    bool swingStructureReady = not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLow) and swingLevelsCompatible
    int structureEvidenceMax = swingStructureReady ? 1 : 0
    int bullishStructureEvidence = swingStructureReady and resolvedStructureBias == 1 ? 1 : 0
    int bearishStructureEvidence = swingStructureReady and resolvedStructureBias == -1 ? 1 : 0

    // الحجم وVSA مجموعة مشاركة واحدة لأنهما يقرآن جهد الشمعة نفسه.
    bool directVolumeEvidenceEnabled = useVolumeFilter and setupCandidateActive and setupCandidateIsDirectBreak and volumeAvailable
    bool vsaEvidenceEnabled = useVsaAnalysis and volumeAvailable
    int participationEvidenceMax = directVolumeEvidenceEnabled or vsaEvidenceEnabled ? 1 : 0
    int bullishParticipationEvidence = (directVolumeEvidenceEnabled and standardVolumeEffort and closeLocation >= 0.55) or (vsaEvidenceEnabled and vsaSignal == 1) ? 1 : 0
    int bearishParticipationEvidence = (directVolumeEvidenceEnabled and standardVolumeEffort and closeLocation <= 0.45) or (vsaEvidenceEnabled and vsaSignal == -1) ? 1 : 0

    float evidenceVwapValue = useVwapFilter ? entryVwapValue : useStructuralVwap and structuralVwapReady ? structuralVwapValue : na
    bool vwapEvidenceReady = not na(evidenceVwapValue)
    bool zoneEvidenceReady = demandZoneEvidenceRelevant or supplyZoneEvidenceRelevant or bullishBreakerEvidenceRelevant or bearishBreakerEvidenceRelevant
    bool horizontalSrEvidenceReady = horizontalSrSignal != 0
    int locationEvidenceMax = math.min(3, (channelEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (vwapEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (zoneEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (horizontalSrEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (dealingRangeEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0))
    int bullishLocationEvidence = math.min(locationEvidenceMax, (channelEvidenceReady and effectiveChannelSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (vwapEvidenceReady and close > evidenceVwapValue ? 1 : 0) + (zoneEvidenceReady and zoneSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (horizontalSrEvidenceReady and horizontalSrSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (dealingRangeEvidenceReady and dealingRangeSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0))
    int bearishLocationEvidence = math.min(locationEvidenceMax, (channelEvidenceReady and effectiveChannelSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (vwapEvidenceReady and close < evidenceVwapValue ? 1 : 0) + (zoneEvidenceReady and zoneSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (horizontalSrEvidenceReady and horizontalSrSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (dealingRangeEvidenceReady and dealingRangeSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0))

    int barsSinceBullishPriceAction = ta.barssince(bullishInsideBarBreakout or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline)
    int barsSinceBearishPriceAction = ta.barssince(bearishInsideBarBreakout or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance)
    bool recentBullishPriceAction = not na(barsSinceBullishPriceAction) and barsSinceBullishPriceAction <= contextLookback
    bool recentBearishPriceAction = not na(barsSinceBearishPriceAction) and barsSinceBearishPriceAction <= contextLookback
    // المرشح النشط هو زناد الخطة الأساسي. الأدلة السلوكية الأخرى تؤكد جودته
    // ولا تجعل BOS الصالح يبدأ من صفر في مجموعة الزناد.
    int triggerEvidenceMax = math.min(2, 1 + (useCandleConfirmation ? 1 : 0) + (usePriceActionBreakouts ? 1 : 0) + (useRsiDivergence ? 1 : 0))
    int bullishTriggerEvidence = math.min(triggerEvidenceMax, (setupCandidateActive and setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (recentBullishAuctionTrigger ? 1 : 0) + (useCandleConfirmation and recentBullishCandle ? 1 : 0) + (usePriceActionBreakouts and recentBullishPriceAction ? 1 : 0) + (useRsiDivergence and recentBullishDivergence ? 1 : 0))
    int bearishTriggerEvidence = math.min(triggerEvidenceMax, (setupCandidateActive and setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (recentBearishAuctionTrigger ? 1 : 0) + (useCandleConfirmation and recentBearishCandle ? 1 : 0) + (usePriceActionBreakouts and recentBearishPriceAction ? 1 : 0) + (useRsiDivergence and recentBearishDivergence ? 1 : 0))

    int evidenceMaximum = math.max(1, structureEvidenceMax + participationEvidenceMax + locationEvidenceMax + triggerEvidenceMax)
    int bullishEvidence = bullishStructureEvidence + bullishParticipationEvidence + bullishLocationEvidence + bullishTriggerEvidence
    int bearishEvidence = bearishStructureEvidence + bearishParticipationEvidence + bearishLocationEvidence + bearishTriggerEvidence
    [evidenceMaximum, bullishEvidence, bearishEvidence, 100.0 * float(bullishEvidence) / float(evidenceMaximum), 100.0 * float(bearishEvidence) / float(evidenceMaximum)]

[evidenceMaximum, bullishEvidence, bearishEvidence, bullishEvidencePercent, bearishEvidencePercent] = f_calculate_evidence_aggregates()
// Preliminary opportunity layer. It may update while the realtime candle forms,
// but it never creates an entry, stop, targets, or trade alert. Confirmed setup
// candidates above are close-based and persist independently.
f_calculate_formation_watch() =>
    int formationDirection = 0
    string formationSource = "لا توجد مراقبة أحادية الاتجاه"
    float formationEvidenceMinimum = math.max(25.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent - 30.0)
    float formationProximity = safeAtr * (chartSeconds <= 15 * 60 ? 0.35 : chartSeconds <= 60 * 60 ? 0.45 : 0.60)
    float bullishWatchLevel = f_nearest_price_level(true, close, syminfo.mintick, array.from(not highWasBroken ? lastSwingHigh : na, strongResistanceReady and not strongResistanceRoleReversed ? strongResistanceHigh : na, channelReady ? channelUpperNow : na, majorChannelReady ? majorChannelUpperNow : na, bullishInsideBarSetup ? high[1] : na, sidewaysRangeReady and precedingDownContext ? sidewaysRangeHigh : na, wyckoffMode == 1 ? wyckoffRangeResistance : na))
    float bearishWatchLevel = f_nearest_price_level(false, close, syminfo.mintick, array.from(not lowWasBroken ? lastSwingLow : na, strongSupportReady and not strongSupportRoleReversed ? strongSupportLow : na, channelReady ? channelLowerNow : na, majorChannelReady ? majorChannelLowerNow : na, bearishInsideBarSetup ? low[1] : na, sidewaysRangeReady and precedingUpContext ? sidewaysRangeLow : na, wyckoffMode == -1 ? wyckoffRangeSupport : na))
    bool bullishApproachWatch = not na(bullishWatchLevel) and bullishWatchLevel - close <= formationProximity and high >= bullishWatchLevel - formationProximity
    bool bearishApproachWatch = not na(bearishWatchLevel) and close - bearishWatchLevel <= formationProximity and low <= bearishWatchLevel + formationProximity
    bool bullishRetestWatch = (watchAfterBreak and priorWatchedBreakDirection == 1 and low <= priorWatchedBreakLevel + retestTolerance * 1.5 and close >= priorWatchedBreakLevel - retestTolerance) or (demandZoneActive and low <= demandZoneHigh + retestTolerance and close >= demandZoneLow) or (bullishBreakerActive and low <= bullishBreakerHigh + retestTolerance and close >= bullishBreakerLow) or resistanceClusterActsAsSupport
    bool bearishRetestWatch = (watchAfterBreak and priorWatchedBreakDirection == -1 and high >= priorWatchedBreakLevel - retestTolerance * 1.5 and close <= priorWatchedBreakLevel + retestTolerance) or (supplyZoneActive and high >= supplyZoneLow - retestTolerance and close <= supplyZoneHigh) or (bearishBreakerActive and high >= bearishBreakerLow - retestTolerance and close <= bearishBreakerHigh) or supportClusterActsAsResistance
    bool bullishFormationContext = bullishInsideBarSetup or (sidewaysRangeReady and precedingDownContext) or useCandleConfirmation and bullishReversalPatternRaw or inverseHeadShouldersReady or accumulationSpringEvent or bullishSweep
    bool bearishFormationContext = bearishInsideBarSetup or (sidewaysRangeReady and precedingUpContext) or useCandleConfirmation and bearishReversalPatternRaw or headShouldersReady or distributionUtadEvent or bearishSweep
    bool bullishFormationRaw = stockChartAllowed and (bullishApproachWatch or bullishRetestWatch or bullishFormationContext) and bullishEvidencePercent >= formationEvidenceMinimum and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence
    bool bearishFormationRaw = stockChartAllowed and (bearishApproachWatch or bearishRetestWatch or bearishFormationContext) and bearishEvidencePercent >= formationEvidenceMinimum and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence
    formationDirection := bullishFormationRaw and not bearishFormationRaw ? 1 : bearishFormationRaw and not bullishFormationRaw ? -1 : 0
    formationSource := formationDirection == 1 ? (bullishRetestWatch ? "إعادة اختبار أو طلب" : inverseHeadShouldersReady ? "رأس وكتفين مقلوب" : bullishInsideBarSetup ? "انسايد بار" : sidewaysRangeReady and precedingDownContext ? "نطاق بعد هبوط" : useCandleConfirmation and bullishReversalPatternRaw ? "شمعة انعكاس صاعد" : accumulationSpringEvent or bullishSweep ? "سحب سيولة" : "قرب اختراق") : formationDirection == -1 ? (bearishRetestWatch ? "إعادة اختبار أو عرض" : headShouldersReady ? "رأس وكتفين" : bearishInsideBarSetup ? "انسايد بار" : sidewaysRangeReady and precedingUpContext ? "نطاق بعد صعود" : useCandleConfirmation and bearishReversalPatternRaw ? "شمعة انعكاس هابط" : distributionUtadEvent or bearishSweep ? "سحب سيولة" : "قرب كسر") : "لا مراقبة"

    [formationDirection, formationSource]

[formationDirection, formationSource] = f_calculate_formation_watch()
int currentOpinionScore = resolvedStructureBias * 3 + int(math.sign(float(bullishEvidence - bearishEvidence))) + effectiveChannelSignal
int linkedOpinionScore = currentOpinionScore * 3 + higherWeightedScore
int linkedOpinionBias = linkedOpinionScore >= 4 ? 1 : linkedOpinionScore <= -4 ? -1 : 0
int combinedTechnicalOpinionCode = not swingLevelsCompatible ? 1 : structureDirectionConflict ? 2 : channelLayerConflict ? 9 : linkedOpinionBias == 1 and linkedOpinionScore >= 10 and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence + 2 ? 3 : linkedOpinionBias == -1 and linkedOpinionScore <= -10 and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence + 2 ? 4 : linkedOpinionBias == 1 and higherWeightedScore < 0 ? 5 : linkedOpinionBias == -1 and higherWeightedScore > 0 ? 6 : linkedOpinionBias == 1 ? 7 : linkedOpinionBias == -1 ? 8 : 0
string combinedTechnicalOpinion = combinedTechnicalOpinionCode == 0 ? technicalOpinion : f_text_code(TXT_COMBINED_OPINION, combinedTechnicalOpinionCode)
string linkedOpinionClass = linkedOpinionBias == 1 ? (higherWeightedScore < 0 ? "صاعد انعكاسي" : higherWeightedScore > 0 ? "صاعد متوافق" : "صاعد محلي") : linkedOpinionBias == -1 ? (higherWeightedScore > 0 ? "هابط انعكاسي" : higherWeightedScore < 0 ? "هابط متوافق" : "هابط محلي") : "محايد/انتظار"
bool bullishCandleIsLatest = recentBullishCandle and (not recentBearishCandle or barsSinceBullishCandle <= barsSinceBearishCandle)
bool bullishDivergenceIsLatest = recentBullishDivergence and (not recentBearishDivergence or barsSinceBullishDivergence <= barsSinceBearishDivergence)
string activeCandleState = bullishCandleIsLatest ? "انعكاس شموعي صاعد " + f_bar_age(barsSinceBullishCandle) : recentBearishCandle ? "انعكاس شموعي هابط " + f_bar_age(barsSinceBearishCandle) : "لا يوجد نموذج شموعي حديث"
string activeDivergenceState = bullishDivergenceIsLatest ? "دايفرجنس RSI صاعد " + f_bar_age(barsSinceBullishDivergence) : recentBearishDivergence ? "دايفرجنس RSI هابط " + f_bar_age(barsSinceBearishDivergence) : "لا يوجد دايفرجنس حديث"
string behaviorState = "آخر شمعة مغلقة: " + currentCandleDescription + "\n" + activeCandleState + " | " + activeDivergenceState + "\n" + priceActionState
string higherContextState = higherTimeframe1 + ":" + f_bias_text(higherTrendRaw1) + " | " + higherTimeframe2 + ":" + f_bias_text(higherTrendRaw2) + (higherTrendRaw3 == 9 ? "" : " | " + higherTimeframe3 + ":" + f_bias_text(higherTrendRaw3)) + (higherTimeframeConflict ? " | تعارض" : "") + " | الرأي " + linkedOpinionClass + " | شراء " + str.tostring(bullishEvidence) + "/" + str.tostring(evidenceMaximum) + " مقابل بيع " + str.tostring(bearishEvidence) + "/" + str.tostring(evidenceMaximum)

var int closedStructureBias = 0
var bool closedStructureConflict = false
var int closedDowBias = 0
var string closedDowState = "عرضي أو انتقالي"
var string closedWyckoffEvent = "لا يوجد"
var string closedWyckoffPhaseState = "لا يوجد تسلسل وايكوف مكتمل"
var string closedWyckoffTrendContextState = "لا يوجد اتجاه سابق ممتد ومؤهل"
var string closedWyckoffRangeState = "حدود نطاق وايكوف غير مكتملة"
var int closedVsaSignal = 0
var string closedVsaState = "لا إشارة حجم/سلوك قوية"
var string closedVolumeCandleLevelsState = "لا توجد منطقة شمعة حجم مهمة"
var string closedClassicEvent = "لا يوجد"
var int closedZoneSignal = 0
var string closedZoneState = "لا توجد منطقة مؤكدة"
var int closedHorizontalSrSignal = 0
var string closedHorizontalSrState = "لا يوجد مستوى أفقي متعدد اللمسات"
var string closedBehaviorState = "لا يوجد تأكيد سلوكي"
var string closedTechnicalPatternState = "لا نموذج فني"
var int closedHigherTrendBias = 0
var string closedHigherContextState = "الفواصل الأعلى غير جاهزة"
var int closedMajorChannelSignal = 0
var string closedMajorChannelState = "الرئيسي: غير مكتمل"
var int closedChannelSignal = 0
var string closedChannelState = "غير مكتملة"
var string closedTechnicalOpinion = "محايد"
var bool closedVolumeAvailable = false
var float closedRelativeVolume = na
var bool closedRecentBullFvg = false
var bool closedRecentBearFvg = false
var string closedVwapLabel = "دوري"
var string closedDealingRangeState = "سياق العلاوة/الخصم غير جاهز"
var string closedFibRetracementState = "تصحيح فيبوناتشي غير جاهز"
var string closedMomentumContextState = "سياق الزخم غير جاهز"

if barstate.isconfirmed
    closedStructureBias := resolvedStructureBias
    closedStructureConflict := structureDirectionConflict
    closedDowBias := dowBias
    closedDowState := dowState
    closedWyckoffEvent := wyckoffMode == 0 ? "لا يوجد حدث وايكوف حديث" : activeWyckoffEventDescription
    closedWyckoffPhaseState := wyckoffPhaseState
    closedWyckoffTrendContextState := wyckoffTrendContextState
    closedWyckoffRangeState := wyckoffRangeState
    closedVsaSignal := vsaSignal
    closedVsaState := vsaState
    closedVolumeCandleLevelsState := volumeCandleLevelsState
    closedClassicEvent := activeClassicEventDescription
    closedZoneSignal := zoneSignal
    closedZoneState := zoneState
    closedHorizontalSrSignal := horizontalSrSignal
    closedHorizontalSrState := horizontalSrState
    closedBehaviorState := behaviorState
    closedTechnicalPatternState := technicalPatternState
    closedHigherTrendBias := higherWeightedScore > 0 ? 1 : higherWeightedScore < 0 ? -1 : 0
    closedHigherContextState := higherContextState
    closedMajorChannelSignal := majorChannelSignal
    closedMajorChannelState := majorChannelState
    closedChannelSignal := channelSignal
    closedChannelState := channelState
    closedTechnicalOpinion := combinedTechnicalOpinion
    closedVolumeAvailable := volumeAvailable
    closedRelativeVolume := relativeVolume
    closedRecentBullFvg := recentBullFvg
    closedRecentBearFvg := recentBearFvg
    closedVwapLabel := analysisVwapLabel
    closedDealingRangeState := dealingRangeState
    closedFibRetracementState := fibRetracementState
    closedMomentumContextState := momentumContextState

// حالة الخطة ثابتة بين المباشر وBar Replay وإعادة تشغيل الشارت. عند اجتماع
// الوقف والهدف داخل شمعة تاريخية واحدة يبقى الحكم محافظًا لأن OHLC لا يكشف الترتيب.
var int planDirection = 0
var bool planActive = false
var int planStartBar = na
var float planEntry = na
var float planStop = na
var float planTarget1 = na
var float planTarget2 = na
var float planTarget3 = na
var float planInvalidationLevel = na
var int planScore = 0
var int planScoreMax = 1
var bool target1Done = false
var bool target2Done = false
var bool target3Done = false
var bool planTrailingStopActive = false
var int planTrailingStopStartedBar = na
var int target1HitBar = na
var int target2HitBar = na
var int target3HitBar = na
