var float planHighSinceEntry = na
var float planLowSinceEntry = na
var bool planCounterTrend = false
var string planState = "لا توجد خطة"
var string planTradeText = "لا توجد صفقة"
var string planPriceContext = "-"
var string planSetupSource = "-"
var string planTargetHorizon = "-"
var bool planUsesStructureBreakGuard = false
var float planStructureBreakGuardLevel = na
var string lastPlanResult = "لا توجد نتيجة سابقة"
var int lastPlanExitBar = na
var int planOutcomeBar = na
var int planOutcomeKind = 0
var float planOutcomePrice = na

var line entryLine = na
var line stopLine = na
var line target1Line = na
var line target2Line = na
var line target3Line = na
var label entryLevelLabel = na
var label stopLevelLabel = na
var label target1LevelLabel = na
var label target2LevelLabel = na
var label target3LevelLabel = na
var label activePlanEntryMarker = na
var label activePlanTarget1Marker = na
var label activePlanTarget2Marker = na
var label activePlanTarget3Marker = na
// قيّم الخطة القائمة أولا. بهذه الأولوية لا تستطيع إشارة جديدة أن تمسح وقفا
// أو هدفا حدث على الشمعة نفسها. لمس الوقف له الأولوية المحافظة، ثم الإبطال، ثم الأهداف.
if planActive and bar_index > planStartBar
    planHighSinceEntry := na(planHighSinceEntry) ? high : math.max(planHighSinceEntry, high)
    planLowSinceEntry := na(planLowSinceEntry) ? low : math.min(planLowSinceEntry, low)

bool planStopTouchedNow = planActive and bar_index > planStartBar and (planDirection == 1 ? low <= planStop : high >= planStop)
bool planInvalidatedByClose = planActive and barstate.isconfirmed and bar_index > planStartBar and not na(planInvalidationLevel) and ((planDirection == 1 and close < planInvalidationLevel) or (planDirection == -1 and close > planInvalidationLevel))
bool planBreakFailedNow = planActive and planUsesStructureBreakGuard and barstate.isconfirmed and bar_index > planStartBar and not na(resolvedBreakEventLevel) and not na(planStructureBreakGuardLevel) and math.abs(resolvedBreakEventLevel - planStructureBreakGuardLevel) <= math.max(retestTolerance, syminfo.mintick * 2.0) and ((planDirection == 1 and bullishBreakFailed) or (planDirection == -1 and bearishBreakFailed))

if planActive and bar_index > planStartBar
    bool target1Touched = planDirection == 1 ? high >= planTarget1 : low <= planTarget1
    bool target2Touched = planDirection == 1 ? high >= planTarget2 : low <= planTarget2
    bool target3Touched = planDirection == 1 ? high >= planTarget3 : low <= planTarget3

    if planStopTouchedNow
        bool openedBeyondStop = planDirection == 1 ? open < planStop : open > planStop
        // إذا ظهر الوقف مع هدف غير مسجل داخل الشمعة نفسها فالأولوية المحافظة للوقف.
        bool unorderedTargetTouchedNow = (not target1Done and target1Touched) or (not target2Done and target2Touched) or (not target3Done and target3Touched)
        float executableStopPrice = openedBeyondStop ? open : planStop
        planActive := false
        string stopKindText = planTrailingStopActive ? "وقف حماية الأرباح المتحرك" : "الوقف"
        string stopBaseState = target2Done ? "تم ضرب " + stopKindText + " بعد تحقق الهدف 2" : target1Done ? "تم ضرب " + stopKindText + " بعد تحقق الهدف 1" : "تم ضرب " + stopKindText
        string stopGapState = openedBeyondStop ? " بفجوة؛ التنفيذ المحافظ عند الافتتاح" : ""
        string stopSequenceState = unorderedTargetTouchedNow ? "؛ ظهر الوقف مع هدف غير مسجل في الشمعة نفسها فكانت الأولوية المحافظة للوقف" : ""
        planState := stopBaseState + stopGapState + stopSequenceState
        lastPlanResult := planState
        lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeKind := 1
        planOutcomePrice := executableStopPrice
        f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 62, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 12, 2)
        if not target1Done
            f_close_plan_line(target1Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 82, 1)
        if not target2Done
            f_close_plan_line(target2Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 82, 1)
        if not target3Done
            f_close_plan_line(target3Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 82, 1)
    else if planInvalidatedByClose or planBreakFailedNow
        planActive := false
        planState := planInvalidatedByClose ? "ألغيت الخطة: إغلاق تجاوز نقطة الإبطال" : "ألغيت الخطة: الكسر أصبح وهميا"
        lastPlanResult := planState
        lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeKind := 2
        planOutcomePrice := close
        f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 78, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 78, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(target1Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 84, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(target2Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 84, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(target3Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 84, 1)
    else if target3Touched and not target3Done
        if not target1Done
            target1HitBar := bar_index
        if not target2Done
            target2HitBar := bar_index
        target1Done := true
        target2Done := true
        target3Done := true
        target3HitBar := bar_index
        planActive := false
        planState := "اكتملت الخطة عند الهدف 3"
        lastPlanResult := planState
        lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeKind := 3
        planOutcomePrice := planTarget3
        f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 62, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 78, 1)
        target1Line := f_clear_line(target1Line)
        target2Line := f_clear_line(target2Line)
        target3Line := f_clear_line(target3Line)
    else if target2Touched and not target2Done
        if not target1Done
            target1HitBar := bar_index
        target2HitBar := bar_index
        target1Done := true
        target2Done := true
        planState := "تحقق الهدف 2"
        lastPlanResult := planState
        target1Line := f_clear_line(target1Line)
        target2Line := f_clear_line(target2Line)
    else if target1Touched and not target1Done
        target1Done := true
        target1HitBar := bar_index
        planState := "تحقق الهدف 1"
        lastPlanResult := planState
        target1Line := f_clear_line(target1Line)

// Only after the confirmed close do we calculate the next bar's protected
// stop. This prevents the current high from raising a stop that is then tested
// against the current low when their true intrabar order is unknown.
if planActive and usePostTargetAtrTrail and target1Done and barstate.isconfirmed and postTargetTrailReady
    float rawTrailCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? planHighSinceEntry - postTargetTrailAtr * postTargetTrailMultiple : planLowSinceEntry + postTargetTrailAtr * postTargetTrailMultiple
    float breakEvenProtectedCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? math.max(rawTrailCandidate, planEntry) : math.min(rawTrailCandidate, planEntry)
    float priceValidCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? math.min(breakEvenProtectedCandidate, close - syminfo.mintick) : math.max(breakEvenProtectedCandidate, close + syminfo.mintick)
    float tickRoundedCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? f_floor_to_tick(priceValidCandidate) : f_ceil_to_tick(priceValidCandidate)
    if (planDirection == 1 ? tickRoundedCandidate >= planEntry : tickRoundedCandidate <= planEntry) and (planDirection == 1 ? tickRoundedCandidate > planStop + syminfo.mintick : tickRoundedCandidate < planStop - syminfo.mintick)
        planStop := tickRoundedCandidate
        planTrailingStopActive := true
        planTrailingStopStartedBar := na(planTrailingStopStartedBar) ? bar_index : planTrailingStopStartedBar
        planState := target2Done ? "تحقق الهدف 2 | وقف الأرباح المتحرك مفعّل" : "تحقق الهدف 1 | وقف الأرباح المتحرك مفعّل"
        lastPlanResult := planState
        if not na(stopLine)
            line.set_xy1(stopLine, bar_index, planStop)
            line.set_xy2(stopLine, bar_index + 1, planStop)
            line.set_extend(stopLine, extend.right)
            line.set_color(stopLine, color.new(#F23645, planStopLineTransparency))
            line.set_style(stopLine, line.style_dotted)
            line.set_width(stopLine, 1)

int setupCandidateAge = setupCandidateActive and not na(setupCandidateBar) ? bar_index - setupCandidateBar : 100000
bool freshRetestCandidate = allowEarlyQualifiedRetest and setupCandidateActive and not setupCandidateIsDirectBreak and setupCandidateReversalEligible and setupCandidateAge <= (chartSeconds < 24 * 60 * 60 ? 2 : 1)
bool bullishEarlyTriggerNow = freshRetestCandidate and setupCandidateDirection == 1 and (bullishWyckoffTest or accumulationSpringTestEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or bullishDemandRetest or bullishBreakerRetest or bullishHorizontalRetestSetup or bullishRetest or qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout or bearishBreakFailed)
bool bearishEarlyTriggerNow = freshRetestCandidate and setupCandidateDirection == -1 and (bearishWyckoffTest or distributionUtadTestEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or bearishSupplyRetest or bearishBreakerRetest or bearishHorizontalRetestSetup or bearishRetest or qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or bullishBreakFailed)
bool bullishEarlyCloseQuality = bullishEarlyTriggerNow and close > open and closeLocation >= 0.55 and vsaSignal != -1
bool bearishEarlyCloseQuality = bearishEarlyTriggerNow and close < open and closeLocation <= 0.45 and vsaSignal != 1
float entryClusterTolerance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.25, syminfo.mintick * 8.0)
float entryClusterReference = setupCandidateActive ? setupCandidateReference : na
float entryZoneLevel = setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? (setupCandidateIsDirectBreak ? supplyZoneLow : bullishBreakerActive and not na(entryClusterReference) and math.abs(entryClusterReference - bullishBreakerHigh) <= entryClusterTolerance ? bullishBreakerHigh : demandZoneHigh) : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? (setupCandidateIsDirectBreak ? demandZoneHigh : bearishBreakerActive and not na(entryClusterReference) and math.abs(entryClusterReference - bearishBreakerLow) <= entryClusterTolerance ? bearishBreakerLow : supplyZoneLow) : na
array<float> entryClusterLevels = array.from((setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? lastSwingHigh : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? lastSwingLow : na), (setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? strongResistanceLevel : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? strongSupportLevel : na), entryZoneLevel, (setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? wyckoffRangeResistance : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? wyckoffRangeSupport : na), (setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? channelUpperNow : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? channelLowerNow : na), (setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? majorChannelUpperNow : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? majorChannelLowerNow : na), (setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? nearestFibResistanceForTarget : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? nearestFibSupportForTarget : na), ema20, ema50, (setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? highVolumeResistanceLow : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? highVolumeSupportHigh : na))
int entryClusterCount = f_cluster_count(entryClusterReference, entryClusterTolerance, entryClusterLevels)
bool strongEntryCluster = entryClusterCount >= 2
float clusterEvidenceDiscount = strongEntryCluster ? 5.0 : 0.0
bool highQualityRetestCandidate = setupCandidateActive and not setupCandidateIsDirectBreak and setupCandidateReversalEligible and (bullishEarlyCloseQuality or bearishEarlyCloseQuality)
float retestEvidenceDiscount = highQualityRetestCandidate ? 5.0 : 0.0
float effectiveNormalEvidenceMinimum = math.max(45.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent - clusterEvidenceDiscount - retestEvidenceDiscount)
float directEvidenceMinimum = math.max(minimumPlanEvidencePercent, math.min(90.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent + 7.5 - clusterEvidenceDiscount))
float earlyRetestEvidenceMinimum = math.max(45.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent - 7.5 - clusterEvidenceDiscount - retestEvidenceDiscount)
bool longEvidencePass = f_entry_evidence_pass(true, useAdaptiveEntryQuality, setupCandidateIsDirectBreak, bullishEarlyCloseQuality, effectiveNormalEvidenceMinimum, directEvidenceMinimum, earlyRetestEvidenceMinimum, higherLongPenalty, bullishEvidence, bearishEvidence, bullishEvidencePercent, bearishEvidencePercent)
bool shortEvidencePass = f_entry_evidence_pass(false, useAdaptiveEntryQuality, setupCandidateIsDirectBreak, bearishEarlyCloseQuality, effectiveNormalEvidenceMinimum, directEvidenceMinimum, earlyRetestEvidenceMinimum, higherShortPenalty, bullishEvidence, bearishEvidence, bullishEvidencePercent, bearishEvidencePercent)
// الاتجاهات ليست أصواتا مكررة فقط؛ هي بوابة سلامة. يمنع التعارض البنيوي
// أو تعارض القناتين الخطة العادية. الاستثناء الوحيد هو انعكاس مؤكد غيّر
// الهيكل وامتلك فارق أدلة واضحا مع كسر/اختبار فني معتبر.
float counterTrendEvidenceMinimum = math.max(65.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent + 10.0)
bool bullishConfirmedReversalPass = resolvedStructureBias == 1 and (setupCandidateActive and setupCandidateDirection == 1 and setupCandidateReversalEligible) and bullishEvidencePercent >= counterTrendEvidenceMinimum and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence + 2
bool bearishConfirmedReversalPass = resolvedStructureBias == -1 and (setupCandidateActive and setupCandidateDirection == -1 and setupCandidateReversalEligible) and bearishEvidencePercent >= counterTrendEvidenceMinimum and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence + 2
bool weakHighLiquidity = resolvedStructureBias == 1 and not na(lastSwingHigh)
bool weakLowLiquidity = resolvedStructureBias == -1 and not na(lastSwingLow)
bool protectedLowSwing = resolvedStructureBias == 1 and not na(lastSwingLow)
bool protectedHighSwing = resolvedStructureBias == -1 and not na(lastSwingHigh)
bool analysisIntegrityPass = swingLevelsCompatible
bool majorLongOpposed = majorTrendReady and majorChannelSignal == -1
bool majorShortOpposed = majorTrendReady and majorChannelSignal == 1
bool minorLongOpposed = trendReady and channelSignal == -1
bool minorShortOpposed = trendReady and channelSignal == 1
float transitionEvidenceMinimum = math.max(60.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent + 5.0)
bool bullishTransitionPass = setupCandidateActive and setupCandidateDirection == 1 and setupCandidateReversalEligible and close > ema20 and bullishEvidencePercent >= transitionEvidenceMinimum and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence + 2
bool bearishTransitionPass = setupCandidateActive and setupCandidateDirection == -1 and setupCandidateReversalEligible and close < ema20 and bearishEvidencePercent >= transitionEvidenceMinimum and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence + 2
bool longTrendLayersPass = (not channelLayerConflict and not majorLongOpposed and not minorLongOpposed and not structureDirectionConflict) or bullishConfirmedReversalPass or bullishTransitionPass
bool shortTrendLayersPass = (not channelLayerConflict and not majorShortOpposed and not minorShortOpposed and not structureDirectionConflict) or bearishConfirmedReversalPass or bearishTransitionPass
bool higherLongAlignmentPass = not requireHigherTimeframeAlignment or higherWeightedScore > -5 or bullishConfirmedReversalPass or bullishTransitionPass
bool higherShortAlignmentPass = not requireHigherTimeframeAlignment or higherWeightedScore < 5 or bearishConfirmedReversalPass or bearishTransitionPass
int requestedPlanDirection = actionableLongSetup and analysisIntegrityPass and longTrendLayersPass and longEvidencePass and higherLongAlignmentPass and not actionableShortSetup ? 1 : actionableShortSetup and analysisIntegrityPass and shortTrendLayersPass and shortEvidencePass and higherShortAlignmentPass and not actionableLongSetup ? -1 : 0
bool candidateEvidencePass = setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? longEvidencePass : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? shortEvidencePass : false
bool candidateTrendPass = setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? longTrendLayersPass and higherLongAlignmentPass : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? shortTrendLayersPass and higherShortAlignmentPass : false
string candidateGateState = setupCandidateLocalGateCode == 1 ? " | ينتظر تأهيل" : setupCandidateLocalGateCode == 2 ? " | ينتظر عودة للمستوى" : setupCandidateLocalGateCode == 3 ? " | كسر ضعيف؛ ينتظر اختبار" : not entrySessionPass ? " | خارج جلسة الدخول" : setupCandidateDirection == 1 and not vwapLongPass or setupCandidateDirection == -1 and not vwapShortPass ? " | ينتظر VWAP" : not candidateEvidencePass ? " | أدلة غير كافية" : not analysisIntegrityPass or not candidateTrendPass ? " | تعارض اتجاه" : " | جاهز"
bool requestedEarlyEntry = requestedPlanDirection == 1 ? bullishEarlyCloseQuality : requestedPlanDirection == -1 ? bearishEarlyCloseQuality : false
if planActive and barstate.isconfirmed and requestedPlanDirection != 0 and requestedPlanDirection != planDirection
    planActive := false
    planState := "أغلقت الخطة بسبب إشارة عكسية مؤكدة"
    lastPlanResult := planState
    lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
    planOutcomeBar := bar_index
    planOutcomeKind := 4
    planOutcomePrice := close
    f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 72, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 82, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(target1Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 88, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(target2Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 88, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(target3Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 88, 1)

// جميع نهايات الخطة تمر من هنا: أوقف الخطوط، احذف تفاصيل الخطة، واحتفظ
// بعلامة نتيجة واحدة فقط. تتكرر الكتلة أثناء الشمعة الحية حتى تثبت الرسوم بعد الإغلاق.
bool planEndedThisBar = not planActive and not na(planOutcomeBar) and planOutcomeBar == bar_index
if planEndedThisBar
    entryLine := f_clear_line(entryLine)
    stopLine := f_clear_line(stopLine)
    target1Line := f_clear_line(target1Line)
    target2Line := f_clear_line(target2Line)
    target3Line := f_clear_line(target3Line)
    entryLevelLabel := f_clear_label(entryLevelLabel)
    stopLevelLabel := f_clear_label(stopLevelLabel)
    target1LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target1LevelLabel)
    target2LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target2LevelLabel)
    target3LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target3LevelLabel)
    activePlanEntryMarker := f_clear_label(activePlanEntryMarker)
    activePlanTarget1Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget1Marker)
    activePlanTarget2Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget2Marker)
    activePlanTarget3Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget3Marker)
    planHighSinceEntry := na
    planLowSinceEntry := na
bool planCooldownComplete = na(lastPlanExitBar) or bar_index - lastPlanExitBar > planReentryCooldownBars
bool newPlanCreatedEvent = false

if (barstate.isconfirmed and requestedPlanDirection != 0 and not planActive and not planEndedThisBar and planCooldownComplete)
    bool isLong = requestedPlanDirection == 1
    bool newPlanUsesStructureBreakGuard = setupCandidateUsesGuard
    float newPlanStructureBreakGuardLevel = setupCandidateGuardLevel
    float setupReferenceLevel = setupCandidateReference
    float genericSwingRange = not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLow) ? math.abs(lastSwingHigh - lastSwingLow) : na
    float setupRawRange = setupCandidateRange
    string selectedSetupSource = setupCandidateSource + (requestedEarlyEntry ? " | دخول مبكر مؤكد" : setupCandidateIsDirectBreak ? " | كسر مؤكد" : " | اختبار مؤكد") + (strongEntryCluster ? " | كلاستر دخول " + str.tostring(entryClusterCount) : "")
    float baseContextualStopAnchor = setupCandidateStopAnchor
    bool protectedContextStop = setupCandidateStopKind == 2
    float setupSpecificStopAnchor = setupCandidateStopKind == 1 ? setupCandidateStopAnchor : na
    float nearestFibStopLevel = isLong ? nearestFibSupportForStop : nearestFibResistanceForStop
    float minorChannelStopLevel = isLong ? (channelReady ? channelLowerNow : channelBias == 1 ? channelBaseNow : na) : (channelReady ? channelUpperNow : channelBias == -1 ? channelBaseNow : na)
    float majorChannelStopLevel = isLong ? (majorChannelReady ? majorChannelLowerNow : majorChannelBias == 1 ? majorChannelBaseNow : na) : (majorChannelReady ? majorChannelUpperNow : majorChannelBias == -1 ? majorChannelBaseNow : na)
    float stopCandleLevel = f_nearest_price_level(not isLong, close, safeAtr * 0.10, array.from(isLong ? highVolumeSupportLow : highVolumeResistanceHigh, isLong ? lastKeyCandleLow : lastKeyCandleHigh))
    float protectedSwingStop = isLong ? (protectedLowSwing ? lastSwingLow : na) : (protectedHighSwing ? lastSwingHigh : na)
    float weakSwingTarget = isLong ? (weakHighLiquidity ? lastSwingHigh : na) : (weakLowLiquidity ? lastSwingLow : na)
    float stopClusterTolerance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.30, syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    [minimumStopAtrMultiple, maximumStopAtrMultiple] = f_stop_atr_limits(chartSeconds)
    array<float> stopClusterLevels = array.from(protectedSwingStop, isLong ? strongSupportLow : strongResistanceHigh, isLong ? demandZoneLow : supplyZoneHigh, isLong ? wyckoffRangeSupport : wyckoffRangeResistance, minorChannelStopLevel, majorChannelStopLevel, nearestFibStopLevel, ema20, ema50, stopCandleLevel)
    [rankedStopAnchor, rankedStopScore, rankedStopCount, rankedStopRank] = f_ranked_cluster_level(not isLong, close, safeAtr * 0.10, (safeAtr * (maximumStopAtrMultiple + stopAtrBuffer)), stopClusterTolerance, stopClusterLevels)
    float contextualStopAnchor = protectedContextStop ? baseContextualStopAnchor : not na(setupSpecificStopAnchor) ? setupSpecificStopAnchor : nz(rankedStopAnchor, baseContextualStopAnchor)
    string stopReferenceText = protectedContextStop ? " | وقف خلف الإبطال" : not na(setupSpecificStopAnchor) ? " | وقف خلف الإعداد" : rankedStopCount >= 2 ? " | وقف خلف كلاستر " + str.tostring(rankedStopCount) : rankedStopRank <= 3 ? " | وقف خلف قمة/قاع" : rankedStopRank <= 5 ? " | وقف خلف قناة" : rankedStopRank == 6 ? " | وقف خلف فيبوناتشي" : rankedStopRank <= 8 ? " | وقف خلف متوسط" : not na(rankedStopAnchor) ? " | وقف خلف شمعة حجم" : ""
    float validInvalidationAnchor = isLong ? math.min(contextualStopAnchor, close - syminfo.mintick) : math.max(contextualStopAnchor, close + syminfo.mintick)
    float rawStop = isLong ? validInvalidationAnchor - safeAtr * stopAtrBuffer : validInvalidationAnchor + safeAtr * stopAtrBuffer
    float rawRiskDistance = math.abs(close - rawStop)
    float minimumRiskDistance = useAdaptiveRiskGeometry ? safeAtr * minimumStopAtrMultiple : syminfo.mintick
    float volatilityAdjustedStop = rawRiskDistance < minimumRiskDistance ? (isLong ? close - minimumRiskDistance : close + minimumRiskDistance) : rawStop
    float validStopUnrounded = isLong ? math.min(volatilityAdjustedStop, close - syminfo.mintick) : math.max(volatilityAdjustedStop, close + syminfo.mintick)
    float validStop = isLong ? f_floor_to_tick(validStopUnrounded) : f_ceil_to_tick(validStopUnrounded)
    float risk = math.max(math.abs(close - validStop), syminfo.mintick)
    float swingRange = not na(setupRawRange) and setupRawRange > syminfo.mintick ? math.max(setupRawRange, risk) : not na(genericSwingRange) ? math.max(genericSwingRange, risk) : risk * 2.0

    // Risk already scales with the chart's structure and ATR. Do not shrink
    // reward below 1R on fast charts. Larger intervals may widen the ladder.
    float rewardHorizonFactor = math.max(targetHorizonFactor, 1.0)
    float orderedRr1 = math.max(rr1 * rewardHorizonFactor, minimumTarget1RiskMultiple)
    float orderedRr2 = math.max(rr2 * rewardHorizonFactor, orderedRr1 + 0.75)
    float orderedRr3 = math.max(rr3 * rewardHorizonFactor, orderedRr2 + 1.00)
    float minimumTarget1Distance = risk * orderedRr1
    float minimumTarget2Distance = risk * orderedRr2
    float minimumTarget3Distance = risk * orderedRr3
    float minimumTargetGap = risk * 0.50
    // الغلاف الأقصى لا يجوز أن يصبح أصغر من الحد الأدنى الذي اختاره المستخدم.
    float maximumTarget1Distance = math.max(risk * 2.0 * rewardHorizonFactor, minimumTarget1Distance)
    float maximumTarget2Distance = math.max(risk * 4.0 * rewardHorizonFactor, minimumTarget2Distance + minimumTargetGap)
    float maximumTarget3Distance = math.max(risk * 6.0 * rewardHorizonFactor, minimumTarget3Distance + minimumTargetGap)
    float candidateTarget1 = na
    float candidateTarget2 = na
    float candidateTarget3 = na
    float fibObjective1 = na
    float fibObjective2 = na
    float fibObjective3 = na
    if targetMode == RR_MODE
        candidateTarget1 := isLong ? close + minimumTarget1Distance : close - minimumTarget1Distance
        candidateTarget2 := isLong ? close + minimumTarget2Distance : close - minimumTarget2Distance
        candidateTarget3 := isLong ? close + minimumTarget3Distance : close - minimumTarget3Distance
    else
        float brokenLevel = nz(setupReferenceLevel, isLong ? nz(lastSwingHigh, close) : nz(lastSwingLow, close))
        [fibTarget1, fibTarget2, fibTarget3, selectedFibLevel1, selectedFibLevel2, selectedFibLevel3] = f_build_fib_extension_targets(isLong, brokenLevel, swingRange, close, minimumTarget1Distance, minimumTarget2Distance, minimumTarget3Distance, maximumTarget1Distance, maximumTarget2Distance, maximumTarget3Distance, minimumTargetGap)
        candidateTarget1 := fibTarget1
        candidateTarget2 := fibTarget2
        candidateTarget3 := fibTarget3
        fibObjective1 := selectedFibLevel1
        fibObjective2 := selectedFibLevel2
        fibObjective3 := selectedFibLevel3

    float measuredTarget = isLong ? bullishMeasuredTarget : bearishMeasuredTarget
    if not na(measuredTarget) and (isLong ? measuredTarget > close : measuredTarget < close)
        // A measured objective may extend T3, but never beyond the adaptive
        // maximum reward envelope for this chart interval.
        float boundedMeasuredTarget = isLong ? math.min(measuredTarget, close + maximumTarget3Distance) : math.max(measuredTarget, close - maximumTarget3Distance)
        candidateTarget3 := isLong ? math.max(candidateTarget3, boundedMeasuredTarget) : math.min(candidateTarget3, boundedMeasuredTarget)

    // محرك الأهداف: القمم/القيعان التاريخية والعوائق البنيوية أولاً، ثم
    // حدود وأهداف القنوات، ثم امتداد وتصحيح فيبوناتشي معاً، ثم المتوسطات والشموع.
    float minimumStructuralTargetDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.25, syminfo.mintick * 4.0)
    float retracementTargetLevel = isLong ? nearestFibResistanceForTarget : nearestFibSupportForTarget
    float minorChannelBoundaryTarget = isLong ? channelUpperNow : channelLowerNow
    float majorChannelBoundaryTarget = isLong ? majorChannelUpperNow : majorChannelLowerNow
    float minorChannelMeasuredTarget = isLong ? bullishChannelMeasuredTarget : bearishChannelMeasuredTarget
    float majorChannelMeasuredTarget = isLong ? bullishMajorChannelTarget : bearishMajorChannelTarget
    float horizontalObstacleLevel = isLong ? (strongResistanceReady and not strongResistanceRoleReversed ? strongResistanceLow : na) : (strongSupportReady and not strongSupportRoleReversed ? strongSupportHigh : na)
    float opposingZoneBoundary = isLong ? f_nearest_price_level(true, close, syminfo.mintick, array.from(supplyZoneLow, bearishBreakerLow)) : f_nearest_price_level(false, close, syminfo.mintick, array.from(demandZoneHigh, bullishBreakerHigh))
    float targetWyckoffLevel = isLong ? wyckoffRangeResistance : wyckoffRangeSupport
    float targetCandleLevel = f_nearest_price_level(isLong, close, minimumStructuralTargetDistance, array.from(isLong ? highVolumeResistanceLow : highVolumeSupportHigh, isLong ? lastKeyCandleHigh : lastKeyCandleLow))
    float historicalPivotObstacle = f_nearest_stored_pivot(isLong, close, minimumStructuralTargetDistance, maximumTarget3Distance, isLong ? srHighPivotPrices : srLowPivotPrices, isLong ? srHighPivotBars : srLowPivotBars, srMaximumAgeBars)
    bool insideOpposingHardZone = (isLong ? supplyZoneActive and close >= supplyZoneLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= supplyZoneHigh + syminfo.mintick : demandZoneActive and close >= demandZoneLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= demandZoneHigh + syminfo.mintick) or (isLong ? bearishBreakerActive and close >= bearishBreakerLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= bearishBreakerHigh + syminfo.mintick : bullishBreakerActive and close >= bullishBreakerLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= bullishBreakerHigh + syminfo.mintick) or (isLong ? strongResistanceReady and not resistanceClusterActsAsSupport and close >= strongResistanceLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= strongResistanceHigh + syminfo.mintick : strongSupportReady and not supportClusterActsAsResistance and close >= strongSupportLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= strongSupportHigh + syminfo.mintick)
    float nearestHardObstacle = f_nearest_price_level(isLong, close, syminfo.mintick * 2.0, array.from(historicalPivotObstacle, horizontalObstacleLevel, opposingZoneBoundary, targetWyckoffLevel, minorChannelBoundaryTarget, majorChannelBoundaryTarget))
    float hardObstacleDistanceR = not na(nearestHardObstacle) ? math.abs(nearestHardObstacle - close) / risk : na
    float requiredObstacleRoomR = math.max(minimumObstacleRoomRiskMultiple, orderedRr1)
    bool roomToObstacleValid = not useAdaptiveRiskGeometry or (not insideOpposingHardZone and (na(hardObstacleDistanceR) or hardObstacleDistanceR >= requiredObstacleRoomR))
    float clusterTolerance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.30, syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    array<float> targetClusterLevels = array.from(historicalPivotObstacle, horizontalObstacleLevel, opposingZoneBoundary, targetWyckoffLevel, minorChannelBoundaryTarget, majorChannelBoundaryTarget, minorChannelMeasuredTarget, majorChannelMeasuredTarget, fibObjective1, retracementTargetLevel, ema20, ema50, targetCandleLevel)
    [rankedTarget1, rankedTarget1Score, rankedTarget1Count, rankedTarget1Rank] = f_ranked_cluster_level(isLong, close, minimumTarget1Distance, maximumTarget1Distance, clusterTolerance, targetClusterLevels)
    bool firstTargetUsesHardObstacle = not na(nearestHardObstacle) and (isLong ? nearestHardObstacle < nz(rankedTarget1, candidateTarget1) : nearestHardObstacle > nz(rankedTarget1, candidateTarget1)) and math.abs(nearestHardObstacle - close) >= minimumTarget1Distance
    float firstTargetCandidate = firstTargetUsesHardObstacle ? nearestHardObstacle : nz(rankedTarget1, candidateTarget1)
    candidateTarget1 := firstTargetCandidate
    float target2MinimumDistance = math.max(minimumTarget2Distance, math.abs(candidateTarget1 - close) + minimumTargetGap)
    array.set(targetClusterLevels, 8, fibObjective2)
    [rankedTarget2, rankedTarget2Score, rankedTarget2Count, rankedTarget2Rank] = f_ranked_cluster_level(isLong, close, target2MinimumDistance, maximumTarget2Distance, clusterTolerance, targetClusterLevels)
    float fallbackTarget2Distance = math.max(target2MinimumDistance, math.abs(candidateTarget1 - close) + minimumTargetGap)
    float fallbackTarget2 = isLong ? close + fallbackTarget2Distance : close - fallbackTarget2Distance
    candidateTarget2 := nz(rankedTarget2, fallbackTarget2)
    float target3MinimumDistance = math.max(minimumTarget3Distance, math.abs(candidateTarget2 - close) + minimumTargetGap)
    array.set(targetClusterLevels, 8, fibObjective3)
    [rankedTarget3, rankedTarget3Score, rankedTarget3Count, rankedTarget3Rank] = f_ranked_cluster_level(isLong, close, target3MinimumDistance, maximumTarget3Distance, clusterTolerance, targetClusterLevels)
    float fallbackTarget3Distance = math.max(target3MinimumDistance, math.abs(candidateTarget2 - close) + minimumTargetGap)
    float fallbackTarget3 = isLong ? close + fallbackTarget3Distance : close - fallbackTarget3Distance
    candidateTarget3 := nz(rankedTarget3, fallbackTarget3)
    candidateTarget1 := isLong ? f_ceil_to_tick(candidateTarget1) : f_floor_to_tick(candidateTarget1)
    candidateTarget2 := isLong ? f_ceil_to_tick(candidateTarget2) : f_floor_to_tick(candidateTarget2)
    candidateTarget3 := isLong ? f_ceil_to_tick(candidateTarget3) : f_floor_to_tick(candidateTarget3)
    bool structuralTargetUsed = not na(firstTargetCandidate) or not na(rankedTarget2) or not na(rankedTarget3)
    int selectedTargetRank = firstTargetUsesHardObstacle ? 0 : not na(rankedTarget1) ? rankedTarget1Rank : not na(rankedTarget2) ? rankedTarget2Rank : rankedTarget3Rank
    int selectedTargetClusterCount = math.max(firstTargetUsesHardObstacle ? 1 : rankedTarget1Count, math.max(rankedTarget2Count, rankedTarget3Count))
    string targetReferenceText = selectedTargetClusterCount >= 2 ? " | أهداف كلاستر " + str.tostring(selectedTargetClusterCount) : selectedTargetRank <= 3 ? " | أهداف قمم/قيعان" : selectedTargetRank <= 7 ? " | أهداف قناة" : selectedTargetRank <= 9 ? " | أهداف فيبوناتشي" : selectedTargetRank <= 11 ? " | أهداف متوسطات" : structuralTargetUsed ? " | أهداف شموع حجم" : ""
    float planRiskPercent = close > 0 ? risk / close * 100.0 : 1000.0
    float planRiskAtrMultiple = safeAtr > 0 ? risk / safeAtr : 1000.0
    float effectiveRiskPercentLimit = useAdaptiveRiskGeometry ? math.min(maxPlanRiskPercent, (((chartSeconds) <= 60 ? 3.0 : (chartSeconds) <= 5 * 60 ? 4.0 : (chartSeconds) <= 15 * 60 ? 5.5 : (chartSeconds) <= 30 * 60 ? 7.0 : (chartSeconds) <= 60 * 60 ? 9.0 : (chartSeconds) <= 4 * 60 * 60 ? 12.0 : (chartSeconds) < 24 * 60 * 60 ? 15.0 : 20.0))) : maxPlanRiskPercent
    bool riskPercentValid = planRiskPercent <= effectiveRiskPercentLimit
    bool riskAtrValid = not useAdaptiveRiskGeometry or planRiskAtrMultiple <= maximumStopAtrMultiple + 0.0001
    bool riskDistanceValid = riskPercentValid and riskAtrValid
    bool stopPriceValid = validStop >= syminfo.mintick and (isLong ? validStop < close : validStop > close)
    bool planNumbersReady = not na(close) and not na(validStop) and not na(risk) and not na(candidateTarget1) and not na(candidateTarget2) and not na(candidateTarget3)
    bool targetPricesPositive = planNumbersReady and candidateTarget1 >= syminfo.mintick and candidateTarget2 >= syminfo.mintick and candidateTarget3 >= syminfo.mintick
    bool targetOrderValid = planNumbersReady and (isLong ? candidateTarget1 > close and candidateTarget2 > candidateTarget1 and candidateTarget3 > candidateTarget2 : candidateTarget1 < close and candidateTarget2 < candidateTarget1 and candidateTarget3 < candidateTarget2)
    float target1RiskMultiple = planNumbersReady ? math.abs(candidateTarget1 - close) / risk : na
    float target2RiskMultiple = planNumbersReady ? math.abs(candidateTarget2 - close) / risk : na
    float target3RiskMultiple = planNumbersReady ? math.abs(candidateTarget3 - close) / risk : na
    bool targetRewardValid = planNumbersReady and target1RiskMultiple + 0.0001 >= orderedRr1 and target2RiskMultiple >= target1RiskMultiple + 0.49 and target3RiskMultiple >= target2RiskMultiple + 0.49

    if (planNumbersReady and riskDistanceValid and roomToObstacleValid and stopPriceValid and targetPricesPositive and targetOrderValid and targetRewardValid)
        int score = isLong ? bullishEvidence : bearishEvidence
        planDirection := isLong ? 1 : -1
        planActive := true
        planStartBar := bar_index
        planUsesStructureBreakGuard := newPlanUsesStructureBreakGuard
        planStructureBreakGuardLevel := newPlanStructureBreakGuardLevel
        planEntry := close
        planStop := validStop
        planTarget1 := candidateTarget1
        planTarget2 := candidateTarget2
        planTarget3 := candidateTarget3
        planHighSinceEntry := na
        planLowSinceEntry := na
        planInvalidationLevel := validInvalidationAnchor
        planScore := score
        planScoreMax := evidenceMaximum
        planCounterTrend := isLong ? higherWeightedScore < 0 : higherWeightedScore > 0
        planOutcomeBar := na
        planOutcomeKind := 0
        planOutcomePrice := na
        newPlanCreatedEvent := true
        target1Done := false
        target2Done := false
        target3Done := false
        planTrailingStopActive := false
        planTrailingStopStartedBar := na
        target1HitBar := na
        target2HitBar := na
        target3HitBar := na
        planTradeText := isLong ? "فرصة صعود السهم" : "فرصة هبوط السهم"
        planPriceContext := priceContextText
        planSetupSource := selectedSetupSource + stopReferenceText + targetReferenceText
        planTargetHorizon := currentTargetHorizonText + " | وقف " + str.tostring(planRiskAtrMultiple, "#.##") + " ATR / " + str.tostring(planRiskPercent, "#.##") + "% | الأهداف " + str.tostring(target1RiskMultiple, "#.##") + "R / " + str.tostring(target2RiskMultiple, "#.##") + "R / " + str.tostring(target3RiskMultiple, "#.##") + "R"
        planState := isLong ? "خطة صعود السهم نشطة" : "خطة هبوط السهم نشطة"
        f_clear_setup_candidate(setupCandidateState)

        // هذه هي شمعة القرار الفعلية: لا تظهر العلامة إلا بعد إغلاق الشمعة
        // واكتمال الإعداد والأدلة وإدارة المخاطرة، لا لمجرد اقتراب السعر.

        entryLine := f_clear_line(entryLine)
        stopLine := f_clear_line(stopLine)
        target1Line := f_clear_line(target1Line)
        target2Line := f_clear_line(target2Line)
        target3Line := f_clear_line(target3Line)
        entryLevelLabel := f_clear_label(entryLevelLabel)
        stopLevelLabel := f_clear_label(stopLevelLabel)
        target1LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target1LevelLabel)
        target2LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target2LevelLabel)
        target3LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target3LevelLabel)
        activePlanEntryMarker := f_clear_label(activePlanEntryMarker)
        activePlanTarget1Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget1Marker)
        activePlanTarget2Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget2Marker)
        activePlanTarget3Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget3Marker)
        // One fixed visual language for both rising and falling plans:
        // cyan entry, red stop, and dotted green targets.
        color confirmedEntryColor = isLong ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor
        entryLine := f_new_plan_line(planEntry, confirmedEntryColor, planEntryLineTransparency, line.style_solid)
        stopLine := f_new_plan_line(planStop, #F23645, planStopLineTransparency, line.style_dotted)
        target1Line := f_new_plan_line(planTarget1, #22C55E, planTarget1LineTransparency, line.style_dotted)
        target2Line := f_new_plan_line(planTarget2, #22C55E, planTarget2LineTransparency, line.style_dotted)
        target3Line := f_new_plan_line(planTarget3, #22C55E, planTarget3LineTransparency, line.style_dotted)
        entryLevelLabel := f_new_plan_level_label(planEntry, confirmedEntryColor)
        stopLevelLabel := f_new_plan_level_label(planStop, #F23645)
        target1LevelLabel := f_new_plan_level_label(planTarget1, #22C55E)
        target2LevelLabel := f_new_plan_level_label(planTarget2, #22C55E)
        target3LevelLabel := f_new_plan_level_label(planTarget3, #22C55E)
    else
        planDirection := 0
        planActive := false
        planStartBar := na
        planEntry := na
        planStop := na
        planTarget1 := na
        planTarget2 := na
        planTarget3 := na
        planHighSinceEntry := na
        planLowSinceEntry := na
        planInvalidationLevel := na
        planScore := 0
        planScoreMax := evidenceMaximum
        planCounterTrend := false
        target1Done := false
        target2Done := false
        target3Done := false
        planTrailingStopActive := false
        planTrailingStopStartedBar := na
        target1HitBar := na
        target2HitBar := na
        target3HitBar := na
        planTradeText := "لا توجد صفقة"
        planPriceContext := priceContextText
        planSetupSource := "الإعداد مرفوض بإدارة المخاطرة"
        planTargetHorizon := currentTargetHorizonText
        planState := not riskPercentValid ? "لم تنشأ الخطة: مسافة الوقف " + str.tostring(planRiskPercent, "#.##") + "% تتجاوز حد الفاصل " + str.tostring(effectiveRiskPercentLimit, "#.##") + "%" : not riskAtrValid ? "لم تنشأ الخطة: الوقف أبعد من " + str.tostring(maximumStopAtrMultiple, "#.##") + " ATR على هذا الفاصل" : not roomToObstacleValid ? "لم تنشأ الخطة: أول عائق قوي أقرب من " + str.tostring(requiredObstacleRoomR, "#.##") + "R أو أن الدخول داخل منطقة معاكسة" : not stopPriceValid ? "لم تنشأ الخطة: سعر الوقف غير صالح" : not targetRewardValid ? "لم تنشأ الخطة: العائد إلى المخاطرة أقل من الحد المنطقي" : "لم تنشأ الخطة: الأهداف غير صالحة أو غير مرتبة"
        lastPlanResult := planState

// عزل تحديث الرسومات في دالة يقلل حجم النطاق الرئيسي. الدالة لا تعيد
// إسناد مراجع عامة؛ هي تعدل خصائص line/label فقط، وهي أنواع مرجعية.
f_update_plan_level_label(label id, int x, float level, float offset, string caption, color textColor) =>
    label.set_xy(id, x, level + offset)
    label.set_text(id, caption + f_price(level))
    label.set_color(id, color.new(color.black, 100))
    label.set_textcolor(id, textColor)

f_refresh_plan_display() =>
    if not na(entryLevelLabel)
        int x = bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars
        float offset = math.max(syminfo.mintick * 3.0, safeAtr * (compactIntradayDisplay ? 0.06 : 0.04))
        f_update_plan_level_label(entryLevelLabel, x, planEntry, offset, planDirection == 1 ? "دخول شراء " : "دخول بيع ", planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor)
        f_update_plan_level_label(stopLevelLabel, x, planStop, offset, "وقف ", #F23645)
        f_update_plan_level_label(target1LevelLabel, x, planTarget1, offset, "هدف 1" + (target1Done ? " ✓ " : " "), #22C55E)
        f_update_plan_level_label(target2LevelLabel, x, planTarget2, offset, "هدف 2" + (target2Done ? " ✓ " : " "), #22C55E)
        f_update_plan_level_label(target3LevelLabel, x, planTarget3, offset, "هدف 3" + (target3Done ? " ✓ " : " "), #22C55E)
    true
f_refresh_plan_display()

plot(stockChartAllowed and showFibRetracement and fibRetracementReady ? fibRetracement236 : na, "فيبو 23.6%", color = color.new(fibRetracementVisualColor, 88), linewidth = 1, style = plot.style_linebr, show_last = fibRetracementDisplayBars, force_overlay = true)

// إشارات الشارت بقالب موحد؛ الاختلاف في النص واللون والاتجاه فقط.
int maximumUnifiedChartLabels = fastestIntradayDisplay ? 10 : compactIntradayDisplay ? 14 : 18
bool watchStageMarker = chartSignalDisplayAllowed and formationDirection != 0 and not setupCandidateActive and not planActive and (showHistoricalOpportunityMarkers ? formationDirection != nz(formationDirection[1], 0) : barstate.islast)
bool candidateStageMarker = chartSignalDisplayAllowed and setupCandidateActive and not planActive and (showHistoricalOpportunityMarkers ? (not setupCandidateActive[1] or setupCandidateDirection != nz(setupCandidateDirection[1], 0)) : barstate.islast)
if showHistoricalOpportunityMarkers
    activeStageLabel := f_clear_label(activeStageLabel)
    if watchStageMarker
        f_push_chart_marker(chartEventLabels, maximumUnifiedChartLabels, bar_index, formationDirection == 1 ? low : high, "راقب", formationDirection == 1, formationDirection == 1 ? bullColor : bearColor, safeAtr, 0.30, 0.18)
    else if candidateStageMarker
        f_push_chart_marker(chartEventLabels, maximumUnifiedChartLabels, bar_index, setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? low : high, "مرشح", setupCandidateDirection == 1, setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? bullColor : bearColor, safeAtr, 0.38, 0.18)
else if barstate.islast
    activeStageLabel := f_clear_label(activeStageLabel)
    if watchStageMarker
        activeStageLabel := f_new_chart_marker(bar_index, formationDirection == 1 ? low : high, "راقب", formationDirection == 1, formationDirection == 1 ? bullColor : bearColor, safeAtr, 0.30)
    else if candidateStageMarker
        activeStageLabel := f_new_chart_marker(bar_index, setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? low : high, "مرشح", setupCandidateDirection == 1, setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? bullColor : bearColor, safeAtr, 0.38)

if (chartSignalDisplayAllowed and barstate.isconfirmed and unifiedEventMarkerEnabled and not newPlanCreatedEvent)
    f_push_chart_marker(chartEventLabels, maximumUnifiedChartLabels, unifiedEventBar, unifiedEventLevel, unifiedEventText, unifiedEventDirection >= 0, unifiedEventColor, unifiedEventAtr, 0.40, 0.18)

if stockChartAllowed
    if newPlanCreatedEvent
        activePlanEntryMarker := f_clear_label(activePlanEntryMarker)
        activePlanEntryMarker := f_new_chart_marker(bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? low : high, planDirection == 1 ? "شراء" : "بيع", planDirection == 1, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, safeAtr, 0.25)
    if planActive
        if target1Done and target1HitBar == bar_index
            activePlanTarget1Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget1Marker)
            activePlanTarget1Marker := f_new_chart_marker(bar_index, planTarget1, "هدف 1", planDirection == -1, #22C55E, safeAtr, 0.34)
        if target2Done and target2HitBar == bar_index
            activePlanTarget2Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget2Marker)
            activePlanTarget2Marker := f_new_chart_marker(bar_index, planTarget2, "هدف 2", planDirection == -1, #22C55E, safeAtr, 0.34)
        if target3Done and target3HitBar == bar_index
            activePlanTarget3Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget3Marker)
            activePlanTarget3Marker := f_new_chart_marker(bar_index, planTarget3, "هدف 3", planDirection == -1, #22C55E, safeAtr, 0.34)

var table dashboard = table.new(position.top_right, 2, 5, bgcolor = color.new(#111827, 12), frame_color = color.new(neutralColor, 45), frame_width = 1, border_color = color.new(neutralColor, 78), border_width = 1)
if barstate.isfirst
    table.merge_cells(dashboard, 0, 0, 1, 0)

f_render_dashboard(table dashboardId) =>
    if barstate.islast
        if showDashboard
            color labelBg = color.new(neutralColor, 68)
            color valueBg = color.new(#111827, 12)
            color directionColor = closedStructureBias == 1 ? bullColor : closedStructureBias == -1 ? bearColor : neutralColor
            color opportunityColor = planActive ? (planDirection == 1 ? bullColor : bearColor) : setupCandidateActive ? (setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? bullColor : bearColor) : formationDirection == 1 ? bullColor : formationDirection == -1 ? bearColor : neutralColor
            int displayedAdaptiveBias = barstate.isconfirmed ? adaptiveTrendBias : adaptiveTrendBias[1]
            string currentBias = closedStructureBias == 1 ? "صاعد" : closedStructureBias == -1 ? "هابط" : "تحول"
            string higherBias = closedHigherTrendBias == 1 ? "صاعد" : closedHigherTrendBias == -1 ? "هابط" : "محايد"
            string warningText = showContextWarningLabel ? closedStructureConflict ? " | تعارض هيكلي" : closedStructureBias != 0 and closedHigherTrendBias != 0 and closedStructureBias != closedHigherTrendBias ? " | اختلاف أعلى" : "" : ""
            string directionText = currentBias + " | أعلى " + higherBias + " | تكيفي " + (displayedAdaptiveBias == 1 ? "صاعد" : displayedAdaptiveBias == -1 ? "هابط" : "محايد") + warningText
            string opportunityText = planActive ? (planDirection == 1 ? "خطة شراء" : "خطة بيع") + " | " + planState : setupCandidateActive ? (setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? "مرشح شراء" : "مرشح بيع") + candidateGateState + " | " + setupCandidateSource : formationDirection != 0 ? (formationDirection == 1 ? "مراقبة شراء" : "مراقبة بيع") + " | " + formationSource : "لا فرصة"
            string confirmationText = "شراء " + str.tostring(bullishEvidencePercent, "#.0") + "% | بيع " + str.tostring(bearishEvidencePercent, "#.0") + "% | حجم " + (closedVolumeAvailable ? f_ratio(closedRelativeVolume) : "—")
            string contextText = closedWyckoffPhaseState + " | " + closedTechnicalPatternState + " | " + closedZoneState + " | " + closedChannelState
            table.cell(dashboardId, 0, 0, "ملخص SC-V77-I", text_color = color.white, bgcolor = color.new(neutralColor, 35), text_size = 8, text_halign = text.align_center)
            array<string> rowLabels = array.from("الاتجاه", "الفرصة", "التأكيد", "السياق")
            array<string> rowValues = array.from(directionText, opportunityText, confirmationText, contextText)
            array<color> rowColors = array.from(directionColor, opportunityColor, color.white, neutralColor)
            for rowIndex = 0 to 3
                table.cell(dashboardId, 0, rowIndex + 1, array.get(rowLabels, rowIndex), width = 8, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = labelBg, text_size = 8, text_halign = text.align_right)
                table.cell(dashboardId, 1, rowIndex + 1, array.get(rowValues, rowIndex), width = 34, text_color = array.get(rowColors, rowIndex), bgcolor = valueBg, text_size = 8, text_halign = text.align_right)
        else
            table.clear(dashboardId, 0, 0, 1, 4)
        true

f_render_dashboard(dashboard)


// تنبيهات مختصرة؛ جميعها تعتمد على شمعة مؤكدة أو حدث إدارة مسجل.
bool longPlanAlert = stockChartAllowed and newPlanCreatedEvent and planDirection == 1
bool shortPlanAlert = stockChartAllowed and newPlanCreatedEvent and planDirection == -1
bool protectiveStopAlert = stockChartAllowed and planOutcomeBar == bar_index and planOutcomeKind == 1
bool anyTargetHitAlert = target1HitBar == bar_index or target2HitBar == bar_index or target3HitBar == bar_index
bool confirmedFakeoutAlert = stockChartAllowed and barstate.isconfirmed and (bullishBreakFailed or bearishBreakFailed or qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout)
alertcondition(longPlanAlert, "SC-V77-I دخول شراء", "تم إنشاء خطة شراء مؤكدة")
alertcondition(shortPlanAlert, "SC-V77-I دخول بيع", "تم إنشاء خطة بيع مؤكدة")
alertcondition(protectiveStopAlert, "SC-V77-I ضرب الوقف", "تم لمس وقف الخطة")
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and target1HitBar == bar_index, "SC-V77-I هدف 1", "تحقق الهدف الأول")
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and target2HitBar == bar_index, "SC-V77-I هدف 2", "تحقق الهدف الثاني")
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and target3HitBar == bar_index, "SC-V77-I هدف 3", "تحقق الهدف الثالث")
alertcondition(confirmedFakeoutAlert, "SC-V77-I كسر وهمي", "تأكد كسر أو اختراق وهمي")
